## Long-form entries (formerly "Change Log (this manual only)") — NEW ENTRIES PREPEND HERE
- 2026-10-09 — fix(tasks): the Completed card reads by week; the approval queue reads the substituted rows (F1 + F2) — **B2_TIP_SHA = `190b626d5e8f4c3f78ae419fabcb69ef532f8c25`** (every "unchanged" claim below is relative to THAT tip — B2's `getThisWeeksCompletedTasks` one-liner, B2's leaderboard count and the whole `dynamicLeaderboard` memo are intact vs that SHA). **Files:** `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (import + `CompletedWeekGroup`/`CompletedWeekGroups` + `groupCompletedTasksByWeek`, additions only — the sole deletion is the old import line), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (import, `const completedGroups = …` below `completed`, the Completed-card body, `pendingApprovals` source, three approval-target lookups), 3 NEW test suites; `CHANGELOG.md`. Display-only: no point math, no state setters, no server payload, no schema.
  - **F1 — the grouping.** `groupCompletedTasksByWeek(completedTasks, today = localTodayISO())` returns `{pending, weeks, unattributed}`. Attribution precedence: (1) the server `completedInWeek` stamp **wins and short-circuits**; (2) unstamped — `weekStartForDate(localDateOf(completedAt))`, never a UTC slice; (3) neither — `unattributed`, never dated. Group order: this week first **by identity**, then remaining keys descending (a future-dated stamp cannot displace this week). Within a group: `completedAt` desc, undated last, ties on ascending `id` — every sort is on a `slice()` copy, never the caller's array. Pending-approval rows are pulled out entirely and pinned above the groups, most-recently-tapped first. The current week is labelled `"This week"`, every other group `` `Week of ${weekLabel(key)}` `` (the canonical formatter, e.g. "Week of Sep 21–Sep 27"). It consumes B2's `isCompletedInWeek` as the week authority and never redefines it (cross-checked in the pure suite).
  - **Counts, not points — the header decision.** No header carries a points total; each carries a chore count (`group.tasks.length`). A client-side sum would be both forbidden (the wave's no-client-arithmetic rule) and wrong: `task.points` is the exact field the approval pipeline rewrites (`task-claim.ts:1366` writes the paid amount into `pendingApproval.points` while the row's `points` stays base; `task-approval.ts:1270` clears it). The render suite machine-gates every header: no `/\d+\s*pts?/i`, and a count present.
  - **The mislabel removed.** The old per-row token `task.completedInWeek === weekData.weekStart ? "This week" : "Past"` (plan line `page.tsx:3025`, actual `:3119` at the B2 tip) is **gone**; the week is the group header directly above, so an unstamped this-week row can no longer print "Past" — the class of row B2's predicate exists to rescue. `grep` for the quoted token in `page.tsx` returns no hit (the one remaining mention is an unquoted comment; the render suite also asserts the card's text contains no "Past").
  - **F2 — approval queue source.** `pendingApprovals` is now `interactiveRows.filter(isPendingApproval)` — the same substituted source every other surface reads (queued edit's copy substituted, queued delete's row already hidden). This is a **latent contract defect**, not a reproducible parent-visible symptom: with today's UI a completed pending-approval row cannot be edited or deleted (both gated behind `startEdit`, reachable only from `pending.map`, while `isPendingApproval` requires `completed`), so no tap sequence and no fixture can produce the divergence — hence a source-scan RED, not a render test. Approve-all's `taskIds` selection is **B1a's line and is already fixed** (`!optimisticRemoved.includes(p.id)`); F2 neither touches nor duplicates it, and the suite pins that shape. F2's source change *incidentally* removes queued-deleted ids from `taskIds` (interactiveRows is built from `serverTasks`) — an incidental consequence, not F2's fix.
  - **F2 deviation (forced by the lint gate).** The plan said all three `target` lookups read `interactiveRows.find(…)`. The two inside `submitApproval` are declared ABOVE the `interactiveRows` memo, and capturing that memo from an earlier closure makes the React Compiler bail out (`react-hooks/preserve-manual-memoization` on every existing memo — 8 findings, measured by bisection). Those two now read `pendingApprovals.find(…)` — itself `interactiveRows.filter(isPendingApproval)`, the same substituted queue, and the same pattern B1a's `taskIds` arm already uses. The render-time PIN-dialog lookup (after the memo) reads `interactiveRows` directly. Targeted ESLint is 0 findings.

- 2026-10-09 — fix(tasks): one week-attribution predicate; leaderboard and wall read the family clock (B2) — **Files:** `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (new `isCompletedInWeek`; `getThisWeeksCompletedTasks` is a one-liner over it; the retired `getMemberAllTimeCompletions` routed through it; `needsStreakSave` reads the LOCAL day), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (leaderboard per-member count; the streak-save memo now delegates to `needsStreakSave`), `src/components/leaderboard/hooks/useLeaderboardData.ts` (per-member count), `src/lib/screensaver/compose.ts` + `src/lib/screensaver/payload.ts` (wall scoreboard); tests: NEW `tests/unit/task-week-attribution.test.ts`, extended `this-week-completed-tasks-local-day`, `meals-week-utils-tz`, `screensaver-compose`, `task-approve-route`, `task-claim-undo-integrity`. Display/count correctness only — no point math, no lock order, no write path, no schema, no `page.tsx` Completed-card token (see ESC-1).
  - **D1/D2 — one predicate replaces the divergent re-derivations.** `isCompletedInWeek(task, weekStart, today?)` is the ONE answer to "completed inside the week that starts on this local Monday": the `completedInWeek` stamp wins; an unstamped row converts `completedAt` with `localDateOf` and bounds it by the LOCAL week start and `today`; another week's stamp is never overridden. `getThisWeeksCompletedTasks`, both leaderboard counts (`page.tsx` + `useLeaderboardData.ts`), the retired `getMemberAllTimeCompletions`, the streak-save gate and the wall scoreboard all call it. The old `t.completedAt >= currentMonday` (a UTC instant lexically compared to a date-only Monday) over-counted exactly the Sunday 20:00–24:00 EDT window after the rollover flipped `weekData.weekStart`; the source scan in the new suite makes that raw compare un-reintroducible in the three files that had it.
  - **D1 (major) — the leaderboard counts.** Both surfaces use the predicate; `useLeaderboardData.ts` also takes its week from `weekData.weekStart` instead of a client-side `localWeekStartISO()`, so the count and the points beside it describe ONE week (the import stays — the previous-week-rank fallback still uses it). **Known unpinned edge (recorded):** the hook's *week-source* half is correct but unpinned — reverting `weekData.weekStart` to `localWeekStartISO()` leaves the whole suite green (verified by mutation); the count *formula* is pinned, the week the count is taken against is not. A candidate for the F1/U3 pass.
  - **A2 (major) — the wall scoreboard dropped unstamped completions.** `choreProgress` counted `done` by a strict stamp compare and `open` by `status !== "done"`, so an unstamped completed row fell out of BOTH buckets and the family total silently shrank. It now derives the completed signal as `t.completed ?? t.status === "done"` (legacy status-only rows keep working), counts `done` with the predicate, and takes an optional `today` (the payload passes the one it already computed). The existing `{done:1, total:3}` pin passes unchanged. Plan-fixture correction: the new test's open row is due ON the week end (the plan's `2026-10-13` fixture could not produce the documented `{done:1, total:2}` against the unchanged due-bound rule).
  - **A4 (major) — the streak-save nag sliced a UTC date.** Both copies (`task-utils.ts` `needsStreakSave` and the page's duplicated memo) read `completedAt.split("T")[0]`, so a chore finished 20:00–24:00 EDT read as tomorrow and the banner asked for a save already earned, every evening. The helper now compares `localDateOf(t.completedAt) === today` (its comment corrected to state what the code does); the page memo delegates to the helper and its duplicated body is deleted (the banner's `myEntry?.streak` / `myPendingQuests[0]` inputs are unchanged).
  - **Adjudicated non-defects (pinned, not "fixed").** (D3) claim deliberately uses a bare `localWeekStartISO()` with no `ensureCurrentTaskWeek()`: it creates the current-week row when missing and serialises against the rollover on the SAME week-ledger lock — two new pins (row creation; a held lock blocks the claim until release) and zero production change. (D5) the `localWeekStartISO` seam is zone-invariant: `weekStartForDate` depends only on the date string — characterization under six zones including local-midnight DST gaps. (D6) a past-week stamp approved this week keeps the stamp, pays the AUTHORITY week, and is counted by NO this-week surface — pinned so F1/U3 cannot "reconcile" it. (D4) send-back clears `completedBy`/`completedAt`/`completedInWeek` on BOTH stores; the existing send-back test now seeds all three and asserts the nulls. (A1) the retired all-time helper is routed through the predicate so it cannot be revived into the same bug (still dead code, its suite untouched). (A3) a paid crew completion (`completedBy: "Crew"`) is inside the family StatTile and no per-member count — recorded as a deliberate display decision for F1/U3, not fixed here. (A5) the stamp-only double-tap guard stays; the server's honest `already_completed` refusal for an unstamped `status:"done"` row is now pinned (toast wording is F2/U3's).

  - **What this is, and is not.** A 10-test net pinning what the code ALREADY does across the 23 ledger invariants (I1–I23) — every added test passes on current source, so the commit lands green of its own accord and needs no RED-suite exemption. It closes the seven coverage gaps the audit found (G1–G7) so a later B2/F/U edit that breaks a point invariant fails in the focused tripwire, not at money time. No existing assertion was weakened; the two redeem tests below were strengthened. It installs nothing and changes no point math.
  - **G1 — the RUNNING negative-balance gate, at the seam (tested, NOT installed).** Pins the **private, running** gate `anyAffectedMemberGoNegative` (`src/lib/ledger-operations.ts:322-333`, invoked at `:823` post-merge and `:860` post-write) as member-scoped on the MERGED history: a sibling's stored negative cannot freeze a new earn (`does NOT refuse a NEW earn because a DIFFERENT member's stored balance is already negative`) and the very operation that REPAIRS a deficit applies (`applies the very operation that REPAIRS a member's deficit`). It does **NOT** prove the exported `memberWouldGoNegative` (`src/lib/task-ledger.ts:347`) is enforced — **`memberWouldGoNegative` (`task-ledger.ts:347`) has no production caller and is a second negative policy; do not adopt it alongside `anyAffectedMemberGoNegative`.** B3 **tested** a gate; it did not **install** one. No reviewer should read this entry as new enforcement.
  - **G2 — same-`operationId` approve-all replay (the wave's most likely double-pay).** `is idempotent when the QUEUE replays the batch under the SAME operationId with the taskIds reversed`: first POST pays 2; the verbatim queue replay (`task-command-queue-server.ts:303`) with `[102, 101]` answers `200 {paid: 0, duplicate: true}`, points stay `{Caspian 8, Aurora 5}` and history holds exactly one earn per task. **What this test bites is the `operationId` replay/committed path — not the fingerprint sort:** forcing `committed=false` reds it, while removing either `taskIds` sort (`task-approval.ts:244-250`) does **not**, so the sort canonicalizes the client's build order but is not what the replay dedup rides on. Recorded verbatim so this claim is never read as stronger than the pin.

At the claim seam: `still pays into the authority week when an OLDER week's history will not parse` (older corrupt row, authority write lands) and `answers invalid_task_state — never a duplicate — when the AUTHORITY week itself will not parse` (never a duplicate, because a duplicate would make the outbox delete the entry and leave the points wrong).

- 2026-10-09 — fix(tasks): approvals clear on the ack and the award survives approval (B1a — the live blockers) — **Files:** `src/lib/task-command-store.ts`, `src/lib/task-utils.ts`, `src/lib/task-operation-payload.ts`, `src/lib/task-approval.ts`, `src/lib/task-command-queue-server.ts`, `src/lib/task-commands.ts`, `src/lib/snapshot-tasks.ts`, `src/lib/task-projection-reconciler.ts`, `src/lib/task-claim.ts`, `src/lib/task-recurrence.ts`, `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`, `src/types/tasks.ts`, `src/app/api/tasks/approve/route.ts`, `src/app/api/tasks/sync/route.ts`, `src/app/tasks/page.tsx`, `scripts/tasks/symptom-harness.mjs`; tests: the five applied held suites (`task-approval-cleared-count-honesty`, `task-approval-award-amount-display`, `task-sync-partial-truth-adoption`, `task-command-store-credential-survives-reload`, `task-approval-replay-clear-guard`) + NEW `task-approve-ack-row-clear`, `task-approval-response-shape`, `tasks-award-survives-approval`, `task-awarded-points-projection`, `task-command-store-ack-classification`, `tasks-approval-amount-and-copy`, `task-ack-kill-switch`; eight existing suites updated where a test pinned a deliberately-changed contract.
  - **The four reported symptoms and their root causes.** (a) **Rows stuck after approving** — the approve ack wrote `{pendingApproval:null, sentBackAt:null}` with no proof stamp, and the ack merge carried no `weekData`, so the client's pull gate (`paidElsewhere || sentBackElsewhere`) always refused the clear while the points landed; **send-back cleared and approve did not**, and that asymmetry was the defect's signature. (a′)**approve-all never cleared** — the response described only `active[0]`, so N−1 rows could never clear their own gates. (b) **Points wrong/missing** — the claim records the paid amount in `pendingApproval.points`, but approval cleared it, so every card printed the pre-bonus base after a speed-bonus claim. (c) **A kid's tap never reached the parent's card** — `GET /api/tasks/sync` answered 503 for ANY `reconciled:false`, and both consumers branch on the status alone, so a successfully-read snapshot (row included) was thrown away because the PB mirror repair was pending. (d) **PIN/permission errors** — a PIN-gated command re-sent after a reload carried no credential and was killed terminally, and every persisted `auth-required` was rewritten to `failed` on read, making the "waiting on a PIN" banner dead code.
  - **What now happens.** The approve ack **adopts the clear by task id on the ack itself**, gated only by freshness: `ackClearIsFreshEnough(local, commandCreatedAt, pullGateWouldClear)` refuses to wipe a local tap stamped AFTER the command's creation (the stale-ack hazard: a parent approves, the kid re-taps before the queued approve drains, and the older ack must not erase the newer tap) and falls back to the pull-gate decision when either instant is unreadable. A snapshot still needs ledger proof; an ack needs only to be newer than the tap it answers. **`ACK_CLEAR_AUTHORITATIVE`** (default `true`, one-source constant in `task-command-store.ts`) restores the pre-B1a pull-gate-only clear with a single edit; the suite pins it and the false-branch behaviour is verified by flip (fresh/tie/multi-row clears fail, the stale-tap case and the send-back stamp still pass).
  - **Per-row outcomes and the persisted award.** `ApprovalServiceResult`/`ApproveResponse` gained `clearedTasks` (one row per active task; the route also emits the held suite's `tasks` alias; `task` and the numeric `cleared` keep their meanings), forwarded through `internalResultToOutcome` for the queued path. `approvalPatch` now writes `awardedPoints: intent.amount` on approve (and `null` on send-back); `completedFields`, `reopenTask`, `recurringClone` and the send-back arm clear it; the two PB projection matchers tolerate a missing/0 mirror value; and the cards read `task.awardedPoints ?? task.pendingApproval!.points ?? baseTaskPoints(task)` at the three award sites. **Schema ops:** `tasks.awardedPoints` (number) was added to `pb-seed.ts`, so the NAS must run `npm run pb:seed` BEFORE the redeploy that expects it (the field only exists after the seed heals the live collection).
  - **Unreconciled writes are never banked (D4).** A 2xx whose body carries `reconciled:false`/`repairRequired:true` is retryable `projection_pending` with the server's own sentence — not acknowledged — and the page surfaces a non-terminal ack's `error` as an error-tone toast. **`GET /api/tasks/sync` contract change:** a readable snapshot is handed over on a **200** even when the projection repair is pending (`reconciled:false`, categories on `warnings`, `failed` reserved for read-blocking legs); only the rollover, the day sweep, the snapshot read or a throwing reconciler answer 503 (`snapshot:null`). The old "503 for any unreconciled read" rule was the (c) fault line: it withheld a perfectly readable row from both consumers.
  - **PIN death and the copy fixes.** A credential-requiring operation (`approve`, `ledger`) re-sent with no credential in memory parks **`auth-required`** (reason `pin_required`) instead of dying — both the 401/403 refusal and the approve parser's `400 invalid_body` are that case — `parseTaskOperationEntry` preserves `auth-required` across reloads, and the 24h sweep parks it instead of reporting `queue_expired`. The wrong-PIN review dialog now says "That PIN wasn't right — try again." (the permission sentence is kept for a roster with no parent, `unreachableCopy()` for an outage); all three review openers funnel through one `openApprovalDialog` that clears the dialog-timer registry; the refusal vocabulary covers the approve route's codes (`unknown_task`/`unknown-task`, `ambiguous_task`, `semantic_duplicate`, `repair_required`, `task_store_unavailable`, …); and approve-all's id selection now excludes rows with a queued delete (a 404 there made the queued removal permanent through the stranded-id self-heal).
  - **Verification.** Held phase-0 suites 1, 2, 4, 6, 7 green in the same commit; suite 3 stayed green. Full suite **6,506/6,506 across 572 files** (0B baseline 6,450/559); `npm run typecheck` 0 errors; `npm run build` clean; targeted ESLint 0 findings. The approved pre-existing `tasks-approve-all` teardown errors were re-measured: **2 → 0** (the file's unmount ordering already fixed them; not a baseline update). `npm run harness:tasks` → **ALL CHECKS PASSED (12)**, 4×4 matrix no blank cells: (a) `a8 survivedAck:false`, cleared on the ack with **1 ms** latency (was 55,375 ms); (b) the card is read BEFORE the ack now — `cardMetaLine:"9pts"`, `cardShowsTaskPoints:false`, server paid 9, and the crew line reads `"4pts each"` with the ledger paying 4+4; (c) `c2` cross-device latency **55,574 ms → PASS (≤180,000)**, `c8` still `drainedWithoutReader:false` (the single drain trigger is **B1b's** `task-queue-drain-trigger`); (d) `d4` terminalStatus **`auth-required`/`pin_required`**, `d5 bannerVisible:true`, `authRequiredEntries:1`; `b4` no longer null — the parked approve no longer holds the batch, so the approve-all POSTs (`status:200, paid:2, cleared:2`).
  - **Tests deliberately re-pinned (one line each).** (1) `task-approval-cleared-count-honesty` test 2's original assertion (`cleared < prepared` unconditionally) was mathematically exclusive with test 1's `cleared === paid`; it now uses a REAL partial fixture — another writer tombstones row 102 between prepare and the snapshot write, so the patch conflicts while the ledger still pays both — and asserts `cleared:0`, `reconciled:false`, 202, `projectionFailures:[101,102]`, `clearedTasks:[]`. No tautology, no dead branch. (2) `task-sync-partial-truth-adoption`'s fake returned the snapshot as a JSON *string* (production's json column is an object) — fixture corrected, no assertion weakened. (3) `task-redeem-outbox-payload` ×2, `tasks-outbox-commands` ×1, `rewards-shop-parent-approval` ×2, `tasks-crew-flow` ×1: a 202 `reconciled:false` is now retried, not adopted (D4). (4) `tasks-pending-flow` ×1: wrong PIN now says "That PIN wasn't right", not "Parent PIN required" (D6). (5) `task-route-auth-and-honesty` + `tasks-sync-legs` ×5: projection-repair-pending sync answers 200 with the categories on `warnings` (the B1a sync contract). (6) `tasks-crew-flow` gained the missing `__resetTaskOutboxForTests()` in `beforeEach` that the D4 retry exposed.
  - **Critic fix round (2026-10-09, same ship point).** (a) **Ordering:** an `auth-required` entry is parked, not in flight — `heldByEarlierTaskCommand` now exempts it like `failed`, so a later same-task command (the harness b4 approve-all) POSTs instead of being held forever; the parked retry is proven non-double-paying against the real route (`a retried single approve after an approve-all paid the row is a no-op`), and its lifetime is bounded/visible via the queue banner's `auth-required` count + Cancel and the store's 7-day/cap eviction. (b) **D4's family half:** the queue banner renders a non-failed entry's `lastErrorMessage` (the server's sentence) and `LEDGER_REFUSAL_COPY` gained `projection_pending`; tested by `surfaces an unreconciled retry's sentence in the queue banner, never as a refusal` + `a fully reconciled approval is never described as refused`. (c) **Queued path:** `internalResultToOutcome` no longer stores an ok-but-unreconciled result `resolved`; it retries `projection_pending` (test in `task-command-queue-server`). (d) **Skew:** `APPROVAL_PROOF_SKEW_MS = 5_000` is kept, named and documented (ARCHITECTURE + this entry): the strict comparison could not clear the `task-approval-replay-clear-guard` row (tap stamped 1 s after the proof) and boundary tests now pin both sides — inside clears, one millisecond outside refuses and leaves the newer tap standing. (e) **Kill switch:** `ACK_CLEAR_AUTHORITATIVE` is an `export let` with a test-only setter so both branches are exercised; the false branch feeds the ack's `weekData` into the merge so the pull-gate proof path genuinely clears, and the branch-distinguishing test asserts the switch ALONE decides the no-proof fresh-ack clear.
  - **Final fix round (2026-10-09).** **Honest terminal copy:** a retry-exhausted command is not a refusal — it may have applied server-side while nothing confirmed it — so the terminal toast now says `UNCONFIRMED_CHANGE_COPY` ("Consuela couldn't confirm that change — it may still land.") for any `network`-category terminal, on BOTH the untracked approval path and the tracked ledger-op path; `LEDGER_REFUSAL_COPY.projection_pending` is the same sentence, and an evicted entry names the local drop without the false "the server refused" prefix. Tests: `a command that exhausts retries while unresolved never reads as a refusal` and its tracked sibling `a manual adjust that exhausts retries is never described as refused` (the adjust's "didn't go through" line is never shown). **Harness prose:** every narrative field in the symptom-report JSON is now computed from the measured values (a8 guard/verdict/detail, the b class verdict, b2/b4 cells, the d class verdict, d4/d5 verdicts and detail), so the report can no longer contradict its own run.
  - **Known follow-ups (recorded, not lost).** The persisted `awardedPoints` has no rendered site on an already-approved "Done" row yet (the three B1a-owned reads are the Needs-approval meta, the crew award line and the Completed card's pending row); rendering it on the Done row is U1/U2 territory. The harness's optimistic-row wait could miss a row that flashed for less than one frame, so it accepts an already-acked tap on the strength of the observed claim POST (the same fallback its outbox read already used); the b2/b3 card reads now happen BEFORE the ack and record `cardMetaLine:"9pts"` / `cardCrewLine:"4pts each"` as genuine display-vs-pay evidence. `pendingPointsFor` stays uncalled (no client points arithmetic). The `auth-required` re-prompt currently rides the outbox banner's count + Cancel; a dedicated re-enter-PIN affordance is not part of B1a.
  - **Ops (owned by the deploy step, human-gated).** Snapshot PB (`week_data`, `tasks`, `task_command_queue`) + restore command; run the points reconciliation check (ledger history vs `week_data.points`) before and after; on the NAS run **`npm run pb:seed` FIRST** (heals `tasks.awardedPoints`), then redeploy; smoke `/`, `/tasks`, a real approve, `/api/tasks/queue` → 401/403 unauthenticated. `ACK_CLEAR_AUTHORITATIVE` is one edit away. No new env, dependency or secret.
- 2026-10-07 — fix(tasks): queue status route now 503s on a PB store outage (the documented contract) — listTaskCommandQueueState propagates read failures instead of masking them as an empty queue; the client no longer releases server-queued mirrors on a false-empty read. Files: src/lib/task-command-queue-server.ts, tests.
- 2026-10-07 — feat(tasks): the server command queue replaces the browser outbox — a task tap is now durable on the family server, not in one browser's localStorage — **Files:** NEW `src/lib/task-command-store.ts` (thin client command store), `src/lib/task-command-queue-server.ts` (enqueue/sweep/list/cancel/drain), `src/lib/task-operation-payload.ts` (shared payload + reason vocabulary), `src/lib/task-config-apply.ts` (config apply seam shared by route + drain), `src/app/api/tasks/queue/route.ts`; MODIFIED `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (declares `task_command_queue`), `src/lib/task-command-queue.ts`, `src/lib/task-config-client.ts`, the six command routes (`/api/tasks/{claim,approve,manage,config,ledger}` + `/api/rewards/redeem`), `src/hooks/{useTaskCommandQueue,useTaskOperationOutbox}.ts`, `src/app/tasks/page.tsx`; DELETED `src/lib/task-operation-outbox.ts` + its 4 old suites; NEW tests `tests/unit/task-command-queue-server.test.ts` (16), `tests/unit/task-command-store-lifecycle.test.ts` (13), `tests/unit/task-command-store-unknown-task-heal.test.ts` (3), `tests/unit/task-queue-route.test.ts` (6), `tests/unit/pb-seed-task-command-queue.test.ts` (1); PORTED `tests/unit/task-redeem-outbox-payload.test.ts` (22); 7 suites re-pinned.
  - **The contract.** When a command route's service call fails transiently (task store unreachable mid-write), the route writes the VERIFIED command as a `task_command_queue` row and answers **`202 { queued: true }`** — the family's tap is durable across devices, reloads and reboots, and the sync drain replays it through the same service seam. The local entry stays as a live mirror of that row until `/api/tasks/queue` reports resolution; a `2xx` ack still means the body carries the authoritative week, a `4xx` is an immediate terminal refusal, and network/5xx uses the local replay buffer.
  - **The collection.** `task_command_queue` (16 fields): `operationId`, `route`, `action`, `payload` (json, 1 MiB — sized for the claim route's photo-assignee body ceiling), `actorMemberId`, `actorName`, `actorRole`, `actorAuthentication`, `status`, `attemptCount`, `nextAttemptAt`, `lastErrorReason`, `lastErrorMessage`, `result`, `displayTarget`, `resolvedAt`; a UNIQUE index on `operationId` plus a `(status, nextAttemptAt)` index; every rule stays locked admin-only like every other collection (`pb-seed` re-heals drift).
  - **A PIN never enters a queue row.** Intake verifies the credential and stores only the verified actor identity (`actorMemberId`/`actorName`/`actorRole`/`actorAuthentication`); the PIN lives only in the client's ephemeral in-memory credential registry keyed by `operationId`, and the queue-server suite pins that no `pin`/`parentPin`/`password`/`token` key can reach a row.
  - **`GET`/`DELETE /api/tasks/queue`.** Live-session gated (`requireLiveSession`); GET lists pending rows, recent failures and terminal markers still inside their retention window; DELETE cancels one pending row by operation id — the original actor or any parent, and an already-terminal row is a no-op success; a store outage answers **503 `queue_unavailable`** so a device keeps its local view instead of mistaking an outage for an empty queue (never a false empty 200).
  - **The client is now a thin replay buffer.** Same localStorage key (`consuela-task-operation-outbox-v1`) — now a single JSON array; the legacy index + `:entry:` layout rehydrates once on cold read. Local-only failures back off 2s→5min, 8 attempts, 24h expiry (a parked entry older than a day goes terminal `failed`), replaying on online/visibility/mount; a bounded second flush pass picks up work enqueued mid-flush; same-task commands hold creation order (payload `taskId` + `taskIds`); cap eviction emits terminal events and releases credentials; a failed enqueue forgets its credential; the restored `unknown_task` self-heal tombstones a stranded id device-side and acknowledges the entry instead of failing it forever.
  - **Tests (61 added/ported across 6 suites).** Ported `task-redeem-outbox-payload` **22**; new `task-command-store-unknown-task-heal` **3** (the heal + its two fail-closed guards); new `task-command-store-lifecycle` **13** (B1 in-flight cancel refusal, B2 one terminal event + credential release, B3 eviction event + credential release, B4 two-pass flush, B5 same-task ordering + the stale-`reconciling` re-send fix, B8 legacy rehydrate, B9 failed-enqueue cleanup); new `task-command-queue-server` **16** (enqueue/idempotency, sweep/expiry/retention, actor-or-parent cancel, list, drain through the service seam); new `task-queue-route` **6** (auth, pass-through, 503, malformed body, cancel refusal, success args); new `pb-seed-task-command-queue` **1** (all 16 fields + the unique index). Task 8 re-pinned **9 failures across 7 suites**: `config-outbox-callers` ×2, `kid-claim-outbox` ×1, `kidhome-outbox-commands` ×1, `pb-seed-field-heal` ×1 (collection count 46→47), `task-config-clients` ×1, `task-week-integrity-fixes` ×1, `tasks-outbox-commands` ×2.
  - **Per-test intent changes in the ported redeem suite (one line each).** (1) Double-tap second flush `{1,0,0}` → `{0,0,0}` — the first flush marks the entry in-flight and the new store has no driver-keyed promise coalescing. (2) A bare non-queued 202 is an acknowledgement, not retention — adopt + release; only `202 { queued: true }` keeps a mirror. (3) A 401 is terminal `failed`/`unauthorized` at tap time (credential forgotten), not retryable `auth-required`. (4) The sentinel-401 test is now a terminal refusal — no credential state (missing or restored) re-plays it. (5) Sentinel 409 reason `operation_conflict` → `http_409` (vocabulary reasons only when the server declares them). (6) A 409 no longer triggers a snapshot pull inside flush (`pulls` 1 → 0); authoritative reads belong to sync/poll. (7) A wrong PIN is terminal `failed`/`unauthorized` with the credential forgotten.
  - **Retired contracts (deliberate — one line each).** retired: proof-polling / `ledgerProvesResolved` — replaced by the server queue's receipts and the `202 {queued:true}` contract. retired: the B6 deferred auth ladder (`auth-required` parking + bounded re-POST) — replaced by terminal 401/403 refusal at tap time. retired: B7 driver-keyed flush coalescing (one shared promise per driver) — `requestTaskOutboxFlush` coalesces per microtask and in-flight operation ids prevent duplicate sends. retired: B10 re-queue budget preservation — re-enqueueing a known id resets the LOCAL `attemptCount` to 0 (bounds each burst, not the id's lifetime); the server row's budget survives via the server's idempotent enqueue. retired: local no-credential refusal (old B1: send skipped → `auth-required`) — the store sends bare and lets the server refuse. retired: per-entry storage keys + orphan-index pruning — one JSON array under `TASK_OUTBOX_STORAGE_KEY`, legacy layout folded once. retired: enqueue-path automatic pump scheduling — replaced by `queueTaskCommandAndFlush` and the hook's mount/online/visibility flush.
  - **Verification.** `npm run typecheck` 0 errors · full suite **6,447/6,447 across 558 files** (baseline before the repair wave: 9 failed / 6,438 passed of 6,447) · `npm run build` clean (54/54 static pages) · targeted ESLint 0 findings on all 7 re-pinned files.
  - **Known follow-ups (recorded, not lost).** (a) A `stale_config` 409 no longer adopts the refusal body's authoritative catalog, so the visible list repairs on the next config read rather than at refusal time; the `onTaskOutboxAdopted` repair wiring in both config cards is now dead for this path. (b) `acknowledgeEntry` removes the entry and fires the terminal event BEFORE awaiting adoption, inverting the 2026-09-25 "adopts before release" order — display-only today (the `writeTaskConfig` callers discard the response; the stores adopt a microtask later) but the store header comment still claims the old order. (c) The 202-mirror test pins `status:"queued"`/`nextAttemptAt:null`; a real pending row inside its 2s backoff mirrors as `retrying` — the resolution poll, not the status string, releases the mirror.
  - **Ops.** On the NAS run `npm run pb:seed` (creates `task_command_queue` + its unique `operationId` index + locked rules). **No `migrate:features` run is needed for this feature.** Ordinary redeploy; no new env, no dependency, no secret.
- 2026-10-06 — feat(photos): RAW (.dng + common) uploads — the embedded JPEG preview drives the wall — **Files:** NEW `src/lib/photos/raw-preview.ts`, `tests/unit/raw-preview.test.ts`, `tests/unit/photos-raw-upload.test.tsx`; MODIFIED `src/lib/photos/upload-limits.ts`, `src/components/photos/PhotoUploader.tsx`, `src/app/api/photos/upload/route.ts`, `src/db/features/photos.ts`, `src/db/features/migrate.ts`, `tests/unit/photos-upload.test.ts`, `tests/unit/migrate-reconcile.test.ts`.
  - **The defect.** A `.dng` was rejected at three layers — the uploader's client guard (`isAllowedImageType`), the upload route's allowlist, and PocketBase's `photos.original.mimeTypes`. The deeper reason it was never added: a RAW is a TIFF container no browser can decode, so `createImageBitmap` fails, `resizeForWall` returns null, and the wall feed's `wall || original` fallback would have served an unrenderable file (the widget's own error path then advanced off it).
  - **Fix.** Every accepted RAW (DNG, CR2, NEF, ARW, PEF, SR2, SRW, RWL, `.raw`) is TIFF-based and embeds a JPEG preview. New `raw-preview.ts` is a dependency-free, bounds-checked TIFF-IFD parser that walks IFD0 + SubIFDs (tag 330), reads `JPEGInterchangeFormat`/`…Length` (513/514) and JPEG-compressed strips (273/279 with Compression 7), validates `FF D8 FF … FF D9` framing, and returns the LARGEST preview as `image/jpeg` — else `null`, never a throw. The uploader resizes that preview into the `wall` field (the RAW stays as `original`); a preview-less RAW (CR3/RAF, or an unusual DNG) is archived with `noWall=true` → `showOnWall:false` and honest copy ("Saved to archive — no wall preview"), never a broken tile.
  - **Allowlist + schema.** `ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES` stays the strict wall set; new `ALLOWED_ORIGINAL_TYPES` adds the RAW aliases and `isAllowedOriginalFile()` falls back to the extension (browsers declare RAW as `""`). Because PocketBase detects by content magic, every TIFF-based RAW arrives as **`image/tiff`** — that value is now in `photos.original.mimeTypes` alongside the aliases, pinned to `ALLOWED_ORIGINAL_TYPES` by the parity test.
  - **ORF/RW2 are intentionally not accepted:** their containers are not TIFF-magic-42 and PocketBase's content sniff reports them `application/octet-stream`, which the schema rejects — advertising them would 502 on every upload.
  - **The migration must heal it.** Editing a schema changes nothing on a live collection, so new `reconcileFileFieldMimeTypes()` (sibling of `reconcileFileFieldLimits`) writes the accepted-type list back in the shape it was read (flat ≥0.23 / nested <0.23) and only for a field that already expresses the option. `migrate:features` runs both passes on the skipped path.
  - **Tests.** `raw-preview` 17 (IFD0, SubIFD, strip-JPEG, largest-wins, big-endian, and the honest-null shapes: no preview, non-TIFF, out-of-bounds offsets, non-JPEG candidate, 1025-strip count cap, >64 MiB declared strip total); `photos-raw-upload` 3 (extract→resize the preview, preview-less→`noWall`, ordinary image unaffected); `photos-upload` +5 (client allowlist, declared/empty RAW types, route accept + `noWall`, still rejects `.gif`) and the schema-parity pin; `migrate-reconcile` +4 (flat/nested heal, no drift, option-absent untouched). Full suite **6,560/6,560** across 557 files; `tsc` clean; `npm run build` clean; targeted ESLint clean.
  - **Ops.** On the NAS run `npm run migrate:features` (heals `photos.original.mimeTypes` to include `image/tiff` + RAW aliases), then redeploy. No new env, no new dependency, no collection drop.

Creating `photo_settings` ✅ and `photos.original.maxSize` → 100 MB healed on live; the seven dormant feature collections that reference a `users` collection this NAS has never had remain outstanding by design (recorded in the open-findings doc). Files: `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`, `src/db/features/migrate.ts`, `tests/unit/snapshot-size-cap-guards.test.ts` (+1 unset-drift pin), `tests/unit/migrate-reconcile.test.ts` (fakes gain the `_superusers` auth contract). Full suite 555/555. 
- 2026-10-05 — fix(tasks): the task-page integrity wave — full audit+fix of the task system, +684 tests (5,846 → 6,530), 549 → 555 suites. **Files (selected):** `../docker-compose.yml` (parent), `src/lib/local-date.ts`, `src/lib/task-ledger-command.ts`, `src/app/api/tasks/ledger/route.ts`, `src/lib/task-operation-outbox.ts`, `src/lib/task-command-queue.ts`, `src/hooks/useTaskOperationOutbox.ts`, `src/lib/task-claim.ts`, `src/lib/ledger-operations.ts`, `src/lib/keyed-lock.ts`, `src/lib/task-week-rollover.ts`, `src/lib/task-day-sweep.ts`, `src/lib/hall-of-fame-backfill.ts`, `src/lib/all-time-totals.ts`, `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`, `src/app/api/members/admin/route.ts`, `src/lib/task-projection-reconciler.ts`, `src/lib/task-ledger.ts`, `src/app/api/tasks/{sync,all-time,quarantine,claim,approve}/route.ts`, `src/lib/cron-auth.ts`, `src/lib/member-pins.ts`, `src/lib/server-auth.ts`, `src/app/tasks/page.tsx`, `src/components/tasks/*`, `src/components/leaderboard/*`, `src/components/ui/{SoftButton,SegmentedControl,IconButton,Chip,Toast,SyncStatusBanner,Surface,SwipeableRow,WeatherWidget,EmergencyButton}.tsx`, `src/components/patterns/{PageHeader,StatTile}.tsx`, `src/components/settings/WeeklyPrizesCard.tsx`, `src/lib/theme-config.ts`, `src/lib/ai-suggestions.ts`, `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx`, `src/app/globals.css`, `src/lib/task-manage.ts`, `src/lib/task-emoji.ts`, `src/lib/snapshot-tasks.ts`; 16 NEW test suites (`family-timezone`, `task-ledger-actor-split`, `task-outbox-lifecycle-fixes`, `task-claim-undo-integrity`, `task-week-integrity-fixes`, `task-rename-and-reconcile-resilience`, `task-route-auth-and-honesty`, `cron-auth-timing-safe`, `tasks-page-integrity-fixes`, `tasks-components-a11y-fixes`, `leaderboard-shared-surface-fixes`, `weekly-prizes-shared-fixes`, `task-final-followups`, `task-adjacent-final-followups`, `snapshot-size-cap-guards`, `visual-residual-fixes`) + ~10 existing suites updated where a test pinned a DELIBERATELY-changed contract (each one re-read its title before the edit; nothing was weakened to reach green).
  - **How this ran.** Eight read-only audit agents (4 backend / 4 frontend), then fix waves on disjoint file sets, then four harsh visual critics running shoot→look→measure→fix→re-shoot loops (up to 3 rounds each) at 390/768/1280/1536/1920 × light × dark. Every critical claim was HAND-VERIFIED before fixing — two audit "criticals" ("Add/Edit task 100% broken") were FALSE (the outbox already sanitizes manage payloads) and were dropped. **`git stash` was violated once by the orchestrator mid-wave and immediately popped; nothing was lost, and the rule is now load-bearing (see the findings doc §6.1).**
  - 🚨 **The worst single defect: production ran on the UTC axis.** The parent `docker-compose.yml` (the one `deploy.sh` actually runs) had **no `TZ`**, so the `node:*-alpine` container resolved UTC: the weekly points reset fired **Sunday 20:00 ET** instead of Monday 00:00, Sunday's last 3.5h were seized into the next week, and the day sweep fired 4h early. `vitest.config.ts` pins `America/Detroit`, which is why 5,846 green tests never saw it. Fixed with one compose line, AND `local-date.ts` now validates the zone at module load (`InvalidFamilyTimeZoneError` naming `TZ` on an invalid value like `America/Detriot`, which previously surfaced as an undiagnosed `RangeError` in every time-dependent route).
  - 🚨 **The live incident the spec captures**: every member-assigned "add task" failed with `snapshot_write_failed` — the snapshot row's `data` JSON had grown to **959,836 of the 1,048,576-byte PocketBase cap**, because each task copied the assignee's roster emoji, which are 105–246 KB **photo data URLs** (98% of the blob). Fixed in four legs: (1) write-point slimming — `TASK_MANAGE_MAX_EMOJI_LENGTH` 400,000 → **4,096** (the old constant alone allowed one task to exceed the entire cap), `slimTaskEmoji` on every persist site (rendering is live-roster-first, so rows still show real photos from `members.emoji`); (2) `pb-seed` declares + heals `consuela_data_snapshots.data` `maxSize` to **8 MiB** (raise-only — never lowers a bigger field); (3) the silent `catch`es now log `status`+a redacted 200-char body (`data:` payloads and base64 runs stripped); (4) `slimTaskEmojis` slims the legacy fat blob on read, and the next snapshot write persists it (reconcile guards stay revision-stable). **Ops: `npm run pb:seed` on the NAS, redeploy, then delete the leftover `PROBE-POINTS-1789853187732` task through the app's own delete command.**
  - **Money/points integrity.** (a) Penalties and manual adjusts against a child were **impossible** — the ledger route bound PIN-subject, `role==="parent"` gate, and debit target to one `memberName` field, so a parent PIN under a child's name was always 401 while the UI printed "the family server confirms". The command now carries **actor + optional `targetMemberName`** (defaulting to the actor, byte-identical when absent), verified live as two identities; `LEDGER_PAYLOAD_KEYS` admits the target so an offline-queued child command can no longer strip it and become a self-adjust. (b) Undo now reverses an earn in the week that HOLDS it (previously only the current week was read, so a Sunday-paid earn was unreversible from Monday and the outbox consumed the refusal as a "duplicate"), and a CREW completion — `completedBy:"Crew"`, previously reversible by NO identity — can be reversed by a parent PIN or by each earn's own member. (c) Replay detection is now archive-aware: an `operationId` applied in an archived week can no longer pay a second time on Monday's flush. (d) `withKeyedLock` sections are bounded at 120 s — one hung PocketBase socket previously wedged EVERY points write for the week until a container restart. (e) A member rename migrates the ledger (`week_data`/`week_archive` points+streak+lastActive+history + snapshot weekData) all-or-nothing per week, refuses onto an existing-history name with an honest code, and all-time no longer reports a renamed member's new name as a confident `{points: 0}` (stored-map-only names are now honest `null`s).
  - **Week machinery.** The day sweep now closes deadline crews BEFORE culling (a crew that showed up after expiry previously lost its points to a tombstone); the rollover archives EVERY older week (was only the immediately-prior one, orphaning skipped weeks while all-time still claimed `historyComplete: true`); archive repair is lossless (it previously rewrote the archive from `week_data` and deleted the divergent row, destroying archive-only transactions — at the week boundary this was a real race, now covered by ascending week locks); editing or deleting a weekly prize no longer rewrites FROZEN historical Hall-of-Fame prize text; conflicting duplicate archives now yield honest-unknown all-time instead of whichever row PocketBase happened to return first; two same-titled recurring chores no longer collapse into one lineage and tombstone each other; `week_archive` gained its missing `lastActive` field (would have bricked the rollover the moment it was populated); one unparseable prior-week transaction now fails SOFT with a category instead of throwing out of `ensureCurrentTaskWeek` and bricking every point-earning route. Also: `GET /api/tasks/sync` now actually AUTHORIZES (it was `GET()` with no request — a removed member's 7-day cookie could still trigger the rollover and read the whole family snapshot), and an unreconciled read answers 503 with the full body instead of 200-with-`ok:false`, which both consumers were taking as green.
  - **Outbox honesty.** Cancel no longer aborts an operation the server ALREADY applied (the status now goes in-flight BEFORE the request, and cancel refuses it) — the old race let a cancelled "complete" stay paid server-side while the row snapped back. A terminal failure now emits the acknowledgment so optimistic marks are RELEASED (a rejected add rendered "⏳ Saving" forever beside "⚠️ couldn't be sent"; a rejected delete vanished the chore). Same-task commands are serialized (a backoff no longer lets edit #2 land before edit #1), cap-eviction notifies, the pump picks up work enqueued mid-flush, the deferred-401 ladder has the attempt ceiling (was infinite — an expired session flashed "🔒 waiting on a PIN" forever when the answer was sign-in), two pumps can't run over one outbox, a legacy array-shaped index rehydrates on cold read, a throwing enqueue forgets its PIN, and a re-queue preserves the attempt budget. The hook now exposes `retrying` separately (a 5-minute backoff no longer reads as "Sending").
  - **Auth hygiene.** The three remaining non-timing-safe compares are on digest-both-sides `timingSafeEqual` now (`cron-auth.ts`, `member-pins.ts`, `server-auth.ts` — the emergency route's digest-first idiom, with `member-pins.ts` deliberately pure-JS because it ships to the browser bundle); `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember` no longer stops at the first match (constant roster work); `/api/tasks/{claim,approve,ledger}` — the three middleware-exempt routes — now 413 an oversized body BEFORE reading it (was an unbounded unauthenticated parse); the claim route's error body finally carries `error`/`code` like its siblings; the quarantine route refuses an unknown/malformed `weekStart` as `409 canonical_week_unknown` and never fabricates an empty canonical ledger (the old answer made any byte-divergent device report its whole real history as bogus).
  - **The UI pass.** 30 items on `page.tsx` + 12 leaderboard + 11 tasks-component items, all found by critics on real screenshots: a dismissed crew PIN dialog no longer hijacks the NEXT PIN action (four stale state fields collapsed into one `pinIntent` union); buttons no longer say "the family server confirms" before anything went over the wire; the toast's failure tone is a real argument instead of `text.includes("Failed")` (never matched — every failure rendered mint-green "success"); identity is resolved by full name, never `startsWith` ("Alex" no longer matches "Alexandra" for points, streak and level-up); the rewards/penalties admin surface and the family-goal editor are gated on the VIEWER being a parent (a signed-out guest could reach both); a refresh read failure no longer renders "All caught up"; every PIN dialog announces its error via `role="alert"` (was bare `<p>` text wiped on the first keystroke); success timers, toast timers and the ack listener are leak-free; the per-day swipers' edit affordance exists for keyboard users; every tint sits on the `--color-accent-ink-*` family (measured, not eyeballed: TaskRewardsPanel 3.07:1 → clear, CrewTasksCard 3.63:1 → clear, quarantine notice 2.71/3.99:1 → clear); every board card is `h2`; the leaderboard's tied rows read 1, 1, 3, 3, 5 everywhere (the page previously printed `#0` for every tied row, reading the placeholder rank off the not-yet-mapped array); a zero-point week crowns nobody on ANY surface; `DueDatePicker` is a real grid (one tab stop, arrow keys, human dates, 35/42 cells, a visible selected state at 4.58–12.49:1, and "Next week" lands on the next Monday even on a Monday); `SoftButton variant="secondary"` measured 6.99–10.84:1 dark / 6.00–10.27:1 light across ALL TEN accents (was 1.88–3.42 dark — worst violet 1.88); the documented `text-muted`/`text-dim` 4.49:1/4.21:1 debt is gone (1220 samples, 278/278 AA clears); High Contrast mode was a wireframe (a `border-width: 2px !important` on `*` created 299 borders including on bare text) and is now scoped to surface primitives. The theme preset default no longer inline-pins the LIGHT accent hex on `<html>` over every dark rule.
  - **An AI-output guard worth keeping:** the same `className`-order trap recurred twice more here (`Chip` selected white-on-transparent; `.member-tile.is-active` reading the raw `--chip-color` where a per-member accent can't take the selected-relative ink), and the unlayered-vs-`@layer` rule held exactly as documented. Both are now measured-clean.
  - **Verification.** `npm run typecheck` clean · `npx eslint` clean on every touched file · full suite **6,530/6,530 across 555 files** · `npm run build` exit 0 · 16 new suites are RED-first (the failure mode of each was watched before the fix, e.g. `expected '⚠️' not to be '⚠️'`, `expected null not to be null`, `expected 0 to be greater than 0`) · every contrast claim is a MEASURED ratio table across all ten accents × both themes, composited-pixel sampling not declared-token guessing.
  - **Ops.** On the NAS, in order: (1) `npm run pb:seed` (raises `consuela_data_snapshots.data` maxSize 1 MiB→8 MiB, adds `week_archive.lastActive`); (2) ordinary redeploy so the parent compose's new `TZ=America/Detroit` takes effect; (3) delete the leftover `PROBE-POINTS-1789853187732` task through the app's own delete flow. No new env beyond the optional `TZ` override; no secret change.
  - **Human-decision log (the rest of the audit stands open in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-audit-wave-open-findings.md`):** §1.2 dark mode reading indigo — **approved, keep it**; §3.2 cheap `===` compares — **approved and shipped** (all three sites); the money-mountain match bucket (§2.1/§2.2) stays **human-deferred** by the "points only" ruling (its `weekKey` is independently the local-vs-UTC bug). New side findings from the live-log probe (not this change): a stale-PB-password harness on the Mac's LAN hammering ~2,300 failed superuser logins since 08:00, and the `deepseek/free` provider upstream 404ing today.
- 2026-10-05 — feat(photos): the wall becomes family-tunable + the upload cap finally moves to 100 MB — **Files:** `src/lib/photos/settings.ts`, `src/lib/photos/upload-limits.ts`, `src/db/features/photos.ts`, `src/db/features/index.ts`, `src/db/features/migrate.ts`, `src/app/api/photos/settings/route.ts`, `src/app/api/photos/{route,upload/route,file/route}.ts`, `src/hooks/usePhotoSettings.ts`, `src/components/photos/{PhotosWidget,PhotoUploader}.tsx`, `src/components/settings/{PhotoSettingsSection,HomeSettingsSection}.tsx`, `.wall-photo-*` in `src/app/globals.css`, `src/app/page.tsx`, `src/lib/photos/curate.ts`; tests `photo-settings`, `photo-settings-route`, `photos-widget-settings`, `photos-commit-delay`, `photos-upload`, `photos-feed-route`, `migrate-reconcile`, `photos-reliability`, `photo-settings-section` (new) + `photos-curate`, `settings-home-section` extended.
  - **Why.** Two asks in one: the wall's delay/transition/order/caption were hardcoded (`ROTATE_MS`, one crossfade) with no way to tune them, and uploads were capped at 20 MB. An audit (three read-only agents: backend, frontend, spec-coherence) also surfaced 19 defects in the surrounding photo system, all folded into the approved spec's §10 ledger and fixed here.
  - **Settings, server-backed.** A `photo_settings` singleton (`key = "wall"`, shared family-wide — the wall is its own device, so a phone-side setting had to reach it). `GET /api/photos/settings` is any-session and **never 5xx**s: no row, a hand-edited row, or a read outage returns normalized defaults with `200`, plus `degraded: true` when it fell back — so a config outage can't blank the wall. `PATCH` is parent-only via `verifyLiveParentSession` (live PB re-read; `401`/`403`/`503` fail closed), takes a **subset** body, rejects bad enums (`400`), clamps the delay to 10–600 s, and upserts the singleton (create-race re-reads and merges rather than clobbering).
  - **Widget.** The four controls: seconds-per-photo (slider + Fast 30 s / Normal 75 s / Slow 5 min presets), transition, order, caption. `commitDelayMs` is the single timing authority (crossfade/slide 720 ms, dissolve 300 ms swap under an opaque veil, cut 0); `usePrefersReducedMotion` forces `cut` for every choice and the UI says so rather than faking it; `showCaption` now gates the caption **and** the scrim together. `order` threads into the feed (`?order=`, `400 invalid_order` otherwise) and `newest`/`oldest` sort chronologically over the **whole eligible library**, not the newest-60 window.
  - **100 MB uploads — the part that was actually blocking the family.** One shared `MAX_ORIGINAL_BYTES = 100 MB` replaces four drifting copies (route, uploader, error string, schema); `MAX_WALL_BYTES` stays 8 MB (the wall copy is a ~300 KB client resize). Critical: editing the schema alone changes **nothing** on the NAS — `createCollection` skips existing collections — so `runFeatureMigration` gained `reconcileFileFieldLimits`, which patches a live `file` field's `maxSize` (reading flat ≥0.23 or nested <0.23 and writing back in the same shape). While there, `createCollection` now returns `created|skipped|failed` — its old boolean made every failed create count as "skipped" and the CLI always exit 0, so a half-created schema looked successful.
  - **Audit fixes shipped alongside.** Feed no longer silently caps the library at the newest 60 (`getList(1,60)` → bounded pagination); the `/photos` management grid no longer runs wall *curation* on `?all=1` (it was shuffled, truncated at 40, and could hide a photo of the wall forever) — it returns every record newest-first; moderation `PATCH` no longer trusts a 7-day cookie (any child/pet/guest could flip `showOnWall`) — it re-reads live PB identity and requires a parent; upload validates `takenAt` (`400`, not `502`) and the wall MIME type, and rejects an oversized body on `Content-Length` before buffering it; all three routes log a message (never a payload) on failure. Widget reliability: the cursor is reset after a queue walk (it previously refetched the feed on **every** rotation forever, ~1,150/day), the feed fetch is abortable (`AbortSignal.timeout(15_000)`), error/empty states now recover (visibility change + backoff + a "Try again" button) instead of stranding an always-on panel until reload, a decode failure skips forward within the tick instead of burning 75 s, the wall `.hit-44::before` override is re-centred, and the uploader's retry no longer wipes completed rows or re-applies today's caption. Also fixed a pre-existing date time-bomb in `kid-home-done-today.test.tsx` (seeded the week from the real clock before fake timers, which flipped red on a week boundary).
  - **Tests.** `npx vitest run` on the 13 photo suites: **206/206**. TDD throughout (RED-first in each task). Full suite **6,180/6,219** — the 39 failures are all in `tests/unit/task-*` from a **separate concurrent session's in-flight ledger work** (disjoint file ownership; untouched here), as are the 10 typecheck errors in `tests/unit/task-route-auth-and-honesty.test.ts`. Zero failures or type errors in photo scope; `tsc` clean on every file this change touches.
  - **Ops:** run `npm run migrate:features` once on the NAS — it creates `photo_settings` **and** reconciles `photos.original.maxSize` to 100 MB. Then verify live `original.maxSize === 104857600` (or one real ~100 MB upload) and that `GET /api/photos/settings` returns defaults with a session cookie. `npm run pb:seed` is **not** required (neither collection is in the seed). Ordinary redeploy; no new env or secret.
  - **Contract:** UI Change Record (DESIGN.md 2026-10-05) supersedes the 2026-09-30 photos record's *motion line* while inheriting its discrete-motion contract; route + singleton gates in ARCHITECTURE.md §2.3.
  - **Known, recorded honestly:** the reconcile and live-PB field format were unit-tested against fakes only (never run against the NAS — human-gated). A wall record whose original the browser can't decode (HEIC outside Safari) is original-only, so at 100 MB that path can now stream a large file to the panel when no wall copy exists — accepted for a LAN panel, noted in ARCHITECTURE.md as a follow-up candidate.
  - **Visual pass (2026-10-05).** Ran the repo's `scripts/visual-review` harness against an **isolated production build of this commit** (in-repo worktree, so the concurrent session's dev server was untouched), with `/api/photos` + `/api/photos/settings` stubbed and sanitized. Verified by eye: the Photos card in dark/light/phone, the **degraded** state (controls disabled + honest line), the disabled/reduced-motion note, and the wall tile in ready / "Photos unavailable" + Try again / "No photos yet" + Add a photo. A mechanical scan (type floor, tap targets, clipped content, accessible names, overflow) surfaced **no new error-severity findings** — the only errors are pre-existing ones elsewhere on the page (the `Layout & display` card's `sr-only` widget toggles and intentional `.truncate` ellipses). Transitions render opaquely and correctly; their exact mid-frame timing is racy to screenshot, so the state machine stays pinned by `photos-widget-settings` + `photos-commit-delay` unit tests.
- 2026-10-04 — fix(points): scope the balance gate to the acting member; stop a latent whole-family freeze — **Files:** `src/lib/task-ledger.ts`, `src/lib/ledger-operations.ts`, `tests/unit/points-balance-invariant.test.ts` (new, 12 tests), `tests/unit/task-claim.test.ts`. Sign-off required and given for the points domain. Ops: ordinary redeploy, no PB change, no `pb:seed`, no new env.
  - **Root cause.** The balance invariant was implemented **twice with opposite negative-value policies**, and the gate was evaluated at **week scope instead of member scope**. `recomputeWeekPoints` (`task-ledger.ts`) floored each running step at 0; `hasNegativeOutcome` (`ledger-operations.ts:304`) refused to floor and walked the **entire week's history** for **any** member. Together: one member's legacy negative silently read as **"0 points"** on screen — the display erased the very deficit causing the freeze — while refusing **every** member's next operation with `insufficient_balance`, with no self-heal.
  - **Fix.** `computeMemberBalances()` is now the single summed invariant and `memberWouldGoNegative()` answers for **one** member; the week-scoped duplicate is deleted. The gate no longer refuses on `!Number.isSafeInteger(next)` — it conflated **precision with insolvency**, so a merely fractional running balance returned `insufficient_balance` with nothing negative (amounts are integer-guarded on every write path and the canonical parser rejects a non-finite amount, so a decimal was never grounds to refuse). The pre-check on the *current* history is **removed**: it was what made a stuck week unrepairable, since it refused the very operation that would have repaid a deficit. The only meaningful rule is the post-merge one — after this operation, no member it actually touches may be short.
  - **Kept deliberately, after I initially broke it.** My first attempt made the display sum-then-clamp once, to remove an order-dependence I believed was a defect. `task-ledger-contract.test.ts` failed with `expected 10 to be 5`, and the existing test was **right**: its title — *"replays every ledger type without allowing a negative balance"* — encodes a product semantic that **a child can never owe points**. An adjustment larger than the balance floors at 0 and later credits build on 0, not on the true negative. Reverted. The two forms only diverge on a history the write gate forbids anyway (it rejects any operation whose summed balance would go short, so a stored history never dips negative), and a new test pins that they **agree on every history the gate permitted**.
  - **A test was pinning the bug.** `task-claim.test.ts` *"maps a stable insufficient-balance ledger result to conflict"* used a fixture of one legacy `-1` for **Sam** to assert that **Alex's** claim returned 409 — i.e. it asserted the family-wide freeze. A claim only ever **earns**, so it can never legitimately go negative; that mapping is already pinned where it *is* reachable, on the spending path, by `rewards-redeem-route` (**400** + *"needs 11 more pts"*). Rewritten to assert the opposite, with the reasoning inline.
  - **Severity, measured not assumed.** Checked live read-only: **16 `week_data` + 15 `week_archive` rows, zero negatives and zero non-integers** — so this was **latent, not an active outage**. The value of the fix is removing the trap and enabling the self-heal path. (This corrects the earlier audit's suggestion that the fractional path was a live risk: `signedAmount` in `task-ledger-command.ts:80` already requires `Number.isSafeInteger`, so decimals cannot enter.)
  - **Adjacent finding, reported NOT fixed** (out of the approved scope): two legacy weeks — `week_data`/`week_archive` `2026-09-14` (9 txns) and `2026-06-15` (27 txns) — have histories that fail `parseCanonicalTransactions` on their `meta` block, left over from before the ledger contract was tightened. Consequence: `recomputeWeekPoints` throws, the outer catch maps it to `ledger_write_conflict`, and a write to those two weeks would fail with a misleading code. It **fails safe** (no write, no corruption), the read path `normalizeWeekData` renders both weeks correctly, and the **current week is canonical**, so live behaviour is unaffected. Worth a separate scoped fix.
  - **Verification.** `npm run typecheck` clean · full suite **5,846/5,846** across 526 files · ESLint clean on both changed sources · 56 ledger-adjacent suites 658/658 · post-deploy re-scan of live `week_data`/`week_archive` confirms 0 negatives / 0 non-integers. TDD: 12 new tests, RED first, including `expected 10 to be 5` pinning the display/gate divergence.
- 2026-10-04 — fix(security,meals,chat,money): a full audit + visual-critique remediation wave — 29 commits, `5e8519f..907e09d`, 198 files, **+21,852/−2,304**, tests **4,972 → 5,834**. One P0 credential leak, four P0 defects, ~35 P1s. **HOW THIS HAPPENED / READ THIS FIRST.** Nine parallel read-only audits over the 30 pages + 109 API routes produced 6 P0 and ~35 P1 findings; fixes then ran as six more agents on disjoint files; then four *harsh visual critics* each ran a shoot→look→judge→fix→re-shoot loop, and two more critics took the leftovers. **Two process findings belong in the manual, not just here:** (a) six agents sharing one working tree destroyed each other's work twice via `git stash`/`git reset` — **any multi-agent work in this repo must forbid git write commands and give each agent disjoint file ownership**; (b) **the machine audit was wrong and had to be demoted.** `scripts/visual-review/harness.mjs` reported 38 contrast violations on `/` that were entirely false — the warm-glass backdrop is painted by absolutely-positioned gradient/scrim layers that no ancestor chain resolves, so the checker walked to `body` and measured dark text against a near-black `body`. Contrast is now **advisory only, never error**, and the human critic is the judge of record; the mechanical checks that remain errors (type floor, tap targets, clipped content, overflow, alt, accessible name) have no such ambiguity. It also found a real AA failure the audit was *structurally* blind to: element `opacity` resolves separately from colour, so a `text-eyebrow opacity-60` on white glass measured as the un-faded token while actually compositing to ~2.2:1.
  - **THE P0 — a live Telegram bot token was committed and is on `origin/warm-glass-v2`.** `scripts/restart-consuela.sh:17`. The 2026-09-08 redaction commit `6ce58c0` touched that exact file and replaced only `HERMES_API_KEY`, so the token survived it, and `Dockerfile:5 COPY . .` re-exposed it on every NAS build. Redacted forward-only (no history rewrite — force-push is forbidden). Root cause fixed too: secrets were passed as `docker run -e KEY=value`, which docker persists in container config and which put the value in a tracked file at all; they now come from a gitignored `--env-file`. 🚨 **ACTION REQUIRED BY THE HUMAN: the committed value must be treated as burned and rotated at BotFather.** History rewrite is a human decision only. Related: `push-safe.sh` reported **CLEAN** on this leak, because Tier 1 compares against *current* env values and the credential had been rotated — so it gained Tier 2, shape- and name-based detection (`--self-test`: 17 checks, 0 failed; always prints `shape/<id>` or `name/<KEY>`, never a value).
  - **P0 emergency.** `emergency/route.ts:225` compared `String(providedPin)` to `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS`, whose default is `""` and which was absent from both the compose file and `.env.example`. `{"type":"fire","pin":[]}` made `!providedPin` truthy (an empty array is truthy), `String([]) === ""`, which equalled the empty default — so the gate skipped the credential entirely and fired a real SMS + email blast to every primary contact plus a Home Assistant broadcast, repeatable every 30s. Bypass now resolves to `string | null` where unset/empty/whitespace means **OFF**, candidates are normalized deliberately (digits-only, ≤12 chars, ≤4, deduped), comparison is timing-safe over hashed digests, and a PB lookup failure is 503 rather than a silent pass. `EmergencyButton` no longer renders raw `emergency_cooldown`/`emergency_in_flight` at a parent in a real emergency, and honours `retryAfterMs`.
  - **P0 data corruption.** `mealSync.ts:240` fell through to **7 hardcoded demo meals** on an empty or failed read and wrote their ~33 ingredients into the family's **real** grocery list — reachable from the only Sync to Grocery button on a fresh install. Demo seed is now explicit opt-in; an empty/blocked read is an honest failure with zero writes; the UI's ❌ branch is reachable again (it previously returned zeros and showed a fake "✅ Synced +0 items"); the sweep no longer marks out-of-stock staples `needed:false`; and `quantity`+`unit` now reach the deficit math at all (they were silently dropped, so the documented unit math never ran) with **incompatible units biasing toward listing the item**.
  - **P0/P1 SSRF in recipe ingest.** `isPrivateIP()` matched dotted quads on the submitted string only and then let `fetch` follow redirects, so any hostname (`pocketbase`, `hermes-agent-2`) passed, a public 302 into `127.0.0.1`/`169.254.169.254` was never checked, and the fetched bytes were echoed back through `recipe.instructions`. New `src/lib/safe-fetch.ts` validates scheme, credentials, the literal address, hostname shape and **every DNS answer**, then follows redirects manually so every hop is revalidated. Independently confirmed by three audit agents. The route is now parent-only, which also stops a child spending the paid LLM key.
  - **P0 chat-thread pollution.** `persistChatPair` triggered on `!isClem` — inferred from a field being **absent** — so recipe-parse callers posting a bare `{message}` landed the parse prompt and raw JSON in the shared `chat_messages` on every device. Persistence is now positive intent (`stream:true` or `agent:"clem"`). Related and larger: `/api/hermes/` is the only prefix under `/api/hermes/`, so **the thread's only writer sat entirely outside the session gate** — an unauthenticated caller reached the 20-tool kid surface *and* wrote rows as `"guest"`. An unverified caller now keeps the read role with **zero tools** and never reaches the thread; PB outage → 503.
  - **P1 the dock was painting over 9 dialogs, making their primary action untappable.** Each sheet rendered inline and so inherited `PageShell`'s relative `z-10` `<main>` stacking context, where its own `z-50`/`z-80` could never beat the *portaled* `z-50` dock. Verified on a screenshot: all 7 caps drawn across Cancel/Create Capsule. All portaled to `<body>`.
  - **P1 a cookie role was trusted as `parent` for 7 days** with no revocation, across the Ledger proxy, `/memory`, the chat tool surface, the planner and hall-of-fame. All now re-read live PocketBase identity and fail closed; `middleware` runtime pinned to `nodejs` because the live read needs `node:crypto` + the PB SDK. **Contract change:** a cookie role is never trusted (AGENTS.md §Settings contracts).
  - **P1 a stored AI provider key could be sent to any host.** `ai/models` resolved the key by `providerId` but took the *destination* from the request body, defeating the route's own "key never leaves this function" invariant. New `src/lib/ai/provider-egress.ts` binds a key to its own endpoint, refuses a rebind with a blank key (409), and redacts upstream error bodies out of responses, logs and the parent health panel.
  - **P1 money.** `PATCH /api/money-mountain/[id]` forwarded the raw body into PocketBase with no allowlist and no role gate, so a **child could set `currentAmount` and withdraw it**; a caller-supplied `type:"match"` skipped both the insufficient-funds check and `totalWithdrawals`. All three money writes are parent-only and rebuilt key-by-key from an allowlist, so every server-owned field is unreachable. Separately, `AllowanceWidget`'s kid-mode "Cash Out" made **no API call at all**, deducted nothing, wrote a fake withdrawal to localStorage and told a child "Money goes to your Greenlight card! 🎉" with a hardcoded `****2847`. There is no cash-out endpoint in the app, so wiring it would have deducted real points against a transfer that does not exist — a worse lie. Removed, with the control disabled and honestly explained. `/money-mountain` **stays visible to kids** (watching your own mountain climb is the point): it is now a designed read-only view where a control exists iff it works.
  - **Visual, and the reason this entry is long.** Light mode had measured *worse* after a contrast fix: the dock's active label was **1.65:1** and its icons **1.27:1**, because `CapsuleNav` hardcoded its colours in JS. The accent palette was dead code — `useTheme` wrote a theme-independent raw hex inline on `<html>`, outranking `:root[data-theme="light"]`, so **7 of 10 accents fell below 3:1 on the light canvas**. All six Avatar initials failed AA in light. The **High Contrast boost was inert** (`[data-contrast="boost"]` is (0,1,0) vs `:root[data-theme]` at (0,2,0), so dark+boost rendered byte-identical to dark). All fixed and measured: dock 15.67:1 / 6.32:1, worst accent-button 7.15:1 light / 4.58:1 dark, all 24 Avatar combinations clear.
  - **The lesson the critics found, and it is the general one:** several fixes were **measurably better and visually worse.** Light mode passed contrast while reading as a **muddy brown flood** — because `FogBackground` mounts an opaque full-viewport canvas whose palette came from `AtmosphericTheme`, which keyed off the **clock and never the theme**, and whose night palette was dead code in *both* themes (`hexToNum(x) ?? fallback` never falls through, since `hexToNum` returns a number or `NaN`). Dark mode **in daytime** was never examined and was a full-strength pale flood over a near-black canvas. The palette leg is now the resolved **theme**, and the clock only shifts the veil within that leg. 🚨 **JUDGEMENT CALL FOR HUMAN REVIEW: dark mode now reads indigo rather than amber**, because the amber was that `??` accident and the contrast gain is large; a family who deliberately picked a fog colour keeps it.
  - **A recurring trap worth naming as a contract:** `className` order does **not** win. `Chip`'s selected branch shipped white text on a 20%-opacity fill because two `bg-*` utilities in one Tailwind layer are resolved by **stylesheet** order; `.calendar-empty-grow` sat before the `.calendar-empty` shorthand; `.glass-strong`'s unlayered `position: relative` silently killed `fixed` on `ClemAssistant`'s FAB and `flex-row` on `StatTile`. **Unlayered CSS out-ranks `@layer utilities`, always.** Check it before assuming a class "works".
  - **Also fixed:** 17 of 18 dialogs had no focus trap, 14 no Escape, 16 no focus restore (one shared `useDialogA11y`, with the trigger captured during render because on the activating render the panel is not committed and React's `autoFocus` has not run — an effect captures the input *inside* the panel, which is exactly how focus return was being lost); the in-app reduce-motion toggle was inert for 13 sites that called `matchMedia` directly; the photo carousel autoplayed with no pause control (WCAG 2.2.2 Level A); the dock named no section on any phone (visible label column measured **2.1px at 320**) and had **no edge at all** because `--border-frost-*` are border *shorthands*, so Chromium dropped the declaration; route errors removed the dock (stranding the user) because `error.tsx`/`loading.tsx` rendered no `PageShell`, which the contract test structurally could not see; `/calendar` rendered **two conflicting calendars** in one card, used `.charAt(0)` for weekday letters, had a 170×36 tab with no `hit-44`, and printed a Google event's authored UTC offset in chat while the calendar printed local time (**the same event, two different times**); kids/guests/pets never saw school events because the only Google load was parent-gated; the mic and camera threw unconditionally and rendered raw internal TODO strings to users; a private time capsule could be marked viewed by anyone; capsules never unlocked (no caller, no crontab entry); `SoftButton`'s `disabled:opacity-50` measured 2.16:1 in light; `/grocery`'s bulk bar sat under the FAB making "Re-check all" untappable; a 36×36 control with no accessible name.
  - **Wall mode, a primary target, was "correct but half empty"** — the most recognisable AI-dashboard tell. The prior read-column cap applied one measure to every route, holding lists to a prose measure. `PageShell` now takes a `measure` prop (`board` default / `read` prose), and a rotated 1920×1080 panel no longer misses `data-wall` (it reports landscape). Home went from 2162px tall with the dock over its third row to a 4×3 board with 0 overflow.
  - **Tooling.** `npm run lint` reported **751 problems of which ~717 were phantom** — it was linting `.worktrees/**/.next/**` compiled vendor bundles from a stale sibling worktree, burying the real 34 under noise and making the gate unusable. Now 56. Added `scripts/visual-review/` (hermetic: auth/theme seeded to localStorage, all `/api/**` stubbed with sanitized fixtures, boots `next start` on an OS-assigned port or adopts one dev server via `VISUAL_REVIEW_BASE_URL`, `--tod day,night`), with contract tests pinning the light-fog cap, material, tint sync and the atmosphere leg.
  - **Verification.** `npm run typecheck` clean · **5,834/5,834** tests across 525 files · `npm run build` clean · `npm run lint` 56 (35 errors / 21 warnings, from a 34/21 real baseline — the +1 is the repo's existing test-Harness pattern in one new test file) · `push-safe.sh --self-test` 17/17 · visual harness **0 error-severity findings** on every route at phone + wall in dark + light. **Ops:** normal frontend redeploy; no PB schema change → no `npm run pb:seed`; new env `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS` documented as *empty = OFF*; no new secret requirement.
  - **Known conditions, recorded honestly:** three points/ledger-integrity bugs were reported and deliberately NOT fixed pending human sign-off (the money-mountain match contract is self-contradictory so a parent match is never paid; `weekStart.setDate(...-getDay()) + toISOString()` is the exact local-vs-UTC week-key bug fixed elsewhere in `rewards/redeem`, which would mis-bucket the weekly match cap; `recomputeWeekPoints` clamps negatives to 0 while refusing every member's operation, freezing the whole-family ledger for the week with a confident "0 points" on screen). **SUPERSEDED — see the `fix(points)` entry above:** the third of those is now **fixed** (`0b0e24f`, member-scoped gate + self-heal). The first two remain open by the user's explicit choice ("points only for now"), and all three are tracked as work items in `docs/PLAN.md` with their evidence in the outer repo's `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-audit-wave-open-findings.md`. The ~35 P1/P2 findings this wave surfaced but did **not** fix are recorded in that same plan file — the detailed reports were written to `/tmp`, which is not durable, so that file is now their surviving record. DNS-rebinding TOCTOU in `safe-fetch` is narrowed, not closed (it needs a pinned dispatcher, and `undici` is not a declared dep). `npm run build` still warns that the `middleware` convention is deprecated in favour of `proxy` — the migration was assessed as behaviour-neutral but deliberately not performed inside this wave. The seasonal-gradient tokens are dead code. Two existing test suites needed **mock-only** updates for the new live-identity gates (no assertion changed or removed).
- 2026-10-03 — fix(a11y): every dialog traps focus, closes on Escape and returns focus — **Files:** NEW `src/components/ui/useDialogA11y.ts`; MODIFIED `src/components/ui/{Modal,MemberModal,WeatherWidget}.tsx`, `src/components/auth/PinModal.tsx`, `src/components/wall/WallPinPad.tsx`, `src/components/ha/AlarmPinModal.tsx`, `src/components/meals/{CookMode,RecipeModal,RecipeSearchModal,RecipeImportModal,StorePicker}.tsx`, `src/components/leaderboard/LevelUpModal.tsx`, `src/components/skill-tree/QuestDetail.tsx`, `src/components/money-mountain/{TransactionLogger,CreateMountainForm}.tsx`, `src/components/time-capsule/{CreateCapsuleForm,ContentUploader}.tsx`, `src/components/profile/PhotoCropEditor.tsx`; NEW `tests/unit/{dialog-a11y-hook,dialog-a11y-pin-dialogs,dialog-a11y-fullscreen-editors,dialog-a11y-contract}.test.{tsx,ts}`; this changelog.
  - **The defect.** 17 of the app's 18 `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` overlays were modal in name only. Only the shared `Modal` and the weather details sheet had a focus trap; **14 had no Escape handler at all** (including `PinModal`, `WallPinPad`, `AlarmPinModal` and the full-screen cook mode) and **16 never returned focus to the trigger**, so closing a dialog dropped focus at the top of the document and a keyboard or screen-reader user lost their place entirely. This is WCAG 2.1.1 (Keyboard), 2.1.2 (No Keyboard Trap — here inverted: Tab must not escape) and 4.1.2 (Name, Role, Value). The audit-5.4 note that these sheets "carry role=dialog and aria-modal" was true of the attributes and silent about the behaviour.
  - **Fix — once, centrally, not 17 times.** `useDialogA11y` is now the single implementation and every modal dialog routes through it, so a new dialog inherits the behaviour by wiring one hook instead of re-deriving it. The shared `Modal` was the reference implementation; its three effects were lifted into the hook verbatim and `Modal.tsx` now keeps only its enter/exit phases. Preserved deliberately: the `:disabled` filter that excludes controls disabled by an ancestor `<fieldset>`, and honouring an `autoFocus` child over the first focusable (the Tasks claim modal renders a `<select>` before its autoFocus PIN input).
