- 2026-09-28 — feat(shell): the rail follows you, and chat joins the shell (UI audit Phase 4.1–4.2) — **Files:** `src/modes/adult/SidebarNav.tsx` → `src/components/ui/SidebarNav.tsx` (`git mv`), `src/components/ui/PageShell.tsx` (owns the rail + new props), `src/modes/adult/AdultHome.tsx` (local mounts removed), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (hand-rolled shell → `PageShell`), `src/components/ui/SyncStatusBanner.tsx` (doc), NEW `tests/unit/page-shell-tiers.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/sidebar-nav.test.tsx` (import path), `tests/unit/chat-{page-stream,conversation-reset,speaker-roster-refresh,points-chip}.test.tsx` (`usePathname` added to the `next/navigation` mock), `docs/UI_AUDIT_2026-09.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and this changelog.
  - **What the audit found (finding 7, P1):** the desktop rail was mounted inside `AdultHome` alone — a parent who left Home on a tablet/desktop lost the rail while the phone dock stayed — and `/chat` bypassed `PageShell` entirely (no `<main>`, so no sync banner, no `page-settle`, and it hand-rolled its own dock and banner instead of sharing the shell).
  - **4.1 — rail on every route:** `SidebarNav` moved to `src/components/ui/` and is now mounted by `PageShell` for parent sessions (`navRoleForUser(currentUser) === "parent"`), `md+` only, with `md:pl-60` on the content wrapper reserving exactly the rail's `w-60` so the fixed rail can never sit on top of content (`data-page-rail` marks the wrapper). Children, pets and the signed-out family screen keep the dock alone — nav *roles* (Phase 3's manifest) decide that, not the shell. `AdultHome`'s two local `<SidebarNav />` mounts are gone.
  - **4.2 — chat through the shell:** `PageShell` gained four documented props — `bannerMessage`/`bannerClassName` (surface-specific sync copy: chat's signed-out thread warning now flows through the shell instead of a second banner), `contentClassName` (chat keeps `max-w-lg mx-auto` at *every* breakpoint — a thread must not stretch full-width on desktop — plus its own `flex flex-col min-h-screen` column), `bottomInset={false}` (chat's composer already pads `env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 5.5rem` for the fixed dock; the shell's `pb-32` would add dead scroll space under it), and `clip={false}`. **Why `clip`:** the shell root is `overflow-hidden`, which becomes the *scrollport* for its descendants — a sticky header inside pins to that box instead of the viewport and scrolls away with the page. Chat's pinned top bar and composer need the document as their scrollport, so that page opts out; rail and dock are `fixed` either way, so clipping changes nothing for them.
  - **Contract tests:** NEW `tests/unit/page-shell-tiers.test.tsx` (9) pins the tier contract — rail present at `md+` for parents only, `md:pl-60` ≡ rail `w-60`, `data-page-rail`, the pre-Phase-4 column tiers (`max-w-lg md:max-w-3xl lg:max-w-none`) with `page-settle` on `<main>`, banner copy passthrough (custom copy + generic fallback), and the `bottomInset`/`clip` opt-outs vs. their defaults. The four chat suites now mock `usePathname` because the page renders through the shell.
  - **Verified:** full Vitest **3,333/3,333 (393 files, exit 0)**, `tsc --noEmit` clean, targeted ESLint clean (0 errors), `next build` exit 0.

- 2026-09-28 — feat(nav): one route manifest, one icon set, one active rule — and no orphaned route (UI audit Phase 3) — **Files:** NEW `src/lib/nav-items.ts`, NEW `src/components/ui/NavIcon.tsx`, NEW `src/components/patterns/MoreSheet.tsx`, `src/components/patterns/MoreMenuItem.tsx` (plain `<a>` → `next/link`), `src/components/ui/CapsuleNav.tsx` (rewritten to read the manifest), `src/modes/adult/SidebarNav.tsx` (rewritten), `src/app/page.tsx` / `src/modes/family/FamilyHome.tsx` / `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx` (More… trigger + sheet mount), `src/app/globals.css` (`--color-nav-active*`), NEW `tests/unit/nav-items.test.ts`, NEW `tests/unit/nav-icon.test.tsx`, NEW `tests/unit/sidebar-nav.test.tsx`, NEW `tests/unit/more-sheet.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/capsule-nav.test.tsx`, `docs/UI_AUDIT_2026-09.md`, `docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and this changelog.
  - **What the audit found (finding 6, P1):** three navs, three icon systems and one dead reference — `CapsuleNav` kept a private 7-item list with inline SVGs and `pathname === href`; `SidebarNav` kept a private 6-item list with **emoji** icons ("🏠 Dashboard", "🍽️ Meals") and `startsWith` matching; `lucide-react` covered 25 more files. Consequences fixed here: the two navs disagreed on the active item (`/settings/me` lit Settings in the rail and nothing in the dock), the rail showed a signed-in child House instead of Rewards, the rail's header comment credited a `BottomNav` that no longer exists, and the dock's five hard-coded `rgba(120,240,90,…)` literals ignored the accent system (`--capsule-accent: #78f05a` was written once and never read from the accent tokens; the rail's `rgba(var(--color-accent-selected-rgb, 59,130,246), …)` referenced a variable **defined nowhere**, so its hard-coded fallback blue always won).
  - **The manifest (`lib/nav-items.ts`):** `path, label, iconKey, roles, group, wall` + `NAV_ICON_KEYS`; `navRoleForUser` (guest/parent/child/pet — unknown *signed-in* roles resolve to `child`, mirroring `resolveMode()`), `navModeForRole`, `navItemsForRole`, `moreNavItemsForRole`, `isPathActive`/`isNavItemActive`/`navItemForPath`, and `EXEMPT_ROUTES` (route → written reason). Kid mode is expressed as data — House is `["guest","parent"]`, Rewards is `["child","pet"]` — so both modes still render **exactly 7** caps, which the `--capsule-scale` math depends on, and the dock's `House → Rewards` splice is gone.
  - **No orphaned route (audit P1-5):** `/analytics`, `/memory`, `/money-mountain`, `/skill-tree`, `/time-capsule` had **0** inbound links and `/grocery` only 1 (a design-system demo page). All six now live in the Home `More…` sheet, which every mode mounts (adult, wall/family, kid). Role filtering keeps the parent-only memory bank (middleware also allowlists it) and the finance pages off any signed-out screen — the wall sheet therefore offers exactly `/grocery`, `/skill-tree`, `/time-capsule`, `/analytics`.
  - **Repeatable contract:** `tests/unit/nav-items.test.ts` walks `src/app/**/page.tsx`, collapses dynamic segments and **fails on any shipped route that is neither in the manifest nor in `EXEMPT_ROUTES`** — and also fails on a stale exemption or an exemption with no reason. A new route can no longer ship unreachable.
  - **One icon set (`NavIcon`):** the dock's inline SVGs and the rail's emoji both become one keyed SVG set; `tests/unit/nav-icon.test.tsx` pins the key set to `NAV_ICON_KEYS` so the manifest can never ask for an icon that does not exist (findings 6 + 12; the remaining emoji-as-chrome surfaces are Phase 5). Every icon is `aria-hidden` — the manifest's `label` is the accessible name (the dock already passed it as `aria-label`; the rail now does too, plus `aria-current="page"`).
  - **One accent token:** `--color-nav-active` (+ `-border`, `-sheen`, `-wash`, `-glow`, `-halo`, `-fill`) in `globals.css`, read by both navs. `--fill` is the accent deepened 72% (the `--widget-accent` recipe) so the white active glyph holds ≥3:1 non-text contrast on light accents too. `--capsule-accent` survives only as an alias. **Intentional visual change:** the active dock pill now follows the accent (nori blue today) instead of lime — set `--color-nav-active: #78f05a` to restore the lime dock.

`adult`/`kid`/`wall` reviewed in code). **Contrast:** `--color-text-muted` `#4e5a72 → #8892aa` dark (2.72 → **6.06:1** canvas, 5.04:1 on `surface-2`) and `#8a8a8a → #6f6f6f` light (3.20 → **4.65:1**); chat Send/Stop 40 → 44px; Home `Sign in` pill and member sign-in chips ≥44px; `hit-44` added to `Chip`, `ThemeToggle`, Kitchen-Flow collapse, chat new-conversation / speaker pill and the Home `Quick ask` link; `a.widget-accent-text` footer links given a 44px line box. **Measured result:** sub-12px text nodes **262 → 0** and smallest rendered text 8–9px → 12px across 10 routes × 2 viewports; controls under 44×44 with **no** enlarged hit region **77 → 0** (18 compact visual pills remain by design, each verified to paint a 44px hit region); overflow 0, unlabelled inputs 0, `img` without `alt` 0, `<h1>` present on all routes; only the pre-existing signed-out 401 console noise (Phase 2). **Verification:** `npm run typecheck` passed; targeted ESLint on all changed files 0 errors (1 pre-existing `<img>` warning in `meals/recipes/[id]/page.tsx`); - 2026-09-28 — fix(tasks): close the merge review — the shipped record, three money-path defects, and one dead-Wave-2 file. **(1) The shipped record was false.** The `2026-09-25 … Wave 2` entry above rode through the cherry-pick *without its code*: it claimed `POST /api/admin/update` was closed (it is still the live `git pull` trigger), that `pb:seed` "now fails closed and applies the terminal schema/indices" (no contract, no migration), and that the deploy goes through a replacement-only `deploy.sh` (unchanged, 0 diff). Following that Ops block on the NAS would have run a seed with no fail-closed contract and expected a migration that does not exist. The entry is rewritten to describe only what shipped and says plainly that Wave 2's deploy half was withheld by decision; its Ops line is now "ordinary image redeploy — no `pb:seed`, no migration, `deploy.sh` unchanged". **(2) The claim branch now enforces the Option B invariant.** `task-claim.ts` mirrored `complete`'s `queueOnly` rule on `complete`/`undo` but not on `claim`, so an internal adult `claim` paid immediately; it was unreachable only because `runClaimTaskCommand` happens to be typed `"complete" | "undo"`. The seam now carries the rule, keyed on `authentication` exactly as `complete` does, so chat cannot move points on either paying branch. **(3) The week key no longer diverges east of UTC.** `POST /api/rewards/redeem` derived its `weekStart` with `setHours(0,0,0,0)` + `.toISOString()` — local midnight serialized as UTC, so east of UTC it resolved to the previous day and on a Sunday to the previous week; a redemption then deducted from a `week_data` row the rollover had already closed while the visible balance sprang back. It, and the duplicate `todayMondayISO()` / `weekKey()` pair in `task-utils.ts` (whose `.toISOString()` had the same shape, and which the client compared `completedInWeek` against on both the Tasks page and KidHome), now read the canonical `localWeekStartISO()`. The 23 importers were repointed and the duplicates deleted, so the client and both money paths derive one key. **(4) Planner adjustments can no longer land on the wrong sibling.** `adjust_points` resolved `member` with a first-match `startsWith`, so `"J"` adjusted whichever sibling sorted first — a wrong-person write to a real ledger, not a refusal. It is now exact-normalized-match-first, then a *unique* prefix, and an ambiguous prefix is a 400 that names the candidates. **(5) Dead Wave 2 surface removed:** `SESSION_TTL_SECONDS_BY_ROLE` + `sessionTtlSeconds()` are gone from `session-policy.ts` (no `src/` caller, but a test pinned them, so they read as shipped); `SESSION_ROLES` / `SessionRole` / `isSessionRole` / `SessionIdentity` stay, since the db-gateway routes and the live gate use them. **Tests (TDD, red first):** the claim-branch test failed with `expected null to match object { byName: 'Alex', points: 6 }` (it paid); the ambiguity test failed with `expected 200 to be 400`; the Tokyo test failed with `expected '2026-09-27' to be '2026-09-28'` (the UTC-shifted Sunday). GREEN: `task-claim-adult-queue` 9/9, `planner-apply-route` 45/45, `rewards-redeem-route` 33/33. The Tokyo case re-pins `TZ=Asia/Tokyo` and the system clock at runtime (Mon 00:30 JST) precisely because the suite's vitest `env.TZ` is `America/Detroit`, where the bug is invisible; `live-session` now pins that no per-role TTL vocabulary reappears. `kid-home-quest-pin-safety` mocks the canonical helper instead of the deleted `weekKey` so its stale-cache guard still tests the family's own week row rather than the wall clock. **Ops:** same as the Wave 2 correction — ordinary image redeploy, no `pb:seed`, no migration, `deploy.sh` unchanged; the in-app deploy trigger is still open by design-of-withholding, not by omission. **Contracts:** "chat never moves points" is enforced at the seam on BOTH paying branches, not by one caller's type signature; the ledger week key is `localWeekStartISO()` and nothing else, on the server and in the client; a point adjustment is refused rather than guessed when the member name is ambiguous; no Wave 2 surface may be present in the tree unless its code shipped.

- 2026-09-28 — fix(tasks): adult-owned chores queue from chat instead of refusing (Option B) — `complete_task` no longer returns `reason: "adult_owner"`; the claim seam now branches on `authentication` (`"internal"` queues, `"pin"` pays, an adult `"session"` caller is refused `pin_required`) rather than `role`, which closes the roster-promotion race by construction. Tasks-screen parent completions are unchanged.

- 2026-09-26 — feat(tasks,ai): task-backend remediation, **Wave 3** — the task-adjacent writers, and one directive in `AGENTS.md` that was about to cost someone a day (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-24-task-adjacent-writers.md`; Wave 3 Tasks 1–10). **(1) One ledger operation, two routes.** `POST /api/rewards/redeem` and `POST /api/consuela/planner/apply` were the last two writers holding their own `withWeekLedgerLock` body. Both now execute through the shared `applyWeekLedgerOperation` (`src/lib/ledger-operations.ts`), so the lock order (`week-ledger → snapshot-keyed`), the operation-replay check, the reversal-aware semantic replay, the integer/known-type validation, the recompute-from-history, the re-read proof and the post-verify projection callback are defined in exactly one place. Redemption reads its cost and title from the **stored reward row** (never a client amount), records `tx.meta.operationId`, and a replayed redemption deducts once (`tests/unit/rewards-redeem-route.test.ts`). The planner keeps its live-parent-session + parent-PIN gate and its un-keyed chat adjustment is now **refused** rather than applied (`tests/unit/planner-apply-route.test.ts`; the unlock bonus reads from the canonical catalog too). **(2) The assistant's task writer is a command, and only a command.** `complete_task` / `reopen_task` ride the claim seam (`kind:"complete"` / `kind:"undo"`), `add_task` / `update_task` / `delete_task` the manage seam (`kind:"add"|"update"|"delete"`), all through `executeInternalTaskCommand` — no tool writes a snapshot, a tombstone or a mirror row any more. Completion is **assigned-only**: an open / late-stealable chore must be CLAIMED from the Tasks screen (a claim pays the claimer) and a crew chore needs every member checked in first. The payee and the amount are derived server-side from the chore's canonical owner and stored points; the `assignee` argument only disambiguates which row was meant. Pet assignees are refused. The reopen guard follows how the earn was actually written (a crew approval pays **per member** via `pendingApproval.crew`, so the literal `"Crew"` is not a payee), has no cheap pre-filter, and `reopenTask` writes `completedBy/completedAt/completedInWeek` as `null` — `""` is not nullish and would persist as a lie. **(3) Snapshot-first reads, and the honest third state.** NEW `readCanonicalTasks()` (`src/lib/consuela/live-reads.ts`) reads the tasks **snapshot** first — the rows the family actually sees — falls back to the PB replica only as a *declared* fallback, and returns `unavailable` when both fail. The morning briefing (`src/lib/consuela/briefing.ts`), the assistant live reads and the screensaver payload (`src/lib/screensaver/payload.ts`) all read through it; tombstones are dropped, unresolved (`pendingApproval`) rows are filtered where the surface's meaning needs a settled answer, and the screensaver throws `task_data_unavailable` for an honest 503 instead of drawing a chore progress bar it made up. The briefing no longer renders a stored unknown as "no chores" (`26fe3c3`). **(4) All-time totals are recomputed, never read.** NEW `GET /api/tasks/all-time` + `src/lib/all-time-totals.ts` + `useAllTimeTotals` derive every member's all-time **points and completion count** by parsing canonical transaction history (`parseCanonicalTransactions`) across the live week plus archived weeks. A stored `points` map is never authority, legacy helpers that summed stored maps are retired, and a week whose history cannot be identified sets `historyComplete: false` ⇒ per-member `null`. The podium, your card, member sheet, leaderboard hooks and the kid hero render that null / partial state honestly ("—" plus a short-total note) instead of a smaller number wearing the whole number's label, and levels are no longer derived from a missing value (`3b9f512`, `f37a8ea`, `02ad3ca`). **(5) The normal structured writers are gone — there was almost nothing left to remove.** Wave 1 Task 11 had already deleted the whole `sync*ToPB` family (`syncTasksToPB`, `syncWeekDataToPB`, `syncArchiveToPB`, `syncRewardsToPB`, `syncPenaltiesToPB`, `syncWeeklyPrizesToPB`, `syncAllTasksToPB`, `syncHallOfFameToPB`) and retired the Tasks-page snapshot POST effect, the 5s structured sync effect, `pbSyncPendingRef`, the direct `syncWeekDataToPB(nextWeek)` call, every KidHome `syncTasksToPB` call (localStorage remains a **cache only**) and the task/week/ledger block in `pushLocalToPB`. Verified rather than assumed: zero `sync*ToPB` matches remain anywhere in `src/` or `scripts/`, `pushLocalToPB` pushes exactly the eight non-task migration collections (`grocery_list_items`, `pantry_items`, `meal_plan_entries`, `recipes`, `events`, `schedules`, `family_goals`, `emergency_contacts`) — `syncFamilyGoalToPB` is the one surviving `sync*ToPB` helper and it is a family-goal write, not a task one. Because **no migration-tooling caller survived the Wave 1 deletion, nothing is retained "for migration"**: the helpers stay deleted rather than resurrected as dead exports. NEW `tests/unit/task-normal-writes-disabled.test.ts` (8) pins the writer surface statically — no `POST /api/tasks/sync` in either argument order, no `pbSyncPendingRef`, no sync-helper call in the Tasks page / KidHome / migration push, the exact eight-collection migration list, and the full helper family absent from production modules. The phantom `syncAllTasksToPB` mock in `push-local-honest-counts.test.ts` was a spy on a function that no longer exists; it is removed and replaced with an assertion that no task/week/ledger/reward/config leg can reappear in the results array. **GET `/api/tasks/sync` is kept** — it remains the cross-device read endpoint (rollover + reconcile + snapshot) and only its POST body is refused (410 `legacy_sync_write_disabled` / 400 `invalid_body`). **(6) The prompt surface now matches the code, and is regenerated — never hand-edited.** `ai/TOOLS.md` gains a "Who owns a task write" block: the `executeInternalTaskCommand` seam, one command per `operationId`, report-the-receipt-not-your-intent, canonical payee derivation, assigned-only completion with claim/crew routing, and the point-adjustment exception that proves the rule. `TASK_ACTIONS_ADDENDUM` (`src/lib/consuela-prompts.ts`) teaches the same three truths to the model. `docs/muse-api.md` gains two task-write invariants (every task write is a server command; completion is assigned-only and the payee is never an argument). `src/lib/ai-boot.generated.ts` was re-synced with `node scripts/write-ai-boot.mjs` (this is a re-sync — Wave 3 Task 6 had already regenerated it once; the sync guard is `tests/unit/ai-boot-tools-doc.test.ts`, which byte-matches the generated artifact against `ai/*.md` and would have failed the suite on a stale prompt). **(7) `AGENTS.md` §5.6 said the opposite of the truth, as a directive.** The old paragraph read *"Wave 3 scope — … the rest NOT yet remediated … reward redemption (`POST /api/rewards/redeem` runs its own `withWeekLedgerLock` body instead of the shared ledger operation helper), the planner point adjustment, briefing authority … and all-time totals. Treat only those as unremediated … and migrate them in a later wave."* Every one of those four was already remediated by Wave 3 Tasks 2, 3, 7 and 8. Worse, it was phrased as an instruction, so a future agent would have read correct code, believed it was a bug, and "fixed" the shared-seam routes back into private lock bodies. The paragraph is replaced with a "Wave 3 is COMPLETE — there is NO unremediated writer left in this plan's scope" statement that names what each surface rides now and explicitly retires the two wrong claims (neither route has its own lock body any more). The old `CONTRACTS to keep (Task 5 + Task 6)` list is folded into one ten-item Wave 3 list: the command seam, all-time recomputation from canonical history, the honest-null policy (an unknown is `null` — never `0`, never "no chores", never a level from a missing number), snapshot-first reads, the outbox `parentName` contract (identity, never a credential — re-resolved live with `namesMatch`, then `parentPin` verified; a PIN never enters the entry), prompt/codegen coupling, assistant actor attribution, assigned-only completion with canonical payee, the reopen guard, and GET-sync-is-the-read. A new **UI Change Record — 2026-09-26** and a new Current Dashboard Snapshot entry were added; the 2026-09-21 Hermes ops snapshot entry is deliberately kept past the two-entry cap because its profile/port knowledge exists in no other file. **OPEN HUMAN DECISION — deliberately NOT decided in code:** what should happen when a chore's own canonical owner is a grown-up. `complete_task` refuses today with `reason: "adult_owner"` and points the family at the Tasks screen; whether that should instead become completable-from-chat, routed-to-approval, or permanently refused is an undecided product question. The code is left exactly as it is, and `ai/TOOLS.md` / `docs/muse-api.md` / `AGENTS.md` all mark it provisional so nobody hardens it into a permanent rule. **Tests:** NEW `task-normal-writes-disabled` (8) plus the Wave 3 route/hook suites (`rewards-redeem-route`, `planner-apply-route`, `task-config-clients`, `tasks-pin-error-paths`, `rewards-shop-parent-approval`, `hermes-tools-task-crud`, `muse-task-tools`, `briefing-authority`, `all-time-totals`, `all-time-route`, `use-all-time-totals`); RED proof for the new static test captured against the pre-Wave-1-Task-11 sources (`382475b^`): 6 failed / 2 passed, exit 1. **Ops: none** — no PB collection, field or index changed, so no `pb:seed` requirement and no new terminal migration.
  - **Merge note (2026-09-28, `integration` `f13c8d8`) — the eight-collection push above is now six; the entry body is left as the Wave 3 record.** `pushLocalToPB` ships the audit's **six**-collection `SAFE_LOCAL_PUSH_COLLECTIONS` (`grocery_list_items`, `pantry_items`, `meal_plan_entries`, `recipes`, `events`, `schedules` — `src/lib/push-local-to-pb.ts`), and `tests/unit/task-normal-writes-disabled.test.ts` asserts those six, so read the "exactly the eight" wording above as history, not as the current allowlist. The `family_goals` and `emergency_contacts` legs were retired by audit commit `d9e9432` and both collections keep parent-authorized Settings writers (`SafetySettingsSection.tsx` → `db.insertEmergencyContact` / `updateEmergencyContact`), so nothing lost its only write path. The same merge moved this wave's contracts out of `AGENTS.md` §5.6 into `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.6 (and the UI record into `docs/DESIGN.md`); the migration-push contract is now stated in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.6. Merged in `integration` `f13c8d8` (parents `101cddc` + `e368948`); application behaviour unchanged by this note.

- 2026-09-25 — feat(auth): task-backend remediation, Wave 2 — the IDENTITY half only: PocketBase is the only production identity and every privileged session is revalidated live (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-24-task-identity-deployment-security.md`, which lives in the PARENT Dashboard repo). **Wave 2's DEPLOY half was deliberately WITHHELD from this ship and is NOT here:** the per-role session lifetimes + `POST /api/auth/touch` rotation, the fail-closed `PbCollectionContract` seed verification, the terminal PB migration (`1790250000_terminal_locked_task_schema.js`), the `docker-compose.yml` image pins / `pb_migrations` mount / `pb-seed` service, the Git-free `deploy.sh` replacement runner, `scripts/sync-nas-source.sh`, and the `POST /api/admin/update` → 410 `manual_deploy_required` closure. Each of those is separate plan work that did not make this subset; read the list as “not yet built”, never as “done”. Two consequences the operator must know: **`POST /api/admin/update` is STILL the live in-app deploy trigger** (`src/app/api/admin/update/route.ts` runs `git fetch origin warm-glass-v2; git checkout warm-glass-v2; git pull origin warm-glass-v2` on the NAS), so a parent-session admin can still pull the branch from the app — the closure this plan promised did NOT happen; and the session lifetime is still the single global `SESSION_TTL_SECONDS` of 7 days (`src/lib/session.ts`), with no per-role table. **PocketBase is the SOLE production identity source.** `canonicalMemberFallbacksEnabled()` (`src/lib/member-fallback.ts`) is a TWO-condition gate — `NODE_ENV !== "production"` AND `NEXT_PUBLIC_CANONICAL_MEMBER_FALLBACKS === "true"` — so production is forced off even if the environment accidentally says `true`, and the whole fallback surface now reads that one function: `mergeMemberFallbacks` (the shared server+browser roster merge), `withResolvedPins` in `server-auth.ts` (the seed-side `MEMBER_DEFAULT_PINS` default can no longer synthesize a credential for a PB row with no stored pin), `db/index.ts`, `db/pb-db.ts` and `calendar-member-snapshot.ts` (the Calendar member chips no longer fall back to a hardcoded roster). A PB read failure now resolves to no members at all and authentication fails closed. **Live-session revalidation contract.** NEW `requireLiveSession(request, { requireRole? })` (`src/lib/server-auth.ts`) verifies the HMAC cookie, re-reads the member row from PocketBase by the signed `memberId`, and returns ONLY `{ memberId, name, role }` from live state — 401 `unauthorized` for a missing/expired/unverifiable cookie or a deleted member, 403 `session_role_changed` on any signed-vs-live role drift (including an out-of-vocabulary live role), 403 `adult_only` when a role is required, 503 `identity_unavailable` when PB itself is unreachable. The signed role is a CLAIM, never authority: `authorizeAdminRequest` (`src/lib/admin-auth.ts`) now resolves its cookie branch through the live gate, so a demoted parent is 403 and a deleted one 401. Applied across every privileged route — `/api/auth/whoami`, `/api/chat/messages`, `/api/consuela/briefing` (GET + PATCH), `/api/consuela/suggestions`, `/api/consuela/planner/apply` (live parent + parent PIN), `/api/hermes/chat` (guest/kid surface + live-parent planner), `/api/hall-of-fame/celebrate`, `/api/members/admin` (GET) and `/api/members/profile` (the child own-record branch), `/api/services/runtime`, `/api/tasks/sync`, `/api/tasks/claim`, and both `/api/db/[collection]` write routes — alongside the pre-existing `verifyLiveParentSession` on `/api/tasks/manage`, `/api/tasks/config` and `/api/tasks/quarantine`. **Session vocabulary.** NEW `src/lib/session-policy.ts` owns only the ROLE vocabulary — `SESSION_ROLES` (parent|child|pet), `SessionRole`, `isSessionRole`, `SessionIdentity` — read by the db-gateway write policy and the live gate. The per-role TTL table that rode in alongside this file was removed in the 2026-09-28 correction below, because the role-aware-lifetime leg it described never shipped. **Command write tier.** `/api/db/[collection]` gained a third `WRITE_POLICY` value: `tasks`, `week_data` and `week_archive` are `policy === "command"` and refused `403 command_only` for EVERY role — parent included — before any body parse or PB call, and the check is typed so a collection with a missing role entry can no longer slip past the parent check. **Honest risk lines carried forward from the reviews (not defects, but the real edges):** (1) `signSession` has no runtime role guard — an out-of-vocabulary role yields a signed token that is unverifiable, which the live gate refuses; fail-closed and accepted. (2) The 7-day cookie lifetime is one global constant, so a stolen cookie is good for a week; role-aware lifetimes are the withheld leg and remain open work. (3) `createMemberRecord` still resolves a server-side default PIN in production — a SANCTIONED, documented exception to the PB-only rule (recorded in the plan's Global Constraints), because the creation body may never carry a pin and a member with no pin could never sign in; the parent sets the real PIN at creation or immediately after via `POST /api/members/pin`. (4) The in-app deploy trigger is OPEN — a parent-session admin can still `git pull` warm-glass-v2 on the NAS from the app. That is unchanged from before this ship and is not a regression; it is simply not the closure this plan promised. **Tests:** the Wave 2 suites that actually shipped are `live-session` (the shared live-session contract incl. 404, 503 and out-of-vocabulary role) and `db-gateway-write-policy` (the third `command` policy). The identity-route, production-fallback, seed-contract, migration-parity and deploy-runner suites the original entry named are withheld work and do not exist in this tree. **Ops:** this deploy is an ORDINARY image redeploy — do **NOT** run `npm run pb:seed` on the strength of this entry (the seed script is unchanged and has no fail-closed contract to satisfy), **NO** PB migration is applied (`pb_migrations/` holds nothing newer than `1788500000`), and `deploy.sh` / `docker-compose.yml` are byte-identical to before this merge, so the existing NAS deploy path is used exactly as it was. Contracts: `canonicalMemberFallbacksEnabled()` is the only identity-source gate (never re-derive the production check); `requireLiveSession` is THE live gate and returns only memberId/name/role; a signed role is a claim, never authority; `command` is a first-class gateway write policy refused for every role; the session lifetime is the single `SESSION_TTL_SECONDS`, with no per-role vocabulary until the withheld leg ships.


- 2026-09-25 — feat(tasks): task-authority remediation, Wave 1 — the browser is no longer a writer for task or ledger data (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-task-backend-integrity-remediation-design.md` §6; 44 commits, 118 files, +34312/−3946). **Command routes.** Every task/ledger/config mutation is now a server command: NEW `/api/tasks/manage` (parent-session `add`/`update`/`delete`; server-issued or collision-checked task ids, pet assignees refused, delete writes a server tombstone and is idempotent, completion/approval/ledger fields unreachable through CRUD), NEW `/api/tasks/config` (parent-only reward/penalty/weekly-prize config with its last-write-wins stamps — it never accepts task rows, week data or history), NEW `/api/tasks/ledger` (`penalty`/`adjust`; the points come from the canonical catalog leg, never a client amount), and the existing `/api/tasks/claim` + `/api/tasks/approve` reworked snapshot-primary (assigned completion requires the verified claimant to match the assignee, under-10 PIN-free completion requires a live under-10 child session resolving to the same canonical member, child undo is scoped to its own pending row, crew join/check-in honours `crew.removed` tombstones). `POST /api/tasks/sync` is now read-only: a `tasks`/`weekData` body is 410 `legacy_sync_write_disabled`, anything else 400 `invalid_body`; the dead `sync*ToPB` family (135 lines) is deleted. **Shared ledger operation helper.** NEW `src/lib/ledger-operations.ts` owns `withWeekLedgerLock` itself so lock order is `week-ledger → snapshot-keyed` and no caller can invert it: it reads the canonical `week_data` row, checks operation replay AND reversal-aware semantic replay for task earns, validates integer amounts/known transaction types, recomputes `points` from history, writes, re-reads to prove the operation survived, then runs a caller-supplied projection callback *after* the ledger is verified and *before* the week lock is released. Approve is ledger-first: every explicit `approve-all` id is validated before any write, the whole batch is applied under ONE week lock, pending state is cleared only after the canonical ledger commit, a projection failure returns `202` with authoritative `weekData` + `reconciled:false`, and a ledger failure leaves pending untouched so the outbox keeps the command. `paid` counts payees, `cleared` counts task rows. **Snapshot revision + operation receipts.** The tasks snapshot gained a monotonic decimal `revision` + `updatedAt` (`SnapshotRevision`), per-task `operationReceipts`, `configOperationReceipts`, `pendingProjectionRepairs` and `deletedTaskIds` tombstones, so a replay is provable from the snapshot alone; every command acknowledgment returns the canonical `weekData`/`task` plus that revision, and the client adopts it *before* the outbox entry is released. NEW `src/lib/task-projection-reconciler.ts` repairs stale/cleared pending approvals, completion fields, deleted tasks, crew check-ins and week data idempotently under the existing locks — it never trusts a browser-supplied amount or payee. **Durable browser outbox.** NEW `src/lib/task-operation-outbox.ts` + `src/hooks/useTaskOperationOutbox.ts` + `src/hooks/useTaskCommandQueue.ts`: a device queues each command under the localStorage key **`consuela-task-operation-outbox-v1`** (one `:entry:<operationId>` record) BEFORE any local state moves, retries on the same `operationId` (8 attempts, bounded backoff, `auth-required`/`reconciling` states), adopts the `200`/`202` authoritative data before the entry goes, treats a `409` semantic duplicate as reconciled success, and never stores a credential in the entry or in localStorage (PINs live in a separate in-memory map released on acknowledgment). The Tasks page, KidHome, `RewardSection`, `WeeklyPrizesCard` and `action-runner` have no local point writer left at all — `queueTaskCommand` is the only door. **Server-authoritative weekly rollover.** NEW `src/lib/task-week-rollover.ts::ensureCurrentTaskWeek` replaces the browser side effect: under the week lock it derives the family-local Monday, reads and archives the prior canonical week idempotently by `weekStart` (transaction/semantic dedupe even if a legacy duplicate exists), creates the current empty week, records Hall of Fame + prize outcomes from the archived history, and tombstones consumed recurring assigned/open/crew rows while issuing one fresh open clone with a NEW server id (crew clones start with empty members and an empty week-scoped `removed` list, keeping crew size and speed bonus). It repeats safely across devices and process restarts, so a fresh device has the same archive basis as every other. **Quarantine path.** NEW `src/lib/task-ledger-quarantine.ts` + `/api/tasks/quarantine` + `src/components/tasks/TaskLedgerQuarantineNotice.tsx`: legacy local week history is matched against the canonical server history by transaction id, then by reversal-aware `taskId + member`; exact and semantic matches are dropped and the UNMATCHED local rows are exported to `local-quarantine/task-ledger-<timestamp>.json` (gitignored, family data only) and shown as a one-time parent-visible notice (stamp `consuela-ledger-quarantine-handled-v1`, written only on a real export or an explicit dismiss so an empty first dry-run can't suppress it). Quarantined transactions are never auto-applied; the route is read-only against PocketBase. **Wave 3 is NOT done:** reward redemption, the planner point adjustment, the Hermes/MUSE task writers, briefing authority and all-time totals are explicitly still unremediated — `POST /api/rewards/redeem` still runs its own `withWeekLedgerLock` body instead of the shared helper, and no Hermes/MUSE path calls `executeInternalTaskCommand` yet. **Tests:** 21 new suites (command queue, Tasks/KidHome outbox commands, config-outbox callers, the full no-writer scan, ledger-operations, task-commands, task-config, task-manage registry, task-ledger contract, task-approval rollover integration, task-projection reconciler, reconcile script + import chain, quarantine route/lib/notice, rewards-redeem guest session, the retired-writer suites rewritten for the 410 contract); full suite **3165 passed (362 files)**, typecheck clean, eslint 0 on every touched file. **Ops: redeploy only** — no `pb:seed`, no schema or env change (the revision/receipts live inside the existing `consuela_data_snapshots` blob). Contracts: the `consuela-task-operation-outbox-v1` key and entry shape are never forked; an outbox entry is released only after authoritative data is adopted; lock order is week-ledger → snapshot-keyed only; `executeInternalTaskCommand` is the sole sanctioned internal command seam; `/api/tasks/quarantine` must never grow a `week_data`/snapshot write.

- 2026-09-23 — fix(tasks,kid,db): review wave — the two live symptoms root-caused and closed ("can't see some member photos", "some points missing"). **Symptom (a) photos:** 7 surfaces rendered `task.assigneeEmoji` raw as the member avatar — the 2026-09-23 sanitize gate + backfill had (correctly) collapsed photo avatars to `"👤"` on task rows while `members.emoji` still holds the real photos, so photo members showed a silhouette on exactly the rows the family reads. Fix: roster-first resolution everywhere (`assigneeEmojis` members-only map in `tasks/page.tsx`; pending/approval-queue/on-the-way/completed/AI-suggestion rows; `MemberSheet` pending rows via the sheet `entry.emoji`; `DailyQuestCard` new optional `rosterEmoji` prop) with the stored glyph as the fallback ONLY for non-member assignees ("Open"/"Crew"/"All") — the exact kid-board contract. **Symptom (b) points — four distinct mechanisms, all closed:** (1) **sync clobber (Critical):** `POST /api/tasks/sync` replaced the snapshot's tasks leg VERBATIM, so a parent's stale local list erased the `pendingApproval` the claim route had just written (kid devices never push the snapshot) — no self-heal, the parent's queue never showed it, the points were never paid. NEW `protectPendingOnPush` (`snapshot-tasks.ts`, wired into the route for BOTH parent and non-parent paths): a push may clear a stored live pending only with proof — an earn tx for that task in the PUSHER's `weekData.history`, or a `sentBackAt` stamp that does not pre-date the stored tap — or by carrying an even fresher claim (newest-claim-wins); symmetrically a push may not resurrect a pending the stored row already resolved (paid in the STORED history, or sent back after the tap). (2) **claim loss (Critical):** a failed fire-and-forget claim POST (network / 5xx / an expired 15-min kid session) stranded the tap forever — re-tap blocked, kids are redirected off `/tasks` so the self-cancel was unreachable, and a stuck pending row stopped recurring tasks from regenerating. NEW KidHome claim outbox (`claimOutboxRef`): failures queue and re-POST on every refresh tick until the server confirms (200/409 clear; 401 stalls with an honest "ask a grown-up" notice), LEGACY orphan pending rows older than 2 minutes are adopted on mount (the pre-server-handoff taps that were only ever in localStorage), and a PIN-free "↩ Not done" self-cancel reopens the row with the durable `sentBackAt` stamp. (3) **weekData adoption (Critical):** `postApprove` adopted the server's 200-body `weekData` unconditionally, erasing an offline approval's local-only tx and then pushing the truncated ledger everywhere. NEW `adoptServerWeekData` (`task-utils.ts`) mirrors `mergeTasksSnapshot`'s gates: server week newer → adopt, older → keep, same week → adopt only when the server history is at least as rich. (4) **5xx semantics:** a 502 from the approve route can be a partial apply (snapshot row cleared + week paid inside the lock before a later throw) — postApprove now treats 5xx exactly like a network failure (keep local, saved-locally path) instead of reverting into approve/un-approve churn. **Supporting fixes:** approve `findTask` union-heals a snapshot row whose pending was clobbered from the PB mirror row (the reversal-aware taskId+member ledger makes a stale mirror pending a skipped no-op, never a double-pay); NEW one-time ops script `scripts/consuela/reconcile-pending-approvals.mjs` re-stamps already-orphaned production snapshot rows with the same proof rules; `mergeTasksSnapshot` no longer resurrects an OFFLINE send-back (a local not-done row whose `sentBackAt` post-dates the remote `pendingApproval.at` keeps the reopen; a newer remote re-claim still wins); both claim-route pending patches now clear `sentBackAt` on the snapshot row (parity with the collection write — the 025f817 wave covered undo/crew but not these two); crew member emojis are gated through NEW `persistedCrewEmoji` at every PB write (`crewJoin`, `syncTasksToPB`, `mirrorTaskToCollection`) — same photo-bloat class the wave closed for `assigneeEmoji`; both client fallback roster mappings now carry the DECLARED `fullName` (`m.fullName ?? m.name`) instead of the display name, so a PB-down roster keys the ledger the same way the server and the live roster do. **Tests:** NEW `sync-pending-protection` (11 — stale push, earn-proof clear, send-back clear, stale-stamp no-clear, ghost-after-pay strip, ghost-after-send-back strip, fresh-pending self-heal accept, newest-claim-wins both directions, kid poison-weekData earns no proof, verbatim common path, tombstone wins), `member-fallback-fullname` (2), `task-row-roster-avatar` (5), `kid-claim-outbox` (6 — failure notice, retry-then-clear, 401 stall, legacy adoption, fresh-row in-flight guard, self-cancel stamp); extended `task-approve-route` (union-heal +18), `task-claim` (snapshot sentBackAt parity ×2, crew photo gate ×2 → 35), `tasks-pending-approval` (adoptServerWeekData ×5 → 20), `tasks-pending-merge` (offline send-back ×3 → 13), `tasks-pending-flow` (5xx keep-local), `tasks-member-photo-avatars` (row/queue/open-glyph ×3 → 5), and two pre-existing suites re-pointed to the new contracts (`leaderboard-quest-photo-avatar` MemberSheet photo now arrives via `entry.emoji`; `plan-tab-hydration` fallback lookup uses the declared fullName). **Gates (single sequential run):** `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; full vitest **337/337 files, 2478/2478 tests, 0 failed** (+4 files / +39 tests vs the 2439 baseline) with exactly the 2 documented pre-existing `tasks-approve-all` teardown `NotFoundError`s (did not grow); `next build` exit 0 (Compiled successfully, 46/46 static pages, `Home-ai/.next/BUILD_ID` present). **Ops:** redeploy + `npm run pb:seed` + `npx tsx scripts/consuela/reconcile-pending-approvals.mjs` (heals production orphans the push guard now prevents; idempotent, re-runnable). **Contracts:** the tasks leg's pending/send-back state is proof-gated on BOTH push and pull — never accept a clear/resurrection without an earn tx, a post-dating `sentBackAt`, or a fresher claim; kid claims must POST and stay retryable until confirmed, never silently local-only; `assigneeEmojis`/`entry.emoji` roster-first everywhere, stored glyph as fallback only; ledger adoption gates mirror `mergeTasksSnapshot`; fallback rosters carry declared `fullName`s.
- 2026-09-23 — fix(tasks,kid,db): unblock kid completions that never reached the parent approval queue — four approved fixes. **(1) `persistedTaskEmoji` (NEW `src/lib/task-emoji.ts`, `PB_TASK_EMOJI_MAX=5000`):** photo/data-URL or >5k `assigneeEmoji` values collapse to `"👤"` at every write boundary — claim route (child-claim 203, adult-claim 280, completeTask 424), `syncTasksToPB`, `mirrorTaskToCollection`, and the claim route's `upsertTask` payload — so a full `members.emoji` photo (Emily 212050 chars, etc.) can never again hit PB `validation_max_text_constraint` (root cause A). **(2) Live data backfill (ops, done):** snapshot `zkveguazoh6gfkl` stripped 5 bloat emojis → `"👤"`, PROBE task `1789853187732` dropped; 5 missing PB `tasks` rows created (laundry, Empty Trash, Shoes, Guest Bathroom `e0a9cbf219gzv9w`, kitchen table) with sanitized emoji — all 17 snapshot tasks now exist in PB, zero bloat both sides. **(3) KidHome assigned completions POST `/api/tasks/claim` (fire-and-forget):** under-10 after `tapCompletePending` (no pin, `memberName` = roster full name via `resolveMemberName`), 10+ after `verifyPinRemote` ok (`pin: questPin`); universal/snatch branch already claimed — this was the missing claim leg (root cause B). **(4) `db.upsertTask` rethrows** + `[tasks/sync]` / `db/[collection]/[id]` catch handlers now log `e?.data` and the gateway returns `{error:"db_error", detail, data}` with 502 instead of swallowing (root cause C). Supporting: `normalizeMemberName` prefers `fullName` (approve pays `pendingApproval.byName` into `week_data.points` — first name would split the ledger); no-pin claim branch allows `action === "complete"` for under-10 session; `tasks/page.tsx` still rolls back only on 400/409. Tests: NEW task-emoji-persist (6), task-utils-emoji-persist (3), snapshot-tasks-emoji-persist (2); task-claim 33/33; kid-home-quest-pin-safety 10/10; kid-pin-error-paths 9/9; kid-wall 15/15; tasks-claim-pending-flow 2/2 — target 45/45. Full suite **2439/2439 (333 files) — 0 failed** + the 2 documented pre-existing unhandled teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all.test.tsx` (must not grow); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (46/46, `Home-ai/.next/BUILD_ID` present). Ops: Fix 2 already applied to live PB+snapshot; after deploy run `npm run pb:seed`. Contracts: `persistedTaskEmoji` is the only assignee-emoji gate; kid assigned claims always POST; upsert failures must surface. - 2026-09-23 — feat(tasks): server-authoritative approvals (approve/approve-all/send-back). NEW POST /api/tasks/approve (middleware-exempt, parent-PIN self-gated, snapshot-primary lookup, withWeekLedgerLock → snapshot keyed lock) pays pendingApproval.points with per-payee taskId+member reversal-aware idempotency (fixes B1 speed-bonus loss + B6 two-device double-pay); send-back clears pending + sentBackAt and strips crew checkedInAt (B4); approve-all takes explicit taskIds and fails closed on unknown ids. - 2026-09-21 — fix(chat,tasks): chat task tools now operate on the SNAPSHOT the dashboard renders (they used to write the PB `tasks` collection, which the UI never reads — so chat completions/deletes were invisible, and the browser's periodic `syncTasksToPB` re-upserted its snapshot rows, resurrecting chat deletes and duplicating rows). NEW `src/lib/snapshot-tasks.ts` (server helper: `readSnapshotTasks`, `mutateSnapshot` under the SAME `snapshot:tasks-snapshot` keyed lock the sync/claim routes use, `findSnapshotTask`, `deleteSnapshotTask` + `upsertSnapshotTask`, `mirrorTaskToCollection` best-effort replica sync). Rewired `add_task` / `update_task` / `delete_task` / `complete_task` / `reopen_task` / `get_completed_tasks` (hermes-tools) and `pendingTaskRows` (live-reads, which also STOPPED fabricating points from priority — the old `high→20 / medium→15` guess). **Deletions need a tombstone:** the client merge is add-only, so removals are recorded in the snapshot's `deletedTaskIds` (new `loadDeletedTaskIds`/`saveDeletedTaskIds` in task-utils); `mergeTasksSnapshot` unions local + snapshot tombstones and drops those rows (returns `deletedTaskIds` too); the Tasks page persists them and POSTs them in the snapshot body; `/api/tasks/sync` UNIONs tombstones server-side so a stale device's push can't resurrect a delete (and strips tombstoned rows from the body). **Also fixed:** `mergeTasksSnapshot` deduped by TITLE alone, so same-titled chores for different members ("Walking Dogs" ×3) collapsed to one — it now dedupes by id OR (title + assignee). **Fix A (prompt truth):** NEW `TASK_ACTIONS_ADDENDUM` in `consuela-prompts.ts` (deletes are immediate/no PIN; completions queue at Tasks → "Needs approval" only; there is NO "Settings → Approvals"/bulk-delete approval — the model had been inventing one) + corrected `delete_task`/`complete_task`/`reopen_task` tool descriptions. Tests: `snapshot-tasks` (4), `task-utils-snapshot-tombstones` (4), `task-utils-snapshot-per-assignee` (2), prompt truth (3); updated `hermes-tools-task-crud` (11), `hermes-tools-filters`, `hermes-tools-live-reads`, `tasks-sync-legs`/`race`, `task-utils-snapshot-merge`. Full suite 2385/2385 (327 files), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean. Deployed as image `chat-tasks-snapshot-v1` (previous `reset-countdown-v1` kept as the stopped `consuela-dashboard-old`). Ops: `npm run pb:seed`; one-time reconcile of the `tasks` collection to the snapshot's live ids (14 stale/duplicate rows deleted). **Contracts:** `src/lib/snapshot-tasks.ts` is the ONLY task-write path for chat — never re-point a chat task tool at the `tasks` collection; the `deletedTaskIds` tombstone is unioned, never replaced; the merge dedupes by id OR title+assignee.

- 2026-09-21 — fix(leaderboard): the week-reset countdown read "Resets tonight!" every Monday. `getDaysUntilWeekReset()` (`src/lib/task-utils.ts`) returned `(8 - getDay()) % 7`, which is `0` on Monday — and `PrizeRaceCard`/`KidHome` render `<=0` as "Resets tonight!" while the Home widget renders it as "Resets in 0 days". But the week runs Monday 00:00 → Sunday 23:59, so Monday is the FIRST day of a fresh race — the reset is 7 days away. Fix: `days === 0 ? 7 : days` (Sunday stays 1 → "Resets tomorrow"). Added 3 regression cases to `tests/unit/task-utils.test.ts`. Same session, a live data correction (all in PocketBase + the `consuela_data_snapshots` snapshot): the week of **Sep 14** now holds the kids' work (Bailey 35, Emily 40, Jasmine 20 — chores dated Tue Sep 15, Walking Dogs Sun Sep 20), the week of **Sep 21** (current) is **zero** with no completions, and the week of **Sep 7** is Aurora-only (kids removed from `week_data`/`week_archive`/`hall_of_fame`). Deployed as image tag `reset-countdown-v1` (previous `kid-points-v2` kept stopped as `consuela-dashboard-old`). **Contract:** `getDaysUntilWeekReset()` must never return 0 (Monday = 7); the `<=0` branches in `PrizeRaceCard`/`KidHome` are effectively dead but harmless. **Note:** the dashboard reads its own browser snapshot, so a direct-DB edit only takes effect after the browser reloads and adopts the snapshot.

- 2026-09-08 — fix(backend,sync): schedules + 7 more silent-dead PB write paths unblocked — legacy required fields healed, Calendar schedule sync made honest. Root cause (user-reported "schedule made last night not reflecting on dashboard even after saving through Settings"): live PB `schedules` carried a legacy REQUIRED `userId` the app never sends → every save 400'd, swallowed by `saveSched()`'s `.catch(() => {})`. Tests +16 (schedule-time 12, seed-heal +5, schedule-mapper 6, pending-writes-schedules 3); suite 1339/1339, tsc clean, eslint 0 new, build clean. - 2026-08-31 — fix(auth,ops): PIN login broken after NAS deploy — wrong PB_ADMIN_PASS in container env. **Root cause:** the container swap during the demo-data-purge deploy (2026-08-27) passed `PB_ADMIN_PASS=<REDACTED-NAS-PW>` (the NAS SSH password) instead of the real PB superuser password — the new container's env came from the NAS deploy-dir `.env.local`, which had the same wrong value, instead of the authoritative `/tmp/new.env`. Login (`POST /api/auth/login` → `verifyPinFromPB` → `withAdmin`) needs admin PB auth to read the LOCKED_RULES `members` collection, so every `_superusers/auth-with-password` call returned `400 Failed to authenticate` → every PIN (right or wrong) got `500 {"error":"Login failed"}`. The container was also named `home-dashboard`, silently breaking the admin tools' name allow-list (`/api/admin/restart|update|containers` reference `consuela-dashboard`), and `CRON_SECRET` was empty (fail-closes all 4 cron jobs). **Fix (ops-only, no code change):** rebuilt the env from the running container with `PB_ADMIN_PASS` + `CRON_SECRET` restored from `/tmp/new.env`, swapped to a new container with the canonical name `consuela-dashboard` (same image `multi-store-v2`, `--env-file`, same network, `--restart unless-stopped`), and corrected the poison source (NAS deploy-dir `.env.local` now has the real PB password). **Verified live:** wrong PIN → 401 `{"error":"Invalid PIN"}` (was 500); real login (Rebecca + real PIN from PB) → 200 + session cookie + member payload; all pages 200; gateway unauthenticated → clean 401; no auth errors in container logs; demo data still 0. `DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` gotchas #9 (canonical container name) + #10 (never hand-type env; use `--env-file /tmp/new.env`; check CRON_SECRET) added.

New `tests/unit/stores.test.ts` (2). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, price-compare 6/6 + stores 2/2.

- 2026-08-24 — feat(security): server-side login; PINs leave the client bundle (session-auth Task 9). `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx` — `login()` is now async and POSTs to `/api/auth/login` (PB-verified, httpOnly cookie); `AuthUser` drops `pin`; localStorage `consuela-auth-user` stores id/name/role/emoji/color/avatarSize/glow only; hydrate path sanitizes legacy stored objects; PB-restore path no longer fabricates a pin; `changePin()` removed (ProfileSheet posts current+new PIN to `/api/members/pin` itself). `PinModal` awaits the async login. `src/components/ui/EmergencyButton.tsx` — in-modal family-PIN input replaces the removed `currentUser.pin` (typed per alert; type buttons disabled until 4 digits). `useSuggestions.ts` — pin cache no longer seeded from auth context (needsPin modal flow unchanged). `src/app/api/emergency/route.ts` — verification swapped from the `db.selectMembers()` client-cache loop to `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember(pin)` (server/PB truth); invalid → **401** `Invalid PIN` (was 403); `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS` behavior unchanged (env now read per request so tests can stub it). Client-bundle credential purge: `member-fallback.ts` + `db/fallback-data.ts` rows stripped of PINs; member cache seeding (`resolveMemberPin` injection) removed from `db/index.ts`/`pb-db.ts` fallback branches; seed-side defaults moved to `MEMBER_DEFAULT_PINS` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (server-only) applied by `server-auth.ts::withResolvedPins` so fresh installs still verify + `findOrCreateMemberRecord` still seeds real values into PB. Settings member edit: blank PIN field = "leave unchanged" (no wipe). Tests: new emergency-pin-server (4) + use-auth-login (2); emergency-notify re-pointed at the server verifier; scripts/consuela/test-member-pins.mjs rewritten for the no-default-client-pin contract. Suite 471/471, tsc clean. Known follow-up: Tasks-page completion/undo/claim/parent-approval still verify against PB-served cached pins client-side — needs its own server route.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,tier-2): admin-route PIN gate, honest Telegram delivery, Google 410 self-heal, server-authoritative task claims. **(1) /api/admin/* gated** (`src/lib/admin-auth.ts`): every route (version/containers/update/restart) now requires either `x-admin-pin` verified against a NON-child family member (Settings UI sends the session PIN; kids get 403 `adult_only` even with a valid PIN) or `Authorization: Bearer $ADMIN_SECRET` (server-only env, used by chat-tool internal self-fetches in hermes-tools.ts). Fail-closed when neither is configured. `ADMIN_SECRET` added to docker-compose (required) + .env.example. Tests: admin-auth (4) + admin-routes (8). **(2) Telegram failures are no longer reported as delivered** (`src/lib/ha/notify.ts`, `notify-test/route.ts`): `sendTelegramMessage` returns `{success:false}` instead of throwing, but callers only counted thrown errors — a dead bot token showed "Sent ✓" including on the EMERGENCY path. broadcastHouseAlert now checks `.success` (failed leg lands in notes), notify-test returns 502 on failure. Tests: ha-notify + new ha-notify-test-route (2). **(3) Google sync survives expired tokens**: `googleFetch` errors now carry `.status`; `syncCalendar` catches HTTP 410 GONE, discards the stale syncToken and performs a full-window resync in the same run (previously every cron run failed forever until manual DB surgery). Non-410 errors still fail loudly. Tests: google-calendar-410 (2). **(4) Task claims are server-authoritative** (`api/tasks/claim`): points/title come from the PB task row (client body ignored — no more forged `points:999999`); unknown taskId → 404; post-write re-read detects a lost concurrent write race and reports an honest 409 `claimedBy` instead of silently erasing the first claimant's points. Tests: task-claim (4). Suite: 428/428, tsc clean, eslint clean.


- 2026-08-24 — fix(ops): tier-1 backend fixes — PB SDK crash, cron auth fail-open, compose conflict. **(1)** `autoCancellation()` removed everywhere (`src/lib/pb.ts`, `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` + 2 test mocks): the method no longer exists in pocketbase JS SDK v0.26+ (installed 0.27.0 — auto-cancellation was removed upstream and requests are never auto-cancelled by default, so deletion is behavior-identical). Root cause of the long-standing integration-test crash `cachedAdminPB.autoCancellation is not a function`; underneath it sat a second masked layer: vitest provided no PB admin credentials, so `ensureAuth()` threw after the first crash was cleared. Fixed by adding dummy `PB_ADMIN_EMAIL`/`PB_ADMIN_PASS` to `vitest.config.ts` `test.env` (tests mock `@/lib/pb` entirely — no network). **Test suite now fully green: 407/407.** **(2)** Cron bearer gate fail-closed: new shared `src/lib/cron-auth.ts` `isCronAuthorized()` rejects ALL traffic when `CRON_SECRET` is unset (previously the literal header `"Bearer undefined"` authenticated on 5 routes: ha-todo-mirror, briefing, google-sync, suggestions, telegram-poll); all six cron routes including score-importance now use it. Unit-tested in `tests/unit/cron-auth.test.ts`; score-importance route-level tests still pass unchanged. **(3)** `Home-ai/docker-compose.yml`: resolved stale HEAD-vs-kitchen-ui conflict markers (HEAD side had corrupted `${PB_ADM...D}` placeholders and wrong var name `PB_ADMIN_PASSWORD`) by taking the kitchen-ui side; `PB_ADMIN_EMAIL/PASS` and `CRON_SECRET` are now required (`${VAR:?…}` fails fast) instead of shipping `changeme` defaults; YAML validated with js-yaml.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,ha): alarm arm/disarm is human-only and PIN-gated. `alarm_control_panel` removed from the `/api/ha/call-service` allowlist (`src/lib/ha/service-allowlist.ts`) — the general unauthenticated route now 403s every alarm service. New `POST /api/ha/alarm` (`src/app/api/ha/alarm/route.ts`): validates action ∈ {arm_home→alarm_arm_home, disarm→alarm_disarm} + entity_id `^alarm_control_panel\.[a-z0-9_]+$`, verifies a family-member PIN server-side via `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember` (missing/wrong → 401 `unauthorized`), then forwards over the HA WebSocket. UI: shared `AlarmPinModal` (`src/components/ha/AlarmPinModal.tsx`) + `useAlarmCall()` hook (`useHAState.ts`); SecurityPanel and HomeSecurityWidget arm/disarm buttons now require PIN confirmation (kid mode unchanged — buttons hidden). LLM/chat tools never receive an alarm tool. Tests (TDD): new `tests/unit/ha-alarm-route.test.ts` (7 cases), ha-routes +2 blocked-alarm cases, allowlist tests updated, ha-tab/ha-widgets rewritten to the PIN-modal contract ("no fetch until PIN submitted"). Verified: 403/404 tests pass (1 pre-existing pb-auth failure), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,ha): /api/ha/call-service now enforces a domain/service allowlist. New `src/lib/ha/service-allowlist.ts` (`HA_ROUTE_ALLOWED_SERVICES` + `isHAServiceAllowed`) and enforcement in `src/app/api/ha/call-service/route.ts` (403 `service_not_allowed` before the HA WebSocket forward). Allowed: light/switch (toggle/turn_on/turn_off), climate (set_temperature/set_hvac_mode), vacuum (start/pause/stop/return_to_base), alarm_control_panel (alarm_arm_home/alarm_disarm — Security panel). Blocked: lock.*, script.*, automation.trigger, shell_command.*, notify.*, cover.*, scene/media_player via this route, alarm_arm_away/alarm_trigger. Previously ANY `^[a-z0-9_]+$` domain/service was forwarded unauthenticated (the "alarms and locks excluded" guarantee existed only in the chat tool matrix). Internal trusted callers (todo-mirror, notify fan-out) use the WS client directly and are unaffected; chat house-control tools unchanged. - 2026-08-21 — fix(ops): Home rendering + nav color restored after Turbopack CSS desync. Symptom: entire app unstyled (bottom nav lost its neon-lime pill, widgets stacked in raw DOM order — "calendar at the top of Home", same breakage on every page). Root cause: the long-running `next dev` server (started 07:08) went out of sync with the committed CSS chunks after the HA feature's commit churn (the known Next 16.2.6/16.3 Turbopack CSS-manifest bug documented in the Build Note above) — served HTML linked only the framework chunk and dropped the globals.css chunk entirely (`--capsule-accent` computed empty). Verified via Playwright after restart: `--capsule-accent: #78f05a` + active pill `rgb(120,240,90)` + `nav.fixed` restored; Home, /calendar, /tasks, /meals, /chat, /settings all clean at 1440/768/390 (0 non-HA console errors, no horizontal overflow). **Not touched (other agent's in-flight HA feature):** `/api/ha/sync` still 502s because `HA_HOST`/`HA_TOKEN` are absent from `.env.local` (`getHAConfig()` throws) — the HA widgets render their graceful empty/error states; flagged to user, left for the HA workstream.

- 2026-08-21 — fix(db): PocketBase access rules repaired — Home no longer shows demo fallback data. `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — new `PUBLIC_RULES` const (all five rules = `""`) + `rulesMatch(live)` self-heal in `seedCollections()`: newly created collections get open rules, and existing collections whose rules aren't open are patched (`(rules opened)` marker in the seed log). Root cause: the PB instance was recreated 2026-08-21 (collections created 11:03–11:32 with default closed `null` rules via `pb_migrations`), so every unauthenticated client read (`getPB()` — the app has no client PB auth) returned 403, `safeList` fell back to hardcoded demo data, and the Home screen showed fake events ("Soccer Practice", "Dentist — Bailey", "Team dinner"), fake tasks ("Take out trash", "Grocery run"), and no "Upcoming important" section (its public-client `getList` 403'd into the empty catch). Re-ran `seedCollections()` against the live instance: rules opened on all 22 app collections, missing `auth_sessions` collection created, grocery/pantry/meal/task schema fields patched. Verified via Playwright: 0 PB 4xx/5xx, Today widget shows "Quiet day" + the Upcoming important section (Doctor appointment / Game vs Riverside / Parent Teacher Conference with dates and members), fallback demo markers gone, no horizontal overflow at 1440/768/390; `tsc --noEmit` clean, eslint clean. **Security note (flagged to user):** open rules restore the pre-existing design (client has no auth; data incl. member PIN fields is readable on the LAN). Hardening (auth layer + auth-based rules) is future work.

- 2026-08-20 — fix(ui): weather widget fits its 1×1 bento cell (sun on card, no overflow). Root cause: `WeatherWidget` rendered the centered icon strip OUTSIDE the immersive card (in-flow, ~116px) while the card itself was `h-full` (350px), so icon + card = ~466px in a 350px `auto-rows-[350px]` grid cell — the sun floated in empty space and the card spilled ~116px onto the widget below (tablet + desktop; phone `auto-rows-min` was unaffected). Fix (`src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx`): moved the icon strip INSIDE the card (first in-flow child above the glassmorphism overlay, `z-30`, `pt-3`, icon box 96→80px / scale 1.3333→1.1111) so the sun sits on the card and the whole widget is a single `h-full` flex column that fits exactly 350px. Verified: card top/bottom align with its grid cell (358/708 @1440, no overflow) at 1440/768/390; weather unit tests 2/2; vitest 167/168 (1 pre-existing PB-env); typecheck clean.

- 2026-08-18 — fix(ui): Kitchen "orb" true root cause — glass ::before position containment. `src/app/globals.css` — added `position: relative` to `.material-thin/.glass-subtle`, `.material-regular/.glass`, `.material-thick/.glass-strong`. Root cause: those base classes are `position: static`, so their `::before` specular (`position:absolute; inset:0; radial-gradient(120% 60% at 20% 0%); mix-blend-mode:screen`) resolved `inset:0` against the nearest positioned ancestor `<main class="relative z-10">` (full page), painting a giant top-left glow on every `.glass` card (Meals stat tiles + meal cards, Pantry stat/item cards). Grocery's top cards are `.widget-card` (no `::before`) → always clean. Containment removes the page-wide orb; also calmed `.glass::before` 0.05→0.02, `.liquid-glass::before` 0.06→0.02, `.widget-card` glow 40px@22%→20px@12% (dark) / hover 48px@28%→24px@14% / light 32px@16%→16px@8%. Verified via Playwright: Meals + Pantry top-left now flat dark, matching Grocery. AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

Verified: typecheck clean, lint 0 new (73 baseline), vitest 149/150 (1 pre-existing PB-env integration failure), build clean, Playwright smoke (Home hides toggled-off widget; knob animates). AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

- 2026-08-18 — fix(ui): Settings Layout & display — reorder arrows work with real clicks (drag scoped to the ⋮⋮ handle). Root cause: the Visible-group row `<div>` in `src/app/settings/page.tsx` was `draggable` with `onDragStart` — any mousedown + a few px of movement on a child control (↑/↓ arrows, visibility toggles) fired `dragstart` on the row and cancelled the child's click (Playwright event log: `mousedown: BUTTON → dragstart: DIV`, no `click` ever fired — real mouse/trackpad clicks almost always jitter 2–5px, so the arrows only worked for pixel-perfect clicks). Fix: `draggable` + `onDragStart` moved from the row `<div>` to the ⋮⋮ handle `<span>`; the row keeps `onDragOver` / `onDragLeave` / `onDrop` / `onDragEnd` (`dragend` bubbles from the handle, so drop targeting + cleanup unchanged). Verified via Playwright: plain + jittered arrow clicks both reorder; handle drag-and-drop still reorders; 112/112 unit tests pass; typecheck clean; no new lint errors. AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

- 2026-08-11 — feat(ui): Three-mode Home layouts — phone / tablet / desktop buckets (5 commits: `test(ui)` layout-config 3-mode rewrite (red, 23 tests — replaces the 4 stale failures from the 2026-08-07 filmstrip change) → `feat(ui)` 3-mode core → `feat(ui)` v3 storage → `refactor(ui)` useOrientation/computeLayoutMode + LayoutMode rename → `feat(ui)` settings Tablet bucket). `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — `LayoutMode = "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop"`; `PHONE_MAX_WIDTH = 700` / `TABLET_MAX_WIDTH = 1280`; pure `computeLayoutMode(isPortrait, width)` (landscape OR ≥1280 → desktop; portrait <700 → phone; portrait 700–1279 → tablet; SSR via the hook assumes desktop). `HomeLayoutConfig` = `{ phone: { widgets }, tablet: { widgets }, desktop: { widgets } }` stored as v3 in `consuela-home-layout` (plain JSON via `saveLayoutConfig` — no version constant). New `src/components/ui/CapsuleNav.tsx` replaces the deleted `BottomNav.tsx` as the primary navigation: a floating bottom-center glass capsule (charcoal `rgba(8,10,12,0.60)` dark / `rgba(0,0,0,0.16)` light via the same `data-theme` MutationObserver, 24px blur + saturate, 1px inner top highlight, `0 24px 48px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.55)` drop shadow) hosting the same 6 routes/icons (Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Settings, More; inline SVGs, strokeWidth 2→2.5 active). - 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): final sweep regressions — PIN flow for suggestions, engine condition-dedup, minors (consuela-integration branch, one commit). **C3 client PIN flow:** `useSuggestions.ts` now keeps an in-memory PIN cache (seeded from `currentUser.pin`, never persisted), exposes `needsPin` + `submitPin(pin)`; when no PIN is available the pending dismiss/snooze/act is queued and a new shared `SuggestionPinModal` (mirrors the tasks-page PIN modal; guest copy "Enter any family member's 4-digit PIN") is rendered by the Home widget and /suggestions page; a 401 clears the cached pin and re-prompts with "Wrong PIN. Try again." (never a silent no-op); non-401 failures surface a toast. **C1 engine condition-dedup:** `runEngine` now fetches all pending rows (`status="pending"`, any scopeDate/snooze state) and skips inserts whose `kind` + normalized title (trim + lowercase, mirroring the hash normalization) already have a pending row — exactly one pending row per condition, so persistent conditions no longer accumulate one visible row per day; snoozing hides the single row; acting/dismissing frees the condition for re-creation. **Minors:** M-A `complete_task` "already completed" copy is now generic ("earlier this week or previously") since `done` rows include same-week completions; M-B the suggestions cron route + engine now use `localTodayISO()` (matching the briefing generator) so 5pm–midnight local scans land on today's local scopeDate; M-C `/api/consuela/briefing` validates `scopeDate` against `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$` (400 `{error:"invalid scopeDate"}`, mirrors the L10 threadId hardening); M-D the suggestions PATCH wraps `db.updateSuggestion` in try/catch → 400 `{ok:false,error}` instead of a 500 (act route already did); M-E `public/version.json` regenerated. TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass (test-engine.mjs +3 C1 dedup steps, test-suggestions.mjs +2 C3 client-flow + briefing-validation steps, test-chat-tool-call.mjs asserts the new M-A string) plus a Playwright DOM check of the PIN modal on both /suggestions and the Home widget (guest prompt, wrong-pin re-prompt, valid-pin retry, cancel).
- 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): post-scan bug sweep — 17 fixes (consuela-integration branch, one commit). **Critical:** C1 `selectPendingSuggestions` dropped the `scopeDate>="today"` clause (snoozed rows survived past midnight; scopeDate is now an optional exact-match filter); C2 `/act` route + `action_suggestion` no longer pass `scopeDate: today` so past-day suggestions are findable by id; C3 `/api/consuela/suggestions` PATCH + POST /act now require a family-member PIN (`x-consuela-pin` header or cookie, verified against PB; GET stays public) and the widget/page forward the session PIN. **Important:** I1 chat SYSTEM_PROMPT gained the "use a read tool to check current state" rule and `persistChatPair` skips empty/fallback replies; I2 Telegram poller got a 30s AbortController timeout, `r.ok` check, and 429 handling (offset untouched, `{ok:false, reason:"rate_limited"}`); I3 `insertChatMessage` accepts an optional `createdAt` (poll passes the real send time); I4 `complete_task` excludes `status:"done"` rows and returns "Task was already completed (last week)…" instead of double-earning; I5 `action_suggestion` treats `ok:false` as failure and leaves the suggestion pending; I6 `setState` gained an expected-prev CAS arg (telegram offset writes refuse concurrent advances); I7 new `src/lib/local-date.ts` (`localTodayISO` via `en-CA`/TZ) anchors suggestions + briefings + widget refresh to the local calendar day. **Minor:** L1 dedup hash now includes severity + action args; L2 conflict scanner uses `<30` boundary + overnight spill detection (≥23:00 prev day vs <01:30 today); L3 briefing cron is idempotent (existing row → skip, ack preserved); L4 briefing ack is optimistic with rollback + "Couldn't save — try again" toast; L5 briefing empty sections render nothing (no blank gaps); L6 layout self-heal inserts `morningBriefing` at index 0 / `consuelaSuggestions` at index 1 (not appended); L7 Google quota counter computes DATE per call + serialized queue (no lost updates); L8 google-sync gained an in-process lock (concurrent cron fire → `{ok:false, reason:"already_in_progress"}`); L9 chat page dead `actions`/`runAction` code removed; L10 `/api/chat/messages` rejects malformed `threadId` (400) + Telegram error messages redact the bot token. AGENTS.md §3.5 (UTC thread ids), §5.2 (PIN requirement), §5.3 (tool-call transcripts not persisted) updated. TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass with new per-finding assertions.
- 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): final review sweep (consuela-integration branch). **F1 snooze:** `useSuggestions.ts` snooze now sends only `snoozedUntil` (row stays `status:"pending"`; the `(snoozedUntil=null || snoozedUntil<now)` filter hides it until it expires); `/api/consuela/suggestions` PATCH accepts id + snoozedUntil without status. **F2 conflict parser:** `engine.ts` `parseMinutes` mirrors `parseTimeToMinutes` (12-hour AM/PM aware — 12:45 PM vs 1:15 PM now detected); regression events added to test-engine.mjs. **F3 complete_task:** hermes-tools `complete_task` now updates the `tasks` row (`status:"done"`, `completedInWeek`, `completedAt`) after the week_data earn so tasks leave Pending and can't double-earn; 2nd `tasks` seed schema gained `status` (select pending/done) + `completedInWeek` + `completedAt`; test-chat-tool-call.mjs asserts the row flip + single earn tx. **F4 act button:** new `POST /api/consuela/suggestions/act` (dispatches `actionPayload.tool` via `getTool`, marks the suggestion `actioned`, 400 on error with the suggestion left pending); Home widget + /suggestions page act buttons now POST + toast + refresh; navigation kept only for view tools (get_pending_tasks / get_weekly_meals / open_calendar). **Minors:** GET /api/consuela/suggestions honors `?limit=` (1..200, default 20); `action_suggestion` only marks `actioned` when the tool result has no error (tool throw/error leaves it pending); briefing GET defaults to today's scopeDate (null when today's briefing doesn't exist); `public/version.json` regenerated to branch HEAD; `docker-compose.yml` wires `TELEGRAM_MIRROR_BOT_TOKEN` (empty default); `next.config.ts` gains `allowedDevOrigins: ["127.0.0.1","localhost"]` (fixes dev-server client-bundle blocking that broke Playwright UI tests hitting 127.0.0.1); test-chat-route-mock.mjs counts only Hermes fetches (route persists chat pairs to PB, skewing the old global fetch count). TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass.

- 2026-06-15 — fix(grocery): adding an item no longer wipes the list. `src/hooks/useGrocery.ts` `addGroceryItem` was replacing the entire UI state with the PocketBase cache via `setGroceryItems(fresh.length ? fresh : [...groceryItems, ...])`; because the 11 default items in `initialGroceryItems` are local-only and the `grocery_list_items` PB collection has no schema fields (PB silently drops `name`/`category`/`emoji` on create and returns only `{id, collectionId, collectionName}`), the new item landed as a nameless 📦 and the rest of the list vanished. Rewrote `addGroceryItem` to use functional `setGroceryItems(prev => [...prev, newItem])` and build the new item from the local input. Added `mergeFreshIntoState` and routed `syncMealToGrocery` / `syncPantryToGrocery` through it so sync paths merge DB items by name instead of replacing local-only items. Extended `seedCollections` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` to diff `live.schema` against the seed schema and `pb.collections.update({ schema: merged })` to add only the missing fields — re-running the seed is now self-healing. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean.

Fix: the bridge is now rendered inside the `case "weather"` switch case, directly below `<WeatherWidget />` inside the same `<div className="relative z-10">` wrapper. Bridge `marginTop: -2px / marginBottom: -4px` overlap still flows the gradient and seasonal `theme.particleEmoji` into whatever widget sits immediately below weather in user order. Move weather to position 3 → bridge follows to position 3. The fullscreen `FogBackground` Vanta fog (page-wide ambient, `position: fixed; inset: 0`) is intentionally untouched per user request.
