- 2026-06-15 — fix: Meal/recipe/grocery/pantry reliability + planner motion removal. *(Landed on `warm-glass-v2` as the `feat(meals):` commit above.)* `RecipeModal` now targets either meal or catalog via `mode` prop (was always saving as meal, corrupting meal state when editing catalog recipes). Recipes added from the modal go to the catalog only — assign to a slot later from Meals or Recipes tab. `MealsTab` gets a collapsed `RecipeCatalogStrip` above the day strip so users can browse the catalog while planning, with "Add to {activeDay} as {next empty slot}" one-tap insertion. `usePantry` now writes to the real DB (adds `db.deletePantryItem` to `src/db/index.ts` + `src/db/pb-db.ts`), merges DB + localStorage on load, and accepts `groceryItems` to power a "Grocery items waiting to be restocked" panel with one-tap "Add to pantry". Pantry delete is two-tap with a 3s confirmation window (no modal). `useGrocery` accepts `plannedMeals` from `useMeals` so `mealSyncService.syncMealPlanToGrocery` reflects what the user actually planned (was reading stale `db.selectMeals()`), and sync quantities now include units (`"1 cup"`, `"1 lb"`, etc.). Manual grocery add supports qty/notes inputs, "✨ Auto category" option, and friendly duplicate handling ("already on your list" toast + keep existing row). `useRecipes` normalizes tags/ingredients on save and dedupes by id then name. `GroceryTab`, `PantryTab`, `RecipesTab`, and `MealsTab` had all float/bob/scale/translate animations removed (color transitions, focus rings, and progress bars remain). `src/services/types.ts` `Meal` interface updated with `mealType`, `protein`, `carbs`, `fat`, `instructions`. `emptyRecipe` in `src/data/meals.ts` now includes `mealType`.

- 2026-06-15 — feat(integrations): Real Google Calendar + Tasks + Reminders sync. **Phase 1 of 3** from the API research plan. Replaces the static-JSON stub at `public/google-events.json` and `public/google-tasks.json` with real Google APIs. The previous `/api/google-calendar` route read those JSON files; the new version reads from a new `consuela_google_calendar_events` PB collection populated by `src/lib/google/calendar.ts:listAllEvents` + `syncCalendar`. The same architecture backs Tasks via `consuela_google_tasks`, `consuela_google_tasklists`, `consuela_google_sync_state`, and `consuela_google_api_usage` collections, all created idempotently on first request by `src/lib/google/pb-collections.ts:ensureGoogleCollections` (now uses `fields` + per-type `options` shape, since PB v0.27 silently drops schema fields missing the `options` key). **OAuth via Google Device Flow** (`src/lib/google/device-auth.ts`) so the home-hosted dashboard never needs a public redirect URI — the user signs in at `google.com/device` on any device with a one-time code. Required OAuth client type in Google Cloud Console: **"TVs and Limited Input Devices"** (Client ID + Client Secret). **Tokens are AES-256-GCM encrypted at rest** in `consuela_google_tokens` (`src/lib/google/encryption.ts`, key from `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` env var; 12 unit tests in `scripts/google/test-encryption.mjs`). `src/lib/google/oauth-client.ts` provides a singleton `googleFetch` wrapper that auto-refreshes the access token on 401 with a 30s in-flight lock to prevent thundering-herd refreshes. **5-minute client-side polling** via `useGoogleSync` hook + the `useGoogleReminders` hook on the Tasks page (auto-refreshes on tab visibility change too). New `Settings → Integrations` card (`GoogleConnectCard.tsx`) handles the full lifecycle: unconnected → waiting (code + countdown pill + copy/open buttons) → connected (account email + sync status + Sync now + Disconnect) → error:denied/expired/revoked/config. **Google Tasks is wired but paused** — Google's Device Flow allowed-scopes list does NOT include the Tasks API (confirmed via direct curl: `POST /device/code` with `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/tasks` returns `{"error":"invalid_scope","error_description":"Invalid device flow scope: https://www.googleapis.com/auth/tasks"}`). Calendar and openid/email work fine. Tasks code is complete (auto-creates a "Consuela" tasklist on first connect, Reminders = Tasks with `due` field, `<RemindersSection />` at the top of Pending groups by Overdue/Today/Tomorrow/This week/Later with one-tap complete). The Reminders section shows a "paused — Tasks scope not granted" notice with the upgrade path. To activate Tasks: switch the OAuth client to "Web" type in Google Cloud Console, expose the dashboard over a public HTTPS URL (Tailscale Funnel / Cloudflare Tunnel / ngrok paid tier), add the Tasks scope to `GOOGLE_OAUTH_SCOPES`, and reconnect. New API routes: `/api/google/device-grant`, `/api/google/device-poll`, `/api/google/device-revoke`, `/api/google/state`, `/api/google/sync`, `/api/google/webhook`, `/api/google-tasks`. Webhook route is pre-wired but not used in v1 (no public URL to receive Google's push); falls back to polling. Per-day API quota counter in `consuela_google_api_usage` (soft 50k, hard 100k) to prevent runaway loops from blowing the 1M/day Calendar free tier. Unit tests: 12/12 encryption + 8/8 device-auth passing. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean, `npm run build` clean. New env vars in `.env.example`: `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GOOGLE_OAUTH_SCOPES` (Calendar-only by default), `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, `GOOGLE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`. `tsconfig.json` got `allowImportingTsExtensions: true` so the new Google lib files can use `.ts` extensions on relative imports (lets the unit tests run via `node --experimental-strip-types`). `public/google-events.json` and `public/google-tasks.json` are no longer read by anything — deleted. `pb-auth.ts` got a `.ts` extension on its `pb` import for the same reason. **Live verification**: with the user's TVs OAuth client (project `graceful-mile-496715-k7`, client `255734025602-...us.googleusercontent.com`), the `/api/google/device-grant` endpoint returned a real Google `device_code` + `user_code: FFY-KPK-PXNS` (test run, code expired). User can now tap Settings → Integrations → Connect to get their own code. **Common Journey added**: "How do I connect Google Calendar + Tasks + Reminders?", "Why don't I see Reminders on the Tasks tab?", "How do I add a reminder?", "Why is the Reminders section on the Tasks tab showing 'paused'?"

Added `apricot` and `sage` accents. Verified with `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` (warnings only), and `npm run build`.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Hydration mismatch on the Meals page (the "Who's eating tonight" avatar strip). Root cause: `src/db/index.ts:210-229` hydrates `membersStore` from `localStorage` at module load. On the server, `typeof window === "undefined"` falls back to `membersData` (defaults: 🐱, 🧒, etc.), but on the client it reads localStorage and gets the user's custom avatars (often base64 data URLs like `data:image/webp;base64,UklGR...`). `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx:92-95` was calling `db.selectMembersDetailed()` inside a `useMemo(..., [])`, so SSR rendered `🐱` and the client first render rendered the data URL — a React hydration mismatch (`+ data:image/webp...  − 🐱`). Fix: added the established `mounted` pattern (`useState(false)` + `useEffect(() => setMounted(true), [])`) and gated both the `familyMembers` and the `tip` `useMemo`s with it. Until mount, `familyMembers` returns the default emoji list (matches what the server saw) and `tip` returns `smartTips[0]` (deterministic, no `Date.now()`). After mount, both re-pick their real values. Other pages in the app already use this pattern (`tasks/page.tsx:216`, `page.tsx:43`, `chat/page.tsx`, `settings/page.tsx`, etc.) — MealsTab was the only consumer of `db.selectMembersDetailed()` that didn't. Heads up: `tasks/page.tsx:221` and `chat/page.tsx:289` also call `db.selectMembers()` in a `useMemo([], )`, but they are wrapped in an `if (!mounted) return <Loading/>` early return at the top of the component, so the read happens after mount and they are safe.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Literal `\u2728` (and three sibling escapes) on the Calendar page. Four spots in `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` had unicode-escape strings written as **direct JSX text children** instead of inside a JavaScript expression, so React rendered the six literal characters `\u2728` instead of the ✨ emoji. JSX text is not interpreted as a JavaScript string — only `{`...`}` template literals (or other JS expressions) decode the escape. Fixed lines 507 (`calendar-member-avatar` 👥 in the "All" chip), 607 (`calendar-panel-icon` 📅 in the panel header), 618 (`calendar-empty-icon` ✨ in the "Nothing scheduled" empty state — the one the user reported), and 698 (the 🗑️ delete button label). All four now use the `{`\uXXXX`}` template-literal pattern that the rest of the file already uses (lines 542, 769-777, 798, 808, 838). No other `\uXXXX` JSX-text leaks exist in the codebase.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Liquid Glass 2.2 — matched Consuela card shell. Tuned `.liquid-glass` in `src/app/globals.css` to share the soft glass shell of `.atmospheric-card` (the one used by the "Ask Consuela" card on the Home page), so the Today / Tasks / Daily Schedule rows (and every other `.liquid-glass` instance) feel like part of the same family while keeping their per-row color frost. Added the `0 0 30px rgba(255,255,255,0.03)` soft outer glow that `.atmospheric-card` has. The whisper of top sheen inside the card went from `0.18` → `0.06` in dark mode (matching `.atmospheric-card`) and from `0.45` → `0.10` in light mode — no longer a visible bright line. The `::before` specular layer was reduced to a breath of light at the top (`0.06` dark / `0.12` light) and trimmed from a 3-stop gradient to a 2-stop fade. Light-mode border softened from `0.30` → `0.20`. Hover state matches the new shell: outer glow `0.05`, top sheen `0.10` (dark), no bright white line. Per-row color frosting is unchanged — events still frost mint/violet/amber/cyan/rose/nori, tasks still frost rose/amber/mint, Daily Schedule rows still use their `item.color` — so the color identity of each row is preserved.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Liquid Glass 2.1 — minimized white edge. Reduced the visible white frame around `.liquid-glass` cards in `src/app/globals.css` while keeping the glass depth. The 1px border went from `rgba(255,255,255,0.18)` → `0.06` in dark mode and `0.85` → `0.30` in light mode (no longer a hard white outline). The inner top sheen went from `0.55` → `0.18` in dark and `0.95` → `0.45` in light (a soft gradient, not a bright white line). The `::before` specular highlight was dialed back from `0.32` → `0.14` in dark and `0.55` → `0.28` in light. Removed the `inset 0 0 0 1px` inner ring layer entirely (was the source of a faint second white frame on the inside). Hover state matches: border `0.10` (dark), top sheen `0.28` (dark). The glass effect — backdrop blur `26px saturate(1.6)`, drop shadow lift, frosting tint — is unchanged.
- 2026-06-13 — feat: Liquid Glass 2.0. Strengthened `.liquid-glass` in `src/app/globals.css` so the Today / Tasks / Daily Schedule rows (and the chat quick-actions, recipe cards, pantry items, grocery sections that share the class) actually look like glass. Added a real external drop shadow (`0 8px 24px -8px rgba(0,0,0,0.45) + 0 2px 6px -2px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)`) that lifts the card off the background — the previous version was missing it. Bumped `backdrop-filter` from `blur(20px)` to `blur(26px) saturate(1.6)` so the frost tints read more richly. Added a glossy specular highlight via a `::before` radial gradient (white at 20% 0%, fading to 0 at 60% from the top, `mix-blend-mode: screen` in dark mode). Added a subtle bottom ambient via `::after` so the card reads as a chunk of glass, not a tinted rectangle. Strengthened the inner edge: brighter top inset highlight (0.55 in dark / 0.95 in light), a 1px inner ring (`inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)`) for the "lip of the glass" feel, and a stronger border. Hover now lifts 4px (was 3px) with a deeper drop shadow and brighter inner highlight. Bumped the color frosting on Today rows, Tasks rows, and Daily Schedule rows from `0.32 → 0.16` to `0.40 → 0.20` so the tint sits on the glass more confidently; bumped the Daily Schedule member pill from `0.45 → 0.25` to `0.55 → 0.30` so the pill reads against the stronger glass behind it. Reduced-motion still disables the lift, and the new pseudo-element layers are pointer-events:none so they never block clicks.
- 2026-06-12 — feat: Unified Appearance control (Display Mode + Time of Day merged). The standalone "Display Mode" radio list in Settings → Theme and the separate "Time of Day" segmented control in Settings → Weather are gone. Both are replaced by a single 3-option **Appearance** segmented control in the Weather card: `🌅 Auto` (system + auto, follows the real clock), `☀️ Day` (force light + day), `🌙 Night` (force dark + night). `useWeather.tsx` now resolves `timeOfDay === 'auto'` to the real local day/night before publishing the `__consuelaTod` hint, so the weather widget and the theme read the same clock (eliminates the previous drift where the theme fell back to its own seasonal sunrise/sunset table). `setAppearanceMode` writes both `theme.mode` and `weather.timeOfDay` together. Storage keys (`home-ai-theme-config`, `home-ai-weather-config`) are unchanged, so existing user data migrates automatically: `system + auto` → Auto, `light + day` → Day, `dark + night` → Night; any other combo is normalized to Auto on first render.
- 2026-06-12 — feat: Settings page wider container. `PageShell` in `src/app/settings/page.tsx` now uses `md:max-w-3xl` (768px) on tablet/desktop so the Accent Studio 4-column grids, controls + live preview 2-column layout, season/holiday pickers, and family/emergency rows have room to breathe. Mobile (`<768px`) keeps the original 512px width.

The 2x2 target preview grid tightened to `gap-3`. The preview now flows as a continuation of the Accent Studio widget instead of looking like a nested panel.

Added category filter pills, active counts. All CRUD preserved.

**End of Agent Operational Manual**

Remember: This file + the 2–3 linked deep docs it points to are sufficient for you to give perfect, up-to-date, safe instructions to any user of the Consuela dashboard. When in doubt, re-read this file first.

### Change Log (continued)

- 2026-06-17 — fix(ui): Schedule time picker hour resetting to 12. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — switched from controlled `value` + `onFocus/onBlur` to `defaultValue` + `key` (re-mounts on edit switch) + `type="number"` with proper mobile keyboard. Empty value falls back to current time from state instead of "12". Also removed `onBlur` clamping logic (caused the "resets to 12" bug). TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-17 — feat(infra): Hermes admin tools for dashboard self-management. Added 5 new tools to `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts`: `check_for_update`, `trigger_update`, `get_container_status`, `restart_container`, `check_pocketbase`. Each tool calls the corresponding `/api/admin/*` endpoint via internal HTTP (using `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`). Created `src/app/api/admin/containers/route.ts` (GET — lists Docker containers consuela-dashboard, pocketbase, hermes-agent-2) and `src/app/api/admin/restart/route.ts` (POST — restarts a named container from an allow-list). Updated `src/app/api/hermes/chat/route.ts` system prompts to inform Consuela of her new admin tools and the confirmation-before-action rule. Added `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` to `.env.docker` and `docker-compose.yml`. AGENTS.md §5 added with full admin capabilities documentation (what Consuela can/can't do). TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-18 — feat(db): Full PocketBase migration (all 8 phases). PB is now the primary data source; localStorage is cache/fallback. New collections: `schedules`, `auth_sessions`, `tasks`, `week_data`, `week_archive`, `rewards`, `penalties`, `family_goals`, `hall_of_fame`, `recipes` (all seeded in `pb-seed.ts`). CRUD methods added to `pb-db.ts` for each collection. `db/index.ts` updated with member cache refresh (Phase 2), `CacheRefresher.tsx` global component for 60s/visibility-change PB polling (Phases 3+8). `useGrocery.ts`/`usePantry.ts` flipped to PB-first init (Phase 4). `useMeals.ts`/`useRecipes.ts` wired to PB writes (Phase 5). `task-utils.ts` gains 9 PB sync functions; tasks page syncs structured data every 5s (Phase 6). `useAuth.tsx` persists sessions to PB with boot restore (Phase 7). `selectRecipes`/`upsertRecipe`/`deleteRecipe`/`upsertTask`/`upsertWeekData`/`upsertReward`/`upsertPenalty`/`upsertFamilyGoal`/`insertHallOfFameEntry` pass-through methods added to `db/index.ts`. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean (0 errors), `npm run build` clean.
- 2026-06-18 — feat: Push-local-to-PB bulk upload utility. `src/lib/push-local-to-pb.ts` reads 13 localStorage keys and pushes all existing data to PocketBase via existing upsert/insert methods (grocery, pantry, meals, recipes, events, schedules, emergency contacts, family goal, tasks/leaderboard). Settings → Data & sync gains a "☁️ Push local data to cloud" button with toast feedback showing per-collection push counts. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): Emergency endpoint now requires PIN authentication. `src/app/api/emergency/route.ts` — POST requests must include `pin` in the JSON body or `x-emergency-pin` header. PIN is verified against any family member's stored PIN. `src/components/ui/EmergencyButton.tsx` updated to send the logged-in user's PIN from auth context. `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS` env var available for testing. Prevents unauthorized SMS/email blasts to emergency contacts.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): PIN no longer persisted to localStorage. `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx` — `login()` and `handleMembersUpdated` now write only `id`, `name`, `role`, `emoji`, `color`, `avatarSize`, `glow` to `consuela-auth-user`. The 4-digit PIN is kept in memory only (as `AuthUser.pin`) for the active session. This prevents PIN exposure via browser devtools or localStorage inspection.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): Removed real PII from fallback data. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — `emergencyFallback` phone numbers and emails replaced with placeholders (`+15551234567`, `parent1@example.com`). Fallback data is only used when PocketBase is unreachable.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(db): Meal/recipe PB writes now stringify array fields. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — `insertMeal`, `updateMeal`, and `upsertRecipe` now `JSON.stringify()` `ingredients` and `tags` arrays before writing to PocketBase. Previously, PB silently stored `[object Object]` or dropped the field, causing `selectMeals` JSON.parse to fail on reload.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(db): `deleteExpiredAuthSessions` now uses PB server-side filter instead of fetching all records. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — Uses `filter='lastActiveAt < "${cutoff}"'` to let PocketBase handle the filtering, reducing data transfer from O(n) to O(expired).
- 2026-07-16 — fix(tasks): Duplicate task completion guard. `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — `submitPin` now checks `task.completedInWeek === weekKey()` before allowing completion, preventing the same task from being completed twice in one week even if two devices sync simultaneously.
- 2026-07-17 — feat(ui): Apple-glass material consolidation (Stream A). `src/app/globals.css` — consolidated glass variants into 3 Apple-style materials: `.material-thin` (12px blur, saturate 1.2), `.material-regular` (20px blur, saturate 1.4), `.material-thick` (26px blur, saturate 1.6). Each carries a specular top-light via `::before` (radial white gradient, `mix-blend-mode: screen`) — the Apple "liquid glass" signature. Aliases `.glass` → material-regular, `.glass-subtle` → material-thin, `.glass-strong` → material-thick now share the same selector groups (fixed Tailwind v4 `@apply` incompatibility). Removed neumorphic `.neu-raised`, `.neu-pressed`, `.neu-flat` classes and `--neu-*` variables; `.soft-press` now uses inset shadow instead of `var(--neu-pressed)`. Light-mode + `prefers-color-scheme` overrides added for all 3 materials (fixes old near-white dark-mode bug via alias to material-thick). `[data-contrast="boost"]` rule now thickens borders + brightens border color instead of dimming all UI to 0.7 opacity. `src/components/ui/Surface.tsx` — removed `neu`/`neu-pressed`/`flat` variants, added `material-regular`/`material-thick`; `flat` maps to `material-regular` with surface bg. `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx` — modal now uses `material-thick` (translucent `surface-0/80` + `backdrop-blur-2xl`) with spring entrance via `modalEnter` keyframe (cubic-bezier overshoot). `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` — neutral tone uses `surface-0/80`; slide-down spring entrance via `toastSlide` keyframe. `src/components/ui/SoftButton.tsx` / `IconButton.tsx` / `Chip.tsx` / `ListRow.tsx` / `Surface.tsx` — unified press scale to `active:scale-[0.97]`. Added `modalEnter` + `toastSlide` keyframes to `globals.css`. `src/app/design-system/page.tsx` — demo cards updated from `neu`/`neu-pressed` to `material-regular`/`material-thick`. TS / lint / build clean.

New state `weeklyPlanLoading`/`weeklyPlanError` returned. `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx` — "✨ Generate" button in the week nav header (between Archive and next-week arrow); disabled + "Generating..." while running; inline error pill on failure; success toast via new `showToast` prop (passed from `page.tsx`). `src/app/meals/page.tsx` — destructured + passed `generateWeeklyPlan`/`weeklyPlanLoading`/`weeklyPlanError`/`showToast` to MealsTab. Two-week planning workflow: plan this week's real meals, tap › to next week, Generate for a full AI-planned blank week. TS / lint / build clean.

- 2026-09-03 — fix(sync): cross-device auto-update for grocery/pantry/tasks/calendar + signed-out banner + store-pill persistence. Root cause (user-reported "edits on one browser never appear on another"): every browser holds its own localStorage cache + session, and only useMeals/useRecipes re-merged the 60s `consuela-data-refreshed` cycle — grocery/pantry/tasks/calendar sat stale until manual reload, while a signed-out browser silently showed its old copy. Fixes (TDD, 8 new tests): `useGrocery.ts`/`usePantry.ts` mount loads extracted to `pullGrocery`/`pullPantry` + refresh listeners (same pattern as useMeals); `tasks/page.tsx` snapshot restore extracted to `restoreFromSnapshot` + refresh re-pull (tasks merge now returns prev when nothing is new, so steady-state refreshes never trigger the debounced push-back); `calendar/page.tsx` family-events pull extracted to `pullFamilyEvents` + refresh listener; NEW `SyncStatusBanner.tsx` (🔐 "Signed out — showing your saved copy…", amber, `role="status"`, `data-testid="sync-status-banner"`) rendered by `PageShell` (covers Home/Meals/Tasks/Calendar/Settings; chat keeps its own shell); `updateGroceryItem` now accepts partial updates (`{store}`-only pills from ShopTab/price-compare no longer crash on `updates.name.trim()`) and `mapDbToGrocery` carries `store` (was dropped on read). Verified: suite 914/914, tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files (1 pre-existing calendar set-state-in-effect untouched). Behavior contract: signed-in screens merge another device's changes within ~60s (or on tab-wake/login) with no reload; signed-out screens announce it instead of looking synced.

- 2026-09-15 — feat(tasks,leaderboard): Weekly Prize Race — parent-set podium prizes + race-gap lines + Monday win ceremony + week-vs-all-time labeling (12-task feature, docs final wave). `weekly_prizes` PB collection (UNIQUE `idx_weekly_prizes_rank`, parent-write gateway) + LWW localStorage mirror (`consuela-weekly-prizes` + stamp); `hall_of_fame` gains `prize`/`celebrated`; rollover's `archiveWeekWinner` enshrines the TOP 3 with frozen prize text (idempotent per member+weekStart, hall keeps the latest 12 WEEKS, zero-point weeks record nothing). Settings gains the parent-only 🏆 Weekly prizes card (footer "Winners are locked in when the week resets Monday."). Tasks Leaderboard gains `PrizeRaceCard` (competition-rank holders + "Up for grabs", ⏳ countdown, personal gap line targeting the LOWEST configured prize rank — two prizes ⇒ gap chases #2, pinned by test: rank-4 at 10 pts vs #2 at 60 reads "50 pts"); `Podium` gains 🎁 ribbons (points > 0 only) + 10px "{N} all-time"; `YourCard` "· {N} all-time" (text-xs); "Earned this week" StatTile with all-time detail; KidHome hero all-time caption (ledger-resolved name). NEW `WeeklyWinModal` ceremony on Home/Tasks/KidHome (never the wall profile) + session-gated `POST /api/hall-of-fame/celebrate` (child/pet sessions claim ONLY their own win — the gateway's parent-only hall writes made that impossible otherwise; 404 no prized win; idempotent re-claim) — single-fire across devices; both buttons + scrim + Esc all claim (local flag first, best-effort POST). Weekly Champ 🥇 badge finally earnable (any rank-1 hall entry). Home widget one-line race footer (30-char prize clip, `line-clamp-1`) + KidHome positive-only race line. No new keyframes — the ceremony reuses the existing confetti and is reduced-motion guarded. Final-wave minors folded in: footer copy fix, YourCard 10px→xs, tasks-page comment dedent, +3 tests (card edit round-trip, two-prize gap, rank-1 tie ribbons), pb-seed index assertion. Verified: full suite **2095/2095 (290 files)**, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean; `npm run pb:seed` applied the rank index live and re-runs idempotently (`weekly_prizes (already exists)`). **Ops on deploy: `npm run pb:seed`.** Spec/plan `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/2026-09-15-weekly-prize-race*` (outer repo); full contracts in the 2026-09-15 UI Change Record.

# This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (resolved from this file's directory; in monorepos the `next` package may not be visible from the repo root) before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.

This block is written and re-added by `next dev` — verify at `node_modules/next/dist/server/lib/generate-agent-files.js`. Removing it from a diff only re-creates the uncommitted change; committing it with your work keeps the tree clean.

## UI Change Records (legacy — formerly the Appendices dated block)

- 2026-09-20 — fix(chat): Ask Consuela thread stays visible while thinking + streams survive tab navigation (zero backend changes). Two root causes fixed. (1) `showHero` treated "first user message + isTyping" as a hero state, swapping the whole thread (the user's own bubble included) for a 200px "Thinking…" orb until the first content token — with buffered tool rounds that could be minutes. Now `showHero = !hasRealMessages`; the typing-dots bubble (with the tool `statusLine`) is the thinking affordance under the last message; the hero orb branch is deleted. (2) Chat state was component-local and every tab navigation unmounts /chat, so an in-flight stream's updates landed on a dead component — the reply only appeared after a remount refetched today's PB thread (the "if I change pages then it shows" report). NEW `src/lib/chat-store.ts` (module singleton + `useSyncExternalStore`, same idiom as `calendar-member-snapshot.ts`) owns messages + the live stream (AbortController, watermark, hydration); navigating away interrupts nothing and returning re-attaches instantly. `ensureHydrated()` runs once per page load; remounts do an incremental `since` reconcile; localStorage persistence moved into the store; the page keeps only UI-local state. NEW `tests/unit/chat-store.test.ts` (7) + 2 page regressions + reset seam in all four chat page suites. Verified: chat suites 58/58, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2362 passed (323 files; the 2 uncaught teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all` are pre-existing — reproduce in isolation), `next build` clean. Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- 2026-09-20 — fix(tasks,pb,hall): guest completions + week champions. (1) /api/tasks/claim + /api/members/verify middleware-exempt (PIN is the credential); NEW server-authoritative `complete`/`undo` actions on the claim route (idempotent, reversal-aware, kid completions land pendingApproval); Tasks page assigned-completion + undo persist through them. (2) hall_of_fame.emoji 5000-char cap silently rejected every photo-avatar enshrinement — seed now declares max 400000 (self-healed live) + NEW hall-of-fame-backfill module recomputes champions from week_archive inside GET /api/tasks/sync (idempotent, celebration-safe, older weeks pre-celebrated). Live-verified: guest complete → 200 + snapshot + week_data; Aurora's 2026-09-07 win enshrined with prize text; June week recovered as history. Ops: pb:seed run (emoji heal landed).

- 2026-09-19 — feat(tasks): Open & Crew Tasks — full plan Tasks 2–16 (the earlier partial landing `6a6db2f` had only the crew fields). Aligned the model to the approved spec (`crew: { members:[{name,emoji,joinedAt,checkedInAt?}], removed? }`, `pendingApproval.crew`), fixed the PB `0`-coercion merge churn, added crew helpers + union merge with tombstones, crew-join/check-in/remove server actions (keyed lock; set-once check-ins; last-slot 409; PIN-free session path for under-10s), open-task speed bonus, crew approval paying each member full points (taskId+member idempotency, reversal-aware), the Assigned/Open/Crew modal + Open board on the Tasks page, parent crew management, KidHome crew join/check-in, and open/crew AI suggestions (validator + mapper). - 2026-09-18 — fix(shop): mobile grocery rows squeezed product names to ~10px — actions collapsed into a ⋯ overflow sheet. Root cause (measured live at 390×844): ListRow's title (`min-w-0 flex-1 truncate`) always yields to the `shrink-0` trailing cluster (StorePill 40-47px + priority Chip ~62px + 44px lock/edit/delete + gaps ≈ 256-294px), leaving ~10px for the name on phones — emoji visible, name gone. Progressive regression (store pills 2026-08-31; 44px targets earlier); desktop (xl `1fr_280px` grid) unaffected. Fix (commits 8324694, 9eadb5a, 089be56, 6bb1db6, 1293c65): NEW `src/components/meals/GroceryItemActionsSheet.tsx` (shared-Modal sheet — 🥫 Send to pantry (checked-only, `pantryBusy`-gated) / 📌 Lock/Unlock auto-sync (flips on `manualOverride`) / ✎ Edit details / 🗑️ Delete (rose)) + `ShopTab.tsx` trailing restructure (StorePill visible at ALL sizes; Pantry + Chip + lock + edit + delete UNCHANGED inside `hidden sm:flex`; `sm:hidden` ⋯ with `aria-label="More actions for {name}"`; split `actionsItemId`/`actionsSheetItem` state keeps the sheet's item alive through the 150ms Modal exit; checkbox wrapper + ⋯ `h-11`→`h-9` + `.hit-44` = 36px visual / 44px effective); priority Chip phones-hidden BY USER DECISION (not in the sheet). Verified: probe `scripts/consuela/verify-shop-mobile-actions.mjs` **34/34 ALL CHECKS PASSED** (390×844 + 1280×800, dark + light, guest — titles ≥140px, measured 141/148/148px; full names; no overflow; sheet flows; desktop untouched), NEW suites `grocery-item-actions-sheet` (5) + `shop-tab-mobile-actions` (5), `grocery-handoff`/`grocery-override-ui` green, tsc clean, eslint 0 on the 4 touched files, full suite 2239 passed / 2 failed (308 files — only the 2 documented pre-existing TZ-streak failures; the initially-reported full-suite-only `shop-tab-mobile-actions` failures were a harness race — the sheet's 150ms Modal closing timer firing into a `document.body` wiped by the next test — resolved by unmounting the test root in `afterEach`, which clears the timer before the wipe). Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (no PB schema/migration).

- 2026-09-18 — fix(chat): Ask Consuela thread ordering + clean new conversation. Root causes: (1) the mount PB hydration did a blind `setMessages(pbMsgs)` replace, so a send that raced the initial fetch was dropped and re-appended at the end by the post-send reconcile → reply above request; (2) `/new` appended a divider but the render mapped the full `messages` array, so history never cleared; (3) `mergePBThread` keyed dedupe on `role:speaker:content`, leaving a duplicate for signed-out rows. Fix (TDD): NEW pure `src/lib/chat-thread.ts` (`threadKey` role+content; `sortThread` by `Message.at` with user→divider→reply ties; additive `mergeThread`; `visibleThread` slices at the newest `system` marker and drops the seed greeting once a user row exists); both hydration effects merge instead of replace; `visibleMessages` drives the thread map + hero/quick-actions/read-aloud; `showHero` hands over once any real message exists; `selectChatMessages` sorts `"createdAt,id"`; confirm copy + journey updated. Tests: NEW `chat-thread` (18) + delayed-hydration ordering page test; chat suites 42/42; full suite 2228 passed / 3 failed (all 3 stash-proven pre-existing on HEAD: TZ-streak ×2 + weather-strip ×1); tsc clean; eslint 0 on touched files. Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (no PB schema/migration).

- 2026-09-18 — fix(home): Home "Today" widget never merged Google Calendar events. Root cause: `src/app/page.tsx` `refreshTodayEvents` read only the family `events` collection via `db.selectTodaysEvents()`; Google rows (`consuela_google_calendar_events`) were merged only on the Calendar page / chat tools / planner / screensaver (the 2026-09-15 multi-day fix's scope), so Google-only events like today's all-day "Bailey & Emily at home!" never appeared on Home. Fix (TDD red→green): the widget now fetches `/api/google-calendar` and merges rows covering today via the shared `googleEventCoversDay` contract + `mapGoogleEvent` (per-calendar `colorHex` honored by `eventLineColor`; all-day rows first, then by time; Events stat tile inherits the count; fetch failure/401 gracefully keeps family rows). Tests: NEW `tests/unit/home-today-google-events.test.tsx` (4). Verified: full suite 2205/2207 (2 failures = documented pre-existing TZ-streak, stash-proven), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean, live Playwright probes 11/11 (guest fallback, signed-in payload contains the Bailey row, real-browser render of the card rows + counts + ended-yesterday exclusion). Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (deploy picks it up; no pb:seed).

Tests: `muse-authorize` live-semantics case table + `muse-tools-route` +2 cross-cases; `muse-*` suites 65/65. No schema/ops change.

(2) Kid write-tool gates: `/api/consuela/suggestions/act` 403 `adult_only` for child/pet PINs on the 6 write tools (reads + `dismiss_suggestion` still any-member); the Clem branch of `/api/hermes/chat` now passes the session-derived `role` to `buildToolsForOpenAI` — child/pet/guest Clem sessions arm only the 4 read grocery tools (previously the CLEM filter bypassed the kid allowlist). (3) NEW `keyed-lock.ts` generic in-process mutex + `week-ledger-lock.ts` domain name; claim + redeem run their whole ledger section under it (orchestrated races red-proved double-success + silent tx erasure). (4) Both alert crons: keyed lock around load→decide→push→save + NEW `ha/alert-state.ts` CAS save (expected-prev, the setState idiom) — overlapping runs pushed every alert twice before. (5) hall_of_fame: UNIQUE `(member, weekStart)` index in pb-seed + `dedupeHallOfFameRows` pre-index heal (keeps celebrated, else earliest) + `insertHallOfFameEntry` upsert that never loses `celebrated:true`. (6) `mergeTasksSnapshot` dedupes within the snapshot (id AND title). (7) `/api/tasks/sync` non-parent leg-merge under the keyed lock — the stale parent-legs resurrection race is closed. Verified: typecheck clean, full suite 796/796, eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-09-01 — fix(composio,instacart): Composio key stored but unreadable — honest connection dots + Instacart order flow fixed. **Root cause (4 compounding bugs):** (1) the Composio key Jeffery saved to PB on 2026-08-29 was encrypted with a CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY that matches neither local dev nor the live NAS container (container was recreated 2026-08-31 with a different key) → `decryptSecret` silently failed → env fallback empty → route returned honest 503 "Instacart integration is not enabled." (2) `createShoppingListViaComposio` in `src/lib/instacart.ts` parsed the wrong response shape — real v3.1 returns `{"data":{"url":...}}` but code looked for `data.result.output.shopping_list_url` → even with a valid key the function threw "Composio did not return a shopping list URL." (3) The Composio Test button in Settings probed a dead v1 endpoint (`/api/v1/actions` → 410 Gone) which 410 < 500 → the old `bearerReachable` treated 410 as "reachable" → **green dot on an invalid/missing key**. (4) The local `.env.local` and NAS container had different `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` values (split-brain) — any secret saved from either environment was unreadable by the other. **Fixes (TDD):** `src/lib/instacart.ts` — `createShoppingListViaComposio` now parses the real v3.1 shape `data?.data?.url` (with legacy fallback) + surfaces Composio's `error.message` on `successful:false`; `src/lib/services/tests.ts` — new dedicated `testComposio()` probing `v3.1/tools?limit=1` (200=green, 401/403/410=red, not green); `src/lib/services/config.ts` — `ServiceFieldStatus` gains `unreadable?: boolean`; `readStoredRow` refactored to `readStoredRowDetailed` returning `{value, raw, unreadable}` so `getServiceStatus` can surface a DB row that exists but can't be decrypted; `src/components/settings/ServicesKeysCard.tsx` — `dotTone` shows "Saved — unreadable (re-enter)" for broken secrets, auto-runs all service tests on card mount (progressive dot updates via `Promise.allSettled`), "Test all" button to refresh, unreadable fields show a rose warning + "Re-enter key" placeholder. **Ops:** re-stored the Composio key via the NAS dashboard PUT (encrypted with the container's correct key); aligned local `.env.local` CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY to match the NAS container's key (prevents future split-brain for the only two encrypted-service rows: composio + any future secrets). **Verified live:** composio test `ok=true key accepted (200)`, Instacart POST returns `https://customers.dev.instacart.tools/store/shopping_lists/19267256...`. New tests: `tests/unit/services-composio-test.test.ts` (5: v3.1 endpoint, 401=red, 410=red, 2xx=green, short-circuit); `tests/unit/composio-instacart.test.ts` (+3: v3.1 `data.url` shape, legacy compat, error surface, `successful:false`); `tests/unit/services-config-resolver.test.ts` (+2: unreadable without env, unreadable with env). Typecheck clean, 702/703 suite (1 pre-existing hermes port test from uncommitted work), eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-31 — fix(profile): photo avatars couldn't save — PocketBase `members.emoji` capped at 5000 chars. Root cause: this PB build treats a text field's `max: 0` as the built-in 5000-char default, so every real photo save (a 256px webp data URL, ~8–60KB base64) hit PB `validation_max_text_constraint` → the profile route 500'd `Failed to update profile`. `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — `members.emoji` schema now defines `options: { max: 400000 }` (was no options → PB applied its 5000-char default even though the field schema reported `max: 0`), and `seedCollections()` now heals **field-option drift** for text fields the seed gives an explicit `max` — existing members fields with `emoji.max` below 400000 are patched on re-seed; the live NAS PB was healed via `npm run pb:seed` (`members (patched +1 field options (emoji))`). `src/app/api/members/profile/route.ts` — `MAX_AVATAR_CHARS` now exported (same 400_000 value) so the route and seed caps stay in lockstep (guard-tested). New `pb_migrations/1788223651_updated_members.js` (emoji.max 0→400000, revert included). New `tests/unit/pb-seed-field-heal.test.ts` (4 tests: schema carries 400000, seed heals a live 0-max field, already-correct field untouched, route cap == seed max). Root cause verified live: a 2,887-char data URL saved, 5,000+ returned 500; after the fix 250,000-char payloads return 200 and a real noisy photo (50,751-char webp) saves end-to-end with `Saved ✓` and persists to PB. Suite 690/690 (was 661 + 4 new + 25 from other work), tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-31 — feat(weather): widget stays live — 15-min auto-refresh + tab-wake refresh (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-weather-live-refresh-design.md). `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — mount-only fetch extracted into `loadWeather()` (`useCallback` on lat/lon, `inFlightRef` overlap guard) and now also runs every 15 min (`setInterval`) and on `visibilitychange` when the tab returns visible with `updatedAt` > 10 min old (`updatedAtRef` mirrors the success timestamp). Silent refresh: `loading` only ever shows before first data; background failure keeps stale data with no banner (`.catch` gated on `dataLoadedRef`) and retries next cycle. The mount effect resets `dataLoadedRef`/`updatedAtRef` when the weather location changes, so a new location's failed first fetch shows the banner (no silent old-city data). Modal auto-live via shared `weatherData`. `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` +7 (poll, stale/fresh wake, silent in-flight swap, failure-keeps-data, location-change banner reset, overlap guard). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, suite 661/661.

- 2026-08-31 — fix(tasks): guest "vanishing tasks" made honest — sign-in empty state replaces the silent empty list. Investigation (live curl + Playwright against the NAS) proved PocketBase never lost the data — all 7 chores created 17:30–17:41 UTC persisted in `tasks` + `consuela_data_snapshots`; the vanishing was the read path: signed-out (incognito/fresh) browsers get 401 on every sessioned read (`/api/tasks/sync`, `/api/db/*`), so the Tasks page fell back to empty local cache and rendered "🎉 All caught up / No pending tasks right now" — indistinguishable from a genuinely empty chore list. Fix (TDD): `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — the mount restore records `guestSyncBlocked` when the snapshot read 401s, and the Pending empty state swaps to an honest 🔐 EmptyState ("Tasks are synced to the family account / Sign in with your PIN to see everyone's tasks. Your chores aren't gone — they're waiting on the family server.") when `!isLoggedIn && guestSyncBlocked && tasks.length === 0`; signed-in empty lists and guests with local tasks keep the existing copy. New `tests/unit/tasks-guest-sync-state.test.tsx` (3 tests: guest-401 shows the hint, signed-in-empty keeps "All caught up", local tasks render without the hint). Verified: suite 693/693, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files.
- 2026-08-31 — fix(ops,auth): NAS deploy un-broken — Clem chat + TheMealDB recipe search/save. Three root causes (systematic debugging): (1) container on wrong Docker network (`bridge` instead of `familydashboard_consuela-net`) → `hermes-agent-2` unresolved (Clem chat dead) + `192.168.0.28:8090` unreachable (`ConnectTimeoutError` in container logs → recipe search 502, gateway writes 502); (2) Secure session cookie over plain HTTP (`secure: NODE_ENV === "production"` in the login route) → browsers never sent the cookie on http://192.168.0.28:3000 → every gated write 401'd ("can't save recipes"); (3) my raw-curl recipe POST bypassed `db.upsertRecipe`'s array JSON-stringify — not a bug (UI path verified stringifies). Fixes: recreated container on the shared network with `NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL=http://pocketbase:8090`, `HERMES_API_URL=http://hermes-agent-2:8642`, `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false`; code change `0a8a75c` adds exported `sessionCookieSecure()` honoring `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false` (+3 tests, suite 659/659) and `docker-compose.yml` gained `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=${…:-false}`. `DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` gotchas #11–13. Verified live: PB + Hermes reachable from the container; login sets a non-Secure cookie; gateway recipe save 200 and persists full ingredients/tags; Playwright E2E (signed in) — 25 chicken search results, detail + Save to catalog lands in PB, Clem FAB opens and Hermes replies.

- 2026-08-26 — fix(meals): /meals member-strip hydration mismatch (server PB-warmed members cache vs client fallback) + signed-in custom avatars now actually land in the strip. **Root cause (systematic):** the "Hydrate once" IIFE in `src/db/index.ts` warms `membersCache` at module load — on the server it reads PB directly (admin access), so SSR of the PlanTab "Who's eating tonight" strip rendered the real PB members (live PB stores Jeffery's customized emoji 😎), while the client's first render still had an empty cache (the browser roster fetch `/api/members/admin` is async and 401s for guests) and rendered the static fallback (Jeffery 👨 from `src/lib/member-fallback.ts`) → React hydration error `+ 👨 / - 😎` at `AnimatedEmoji`. The 2026-06-13 `mounted` gate only protected `selectMembersDetailed()`; the pre-mount branch still read `db.selectMembers()`, which stopped being server/client-deterministic when the PB warm-up landed. **Fix:** (1) `src/db/index.ts` — new `mappedMembersFallback()` + `db.selectMembersFallback()` (the exact empty-cache mapping, but NEVER reflects the warmed cache); `selectMembers()` reuses it. (2) `src/components/meals/PlanTab.tsx` — pre-mount branch now reads `db.selectMembersFallback()`, so SSR HTML and client hydration are byte-identical (deterministic fallbacks; real roster swaps in after mount). (3) `src/db/index.ts` IIFE now dispatches `consuela-members-updated` on the client after a **successful** roster fetch only (a fallback-only cache must not fire it — `useAuth.handleMembersUpdated` treats a missing member as deleted and signs out; `refreshMembersCache` already follows this rule). (4) `PlanTab` listens for the event and bumps a `membersVersion` recompute trigger on the `familyMembers` memo — previously the `[mounted]`-only memo froze at whatever the cache held the moment mount flipped, so signed-in users never saw their custom avatars in the strip (the roster lands ~100ms later). **Tests (+3, TDD red→green):** `tests/unit/plan-tab-hydration.test.tsx` (node env — mocks `withAdmin` so the server-path hydrate warms the cache with the live PB roster incl. 😎; asserts `selectMembersFallback()` stays deterministic and `renderToString(<PlanTab/>)` contains 👨 and NOT 😎) and `tests/unit/plan-tab-members-swap.test.tsx` (jsdom — gates the roster fetch, asserts fallbacks first then 😎 after `consuela-members-updated`, pets excluded). **Verified:** typecheck clean; suite 592/592 (79 files); eslint 0 on touched files; Playwright guest + signed-in (Rebecca) at 390px — 0 hydration console errors on `/meals` and all three legacy deep links (`?tab=grocery/pantry/recipes` still land on Shop/Stock/Recipe box), signed-in strip shows Jeffery's real 😎 after mount, guest strip shows the honest fallback family. Note: the 2 remaining adult console 403s are the pre-existing `useAuth` legacy direct-PB `auth_sessions` reads (documented fail-soft under PB lockdown) — unrelated.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(kitchen): Kitchen redesigned as one Plan → Shop → Stock loop (plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-26-kitchen-plan-shop-stock.md, commits 79508b3..ed4e204). `/meals` rewired from four parallel tabs to a three-step loop — 🍽️ Plan / 🛒 Shop / 🥫 Stock in one `SegmentedControl` radiogroup with a shared collapsible `KitchenFlowCard` stepper (`aria-current="step"` on the active step; collapse persisted to localStorage). New: `KitchenFlowCard.tsx`, `SyncPreviewSheet.tsx` (preview-before-commit modal), `src/lib/kitchen-tabs.ts` (legacy `?tab=` deep-link mapping: grocery→Shop, pantry→Stock, recipes→Plan + Recipe box, meals→Plan), pure `MealSyncService.previewMealPlanToGrocery` / `previewPantryToGrocery`. Renames: MealsTab→PlanTab (inline Recipe box, ghost-member fix, direct sync button removed), RecipesTab→RecipeBox (folded into Plan), GroceryTab→ShopTab (Buy-again row, preview-first meal sync, 44px targets), PantryTab→StockTab (preview-first low/out sync, declutter, async add fix). Backend/hooks/sync engine unchanged. New tests: kitchen-tabs (4), kitchen-flow-card (4), meal-sync-preview (8), sync-preview-sheet (4); grocery-handoff + grocery-override-ui re-pointed at ShopTab. Task 10 verification gate: typecheck clean; lint 0 errors on plan-touched files (KitchenFlowCard `set-state-in-effect` fixed via `useSyncExternalStore` mounted pattern — repo-wide 48 pre-existing errors remain, none plan-touched); suite 589/589 (78 files); build clean; Playwright guest 390px loop 9/9 (deep links, custom meal → preview → Shop list, check-off → bulk send → undo, pantry Low → preview → Shop list, no horizontal overflow). - 2026-08-26 — fix(kitchen): grocery→pantry handoff works for guests + expired sessions — local-first kitchen writes. Root cause (systematic): the 2026-08-24 session-auth migration gated `/api/db/*` behind the httpOnly `consuela_session` cookie, but `usePantry`/`useGrocery` still gated UI state on PB write results — for guests (and expired sessions) every write 401'd, `addPantryItem` returned false, and `GroceryTab.sendCheckedToPantry` counted all items as failed and kept the checked rows stuck ("Sent 0 to pantry (0 already stocked, 5 failed — kept on list)"). Compounding: live PB `grocery_list_items.userId` was `required: true` (legacy migration field, absent from the seed) while the client create paths never sent it — signed-in grocery creates 400'd at PB too. Fixes: `src/hooks/usePantry.ts` — `addPantryItem` is now local-first (PB write attempted best-effort in try/catch; the `PantryItem` is built from local inputs with `saved?.id ?? Date.now()`, state always updates, item always returned); `updatePantryStatus`/`removePantryItem` state-first; `normalizeName(p.item || "")` guards; PB rows lacking both `name` and `item` filtered on load. `src/hooks/useGrocery.ts` — `addGroceryItem` wraps the upsert in try/catch (local append unchanged); `toggleGroceryNeeded`/`updateGroceryItem`/`toggleManualOverride` optimistic state then best-effort write; `deleteGroceryItem` state-first and passes `item.name` — `src/db/index.ts` `deleteGroceryItem(id, name?)` falls back to gateway list + delete-by-name when the id delete fails (local numeric fallback ids don't exist in PB; the rows a toggle materializes are now cleaned up instead of resurrecting). `src/db/index.ts` + `src/db/pb-db.ts` — grocery create defaults `{ userId: "demo", ...data }`; `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` chat grocery create sends `userId: "demo"`; `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — `grocery_list_items` seed gains optional `userId`. Live PB patched via admin API: `userId.required` → false; all test/probe rows removed (grocery 0, pantry 0). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 2 pre-existing unused-disable warnings; suite 569/569; Playwright guest scenario (all writes 401) — toast "🥫 Sent 5 items to pantry", checked rows clear, pantry tab shows the items from local state; signed-in scenario — 5× POST pantry 200, numeric-id deletes 502 then name-fallback deletes 200, PB grocery ends 0 rows / pantry 5.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(calendar): Google-synced events now month-accurate (no ghost events, no dropped months). Root cause: `syncGoogleEvents` in `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` mapped Google events to day-of-month only (`day: dayNum`) AND filtered them to the current month at sync time, while the grid/panels keyed events by day number for every displayed month — so current-month Google events ghosted onto the same day numbers in all other months, and next-month events never appeared when navigated to. Backend audited clean (`src/lib/google/calendar.ts` stores real `start_iso` across a 30d-back/90d-forward window; `/api/google-calendar` returns all cached rows). Fix: NEW `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` — `parseGoogleStart` (all-day date-only parsed as local dates, preserving the existing US-timezone shift fix), `mapGoogleEvent` (keeps day+month+year; id `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month}_{year}_{time}`), `eventInMonth` (no month/year = repeats every month, preserving family-event behavior). `CalEvent` gained optional month/year; sync keeps the full window (no current-month filter), dedupe includes month/year; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay` filter via `eventInMonth`. Tests: NEW `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (9, TDD red→green). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 569/569; Playwright probe with mocked sync (Aug 12/28 + Sep 3/12 events) — August shows 12+28, September shows 3+12 with day 28 clean, July shows no Google ghosts, family repeats intact, no page errors.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar ⇄ Schedule tabs — solid accent active pill. `src/app/globals.css` — `.calendar-tab.is-active` was a 24% accent wash + primary text that blended into the glass track (hard to tell which tab was highlighted); now a solid accent gradient (`--color-accent-button` → 76% accent mixed with `#111827`), white text, inset top highlight, accent glow shadow via `color-mix`; added `.calendar-tab.is-active:hover` so hover doesn't dim the white label; inactive tabs unchanged (muted text, transparent). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms solid gradient + white text in dark and light and the active pill follows the tap.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): selected-today keeps both signals — clear translucent box + today circle. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — day-cell className was an either/or ternary (`is-selected` XOR `is-today`), so today's marker vanished whenever today was selected; now independent flags (`is-selected` AND `is-today`). `src/app/globals.css` — today circle un-scoped from `:not(.is-selected)` (always renders on today); selected today gains a 2px white ring on the circle; new `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected.is-today` swaps the dense gradient for a clear translucent accent box (20% fill dark / 22% light via `color-mix`, 60%+white accent border, inset white ring, softer glow, scale 1.06 kept) with matching overrides in both light blocks (`:root[data-theme="light"] .is-today` outranks the base rule); event dots in selected-today use accent color to stay visible on the tint. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms both classes + solid circle + translucent fill (no gradient) when today is selected, and circle-on-today + gradient-on-selection when another day is selected, in dark and light.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(ui): console hygiene — theme script via next/script + Calendar hydration mismatch fixed. `src/app/layout.tsx` — anti-FOUC theme-init script moved from raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` (React 19 dev warning "Encountered a script tag while rendering React component") to `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` (Next.js 16 app-router pattern: injected into server HTML head, runs pre-hydration; verified data-theme set at domcontentloaded in dark + light). `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — fixed "Hydration failed… server rendered HTML didn't match the client" in the month-grid day dots: `calEvents`/`schedules` lazy-init from localStorage on the client (stored events) while the server rendered fallbacks, so dot counts/event rows differed at hydration. Added a `mounted` flag via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeNoop, clientTrue, serverFalse)` (no effect/setState — the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule flags the classic mounted-effect when the flag feeds memo cascades) + `renderEvents`/`renderSchedules` memos serving deterministic fallbacks until mount; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay`/`sortedSchedules` + routines count/empty-state render sites read the gated values; state/CRUD/write-back effects unchanged. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint back to the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect only; suite 560/560; Playwright probes — zero hydration/script-tag console errors in dark + light, including a repro with stored events differing from fallbacks (dots render correctly after mount); detector findings unchanged (8 pre-existing).
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar month grid adopts Apple Calendar signatures. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — grid header restructured: month title left + `.calendar-month-nav` group (prev chevron ‹ Today pill › next chevron); today's date number gains `is-today-number`. `src/app/globals.css` — today's number renders as a 23px filled accent circle (white numeral + accent-matched glow, scoped `:not(.is-selected)`); `.is-today` tile tint softened (14%→8% dark / 12%→7% light) and re-derived from `--color-accent-selected` via `color-mix` (was `--calendar-accent-rgb` rgba that could mismatch the circle under non-blue accents); `.calendar-month-title` 1.05rem/800 → 1.3rem/600 with muted year; `.calendar-today-btn` full 999px pill; new ghost chevrons (borderless round 36px, dark + light hover); `.calendar-day-dot` 3.2px → 4.2px. Verified: typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes confirm circle (23.2px, 999px radius, accent fill, matching glow) + nav order + pill radius + dot size in dark and light, no page errors, no overflow.

Verified: Home `.widget-card` vs Calendar grid card computed styles byte-identical in dark and light; typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes clean (no page errors, no horizontal overflow at 390/1440, both tabs + open form).
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): audit follow-up — regression tests for input route guards + dead demo cleanup. Added `tests/unit/input-route-guards.test.ts` (5 tests: OCR/voice/photo multipart guards, email-forward validation, email-forward `members` lookup + missing `consuela_saved_locations` fallback); red-green verified by stashing the route fixes (4 failures) and restoring them (5 passes). Deleted dead phantom-collection demo modules: `src/lib/family-ai.ts`, `src/lib/hermes-tools-enhanced.ts`, `src/lib/morning-briefing.ts`, old `src/hooks/useMorningBriefing.ts`, `src/components/morning-briefing/`, `src/components/family-ai/`. Fixed `src/db/features/seed.ts` `users`→`members`. Verified suite 560/560, `tsc` clean, eslint clean on touched files, live route probes unchanged.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): comprehensive backend audit — gateway unblocked (autodate fields), unauthenticated-PB reads fixed, dead/dangerous routes removed. Full sweep of all 78 API routes (safe probes: empty bodies / missing auth / fake ids; side-effect routes avoided) surfaced a second class of breakage. **(1) Gateway unblocked — the biggest win:** NO PB collection had the standard `created`/`updated` autodate fields, but the sessioned gateway `/api/db/[collection]` defaults to `sort=-created`, so EVERY browser-side gateway read 400'd at PB ("Something went wrong…") → bare empty-body 500 → the dual-mode client silently fell back to stale local cache (cross-device sync dead for all 19 allowlisted collections). Fix in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`: new `withAutodate()` injects `created`/`updated` autodate fields into every collection and a shared `buildField()` now drives both the create and schema-patch paths (autodate emits `onCreate`/`onUpdate`, no `required`); re-ran `npm run pb:seed` → all collections patched +2 fields. Gateway routes hardened to return parseable JSON instead of empty 500s: `src/app/api/db/[collection]/route.ts` + `[id]/route.ts` wrap PB calls in try/catch → `db_error` 502, and bad JSON bodies → `invalid_json` 400. **(2) Unauthenticated-PB reads fixed:** several server libs still used the public `getPB()` client, which 403s on every locked collection. New `getAuthedPB()` in `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (same cached admin client + token refresh as `withAdmin`) for call sites that read more naturally with a `pb` handle. Switched: `skill-tree.ts` (GET /api/skill-tree was 500 "Failed to load skill tree data" → now 200, creates/reads real profiles), `schedule-analytics.ts` (was 500 "Only superusers can perform this action." → now 200), `recurring-patterns.ts` (collections already seeded → now returns real data), `family-memory.ts` (its `consuela_family_memories` collection was never seeded → added as the 37th collection in `pb-seed.ts`, now create/read/delete round-trips). **(3) formData + email routes:** `ocr/extract`, `voice/process`, `photo/process` threw Next's raw 500 on non-multipart requests and queried a phantom `users` collection via the public client — now content-type-guarded (clean 400 "Request must be multipart/form-data…"), `users`→`members`, `consuela_saved_locations` read wrapped in a `[]` fallback, all on `withAdmin`. `email/forward` got the same `users`→`members` + `withAdmin` fix (now 200 end-to-end). **(4) Removed dead/dangerous routes:** legacy `/api/family` route + `family.json` (zero callers; leaked plaintext PINs on GET and allowed unauthenticated arbitrary file writes on POST) and the dead `/api/consuela/chat` stub + its integration-test block (unauthenticated PB, phantom `conversations`/`conversation_messages`, hardcoded TODO reply — real chat uses `/api/hermes/chat`). **(5) chat/process hardened:** `/api/chat/process` (enhanced-AI clarification layer `UnifiedInput` calls before `/api/hermes/chat`) 500'd on phantom `consuela_family_members`/`consuela_saved_locations`; those reads now fall back to `[]` and the ambiguity check is skipped when no family context loads, so it passes through cleanly (200, no clarification spam) and chat still flows to Hermes. **Known limitations (graceful degradation, not crashes):** money-mountain + time-capsule collections were never seeded and their schemas use `relation` fields to a nonexistent `users` collection — both degrade to empty 200s (data doesn't persist); the enhanced-AI family (`family-ai`/`consuela-ai-enhanced` context enrichment) is a parallel demo built on phantom collections and only its crash paths were fixed. Verified live (adult session): all GET routes 200 or deliberate 404 on removed routes; gateway reads 200; skill-tree/schedule-analytics/recurring-patterns/family-memory return real data; family-memory create/read/delete round-trips; removed routes 404; formData routes clean 400. Suite 555/555 (removed 2 consuela/chat tests, updated pb-rules-lockdown mock for autodate), `tsc` clean, eslint clean on all touched files.

- 2026-08-26 — fix(integrations): Settings → Integrations backend repaired — live PB reseeded, seed script rewired to the canonical seeder, graceful degradation on config-store/HA failures. **Root cause:** the live PocketBase (recreated 2026-08-21) predated HA Phase 2 + Services & Keys and was missing 14 collections (`consuela_service_config`, `ha_notify_config`, `ha_notify_prefs`, `ha_mirror_state`, `ha_entities/areas/devices/automations`, `skill_tree_profiles`, `skill_branches`, `quests`, `achievements`, `user_achievements`, `recurring_patterns`), so every `/api/services/*` route threw → bare 500s (🔑 card dead, Test buttons dead, runtime 500 → weather hardcoded coords) and the 🔔 card warned "config store unreachable". Compounding: `npm run pb:seed` ran a stale standalone `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` (original 10 collections only, no LOCKED_RULES) while the complete `seedCollections()` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` was wired to nothing. **(1)** `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` rewritten as a thin wrapper: auto-loads `.env.local` via `process.loadEnvFile` when NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL/PB_ADMIN_EMAIL/PB_ADMIN_PASS aren't exported (explicit env still wins for the `scripts/consuela/test-*.mjs` flow), validates, then dynamic-imports `seedCollections()` AFTER env resolution (pb.ts/pb-auth.ts capture env at module load). **(2)** Seed run against the live instance: 14 collections created; `events` patched +importanceScore/importanceReason/importanceUpdatedAt; `recipes` +sourceUrl; drifted rules re-locked admin-only on members/meal_plan_entries/events/tasks/emergency_contacts/schedules/rewards/penalties. **(3)** Hardening: `GET /api/services/config` try/catch → 503 `{services:[], error:"config_store_unreachable"}` (was empty-body 500); `ServicesKeysCard` gained a `loadFailed` one-line error state; `GET /api/services/runtime` try/catch → 200 `{runtime:{}, error:...}` (widgets keep defaults); `GET /api/ha/health` try/catch → 200 `{ok:false, configured:false, reason:"not_configured", wsStatus:"disconnected", wsConnected:false}` (was 500 when HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN absent). Verified live (adult session): config GET 200 all 11 services, PUT/DELETE round-trip with source=db, Test buttons honest (themealdb ok 434ms; hermes unreachable; telegram bot ok but chat-id unset; gmail/HA not_configured), notify-targets warning gone, notify-test honest 400, Google state/device-grant untouched. Tests +3 (config 503 path, runtime empty path, ha-health not_configured). Suite 557/557, tsc clean, 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect in ServicesKeysCard legacy-blob effect, present on HEAD).

- 2026-08-24 — feat(settings): Services & Keys (plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-24-services-keys-settings.md, branch feat/services-keys-settings). consuela_service_config collection + registry whitelist (11 services, boot-critical six excluded); shared secret-box crypto; resolver (PB→env→null) migrated into ha/config+mqtt+rest-client+ws singleton getters (now async), free-communication, telegram/get-updates, hermes/chat (hardcoded key default deleted), instacart (localStorage split-brain removed), themealdb, weather coords (runtime endpoint + hook, 3 literals deduped). Settings 🔑 card w/ status dots, masked fields, Test/Clear/Reconnect, import banner. - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-22 | **Kid mode: crew & open board** — NEW `src/modes/kid/kid-board.ts` (pure helpers: `kidBoardState` / `splitKidBoard` / `crewAvatars` / `crewProgressLabel` / `crewWaitingNames`, crew math imported from `@/lib/task-utils`) + NEW `src/modes/kid/KidCrewBoard.tsx` (presentation only; `data-testid="kid-crew-board"`, null when both lists empty; actionable rows are buttons labelled `Join crew:` / `Done my part:` / `Grab it:`). KidHome renders the board above "🎯 Your Quests"; `pendingTasks` narrowed to assigned-only (crew/open live on the board — XOR, never both); subtitle counts pending + board; 3-way empty state; `onAct={openQuestPin}` so join/check-in/grab reuse `/api/tasks/claim` (zero backend changes). - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Tasks-screen UI lift — both modes (evaluation→plan→4 phases, all shipped as `tasks-lift-v1`).** A critique scored the surface 24/40 (dual-agent was denied by session rules → single-context run, detector 1 finding = the documented --ease-spring false positive). Evidence: on a 390px phone the chore list started at 1223px (guest) / 1750px (parent) behind stacked stat tiles + an EMPTY 393px AI card; the Tasks⇄Leaderboard segmented control was 32px (under the app's own 44px floor); member-tile names were 10.88px (under the 11px floor); PIN inputs had placeholder-only labels; 10 duplicate popBounce <style> blocks polluted the strip's textContent; kids could swipe-to-edit + DELETE family chores ungated with NO confirm; the kitchen display (guest after the 30-min auto-logout) could never complete a task. **P0 safety:** Add/Edit/Delete are parent-only (Delete now confirms via the shared Modal — "goes away for the whole family"); kids/pets hitting /tasks redirect to their KidHome quest surface (every entry path); the dead "Sync Google Tasks" button + RemindersSection (Google device-flow rejects the scope) are removed. **P1 hierarchy:** Pending leads the page (Open board directly above); stat tiles are one compact 3-up row at every width; "Consuela suggests" is parent-only and BELOW Completed (quiet "✨ Get chore ideas" line when idle); the Leaderboard tab leads with Podium + PrizeRaceCard, with Journey/FamilyGoal/HallOfFame folded behind a "🏅 Trophies, journey & history" expander; segmented options min-h-44; tile names .7rem; PIN inputs aria-labeled; AnimatedEmoji's 11 keyframes hoisted to globals.css (zero per-instance <style>). **P2 kid language + sessions:** NEW `src/modes/kid/quest-labels.ts` (pure, tested) — due dates render as "Today"/"Tomorrow"/weekday/"⚠️ Late" chips (accent-toned) instead of raw ISO; open quests say "🫳 Up for grabs" (not another kid's name), crew quests "🤝 Crew"; quests lead KidHome above the weekend banner; "🔄 Switch member" is one-tap on ALL kid surfaces (bedtime hides it); kid/pet sessions auto-logout at 15 idle min (parents 30) with a "⏳ N min left — tap anything to stay" chip in the final 5; /rewards routes to "/" when the kid session ends. **P3 parent efficiency:** "✓ Approve all (N)" pays the whole queue with ONE parent-PIN (same idempotent reversal-aware approve per row); Add-modal Points is a Stepper (0-50, aria-labeled) and Recurring is a select (None/Daily/Weekdays/Weekly). React-compiler bail fixed (mutable loop → reduce; openBoard/pendingApprovals memos → plain computations). Verified live on the NAS (390px): guest Pending at 562px (was 1223), 0 tiny text, 0 sub-44px targets, 0 <style> tags, docH 1790→1108; kid on /tasks lands on "/" in kid mode; KidHome quest chips render "🫳 Up for grabs · ⚠️ Late" (no raw dates), Switch member present, quests above the banner. Gates: tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2348/2351 (3 stash-proven pre-existing: TZ-streak ×2 + weather-strip), next build clean. Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Ask Consuela: the thread stays on screen while Consuela is thinking, and replies survive tab changes (zero backend changes).** Two reported bugs, one architecture fix. (1) **History vanished during a reply** — `showHero` treated "first user message + isTyping" as a hero state, so the whole thread (the user's own bubble included) was replaced by a 200px "Thinking…" orb until the first content token, which with buffered tool rounds could be minutes. Now `showHero = !hasRealMessages`: the hero owns only a truly empty conversation, and the existing typing-dots bubble (with the tool `statusLine`) renders under the last message as the thinking affordance. The big hero orb branch is deleted. (2) **"If I change pages then it shows"** — chat state was component-local, and every tab navigation unmounts /chat, so an in-flight stream's updates landed on a dead component; the reply only appeared on remount because the page refetched today's PocketBase thread. NEW **`src/lib/chat-store.ts`** (module-level observable singleton, consumed with `useSyncExternalStore` — same idiom as `calendar-member-snapshot.ts`) now owns `{ messages, isTyping, statusLine, hydrated, streaming }` plus the whole stream (AbortController, msgCounter, PB watermark, hydration). Navigating away interrupts nothing; returning re-attaches to the live partial instantly. `ensureHydrated()` runs once per page load (localStorage merge + full PB day read) and later mounts do a cheap incremental `since` reconcile; localStorage persistence moved into the store (a reply landing while away is saved). The page keeps only UI-local state (speaker, draft, scroll pin, read-aloud, modals). NEW `tests/unit/chat-store.test.ts` (7) + 2 page regressions ("keeps the user's message and the thinking bubble visible while Consuela replies", "keeps streaming while the page is unmounted and shows the partial on return"); all four chat page suites call `__resetChatStoreForTests()` in `beforeEach`. Verified: chat suites 58/58, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2362 passed (323 files; the 2 uncaught teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all` are pre-existing and reproduce in isolation); `next build` clean. **Ops: none** (no PB schema/route changes). Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Recipe detail page + cook mode shipped — saved recipes are now viewable and cookable (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-recipe-detail-page-cook-mode-design.md`, zero backend changes).** New route `/meals/recipes/[id]` renders the two-column sticky layout (hero + favorite, stats/macros, actions, quantity-styled ingredients left; numbered steps + source right) from the existing `useRecipes` catalog; if the recipe was deleted the page falls back to the meal's `meal_plan_entries` snapshot with an honest banner (edit/delete/favorite hidden) — the banner and not-found state are gated on a catalog-settled signal (recipes non-empty / syncBlocked / 600ms timer), so a slow catalog read never shows a false "deleted" or "not found". Pure helpers `src/lib/ingredient-quantity.ts` (`parseIngredientLine` — leading quantity/unit in amber bold, raw string never lost) and `src/lib/recipe-steps.ts` (`parseInstructionsToSteps` — newline-first, sentence-split fallback, marker stripping) power the page AND the new **CookMode** full-screen checklist (`src/components/meals/CookMode.tsx`: gather-ingredients + steps checkboxes, progress bar, same-day `cookmode:<id>:<YYYY-MM-DD>` localStorage persistence, auto-clear + 🎉 card when done, Esc exits, "No steps to check"/"Nothing to gather" counters on empty recipes). Entry points: RecipeBox card tap (`?from=recipes`; the view/keyboard affordance wraps only the image+name+stats region so inner action buttons are never ARIA-flattened) and the previously dead PlanTab meal-card chevron (`?from=plan`, `.hit-44`, hidden for meals without `recipeId`). Actions on the page: add-to-day picker, 🛒 to grocery, ✏️ edit (existing RecipeModal), 🗑 delete with confirm (finally renders the dead `deleteCatalogRecipe` prop), favorite. Verified: TDD red→green suites (ingredient-quantity 8, recipe-steps 7, cook-mode 6, recipe-detail-page 8, recipe-box-navigation 6, plan-tab-recipe-chevron 2), warm-glass contracts pass, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean, full suite 2325 passed / 2 failed (both documented pre-existing `calculate-real-streak-tz`). **Ops: none** (no PB schema/migration).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Points-display wave 2: guest completions + week champions — the kitchen display could never complete a task, and the Hall of Fame was silently empty.** User report: "this week points still not showing." Two live-verified root causes: **(1) Guest devices got "Wrong PIN" on a CORRECT PIN.** The kitchen display auto-logs-out after 30 min, so it is usually a GUEST session — and the middleware session gate 401'd `/api/members/verify` + `/api/tasks/claim` before any PIN check (verified: 401 with correct creds). Aurora + the kids' uncelebrated wins will fire their ceremonies on next open. - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-18 | **Ask Consuela thread ordering + a genuinely fresh new conversation (2 user-reported bugs, TDD).** (1) **"Reply above my request"** was a mount-hydration race: the initial PB history load did a blind `setMessages(pbMsgs)` replace, so a message sent before the fetch resolved was dropped and then re-appended at the END by the post-send reconcile (`mergePBThread`) → reply above request. (2) **New conversation never cleared the view** — `/new` appended a divider but the render mapped the whole `messages` array. Fix: NEW pure `src/lib/chat-thread.ts` (`threadKey` dedupes on role+content — drops `speaker`, killing the signed-out duplicate row; `sortThread` orders by a new `Message.at` epoch-ms key with user→divider→reply ties; additive `mergeThread` never removes rows; `visibleThread` slices at the newest `system` marker and drops the seed greeting once a user row exists). Both hydration effects (PB + localStorage) now MERGE into state instead of replacing; the page renders `visibleMessages = visibleThread(sortThread(messages))` for the thread map + hero/quick-actions/read-aloud; `showHero` now hands the view to the thread once any real message exists (not only a user row); the seed greeting is never persisted; `selectChatMessages` sorts `"createdAt,id"`; the confirm copy is honest that the screen clears while PB keeps history. Verified: NEW `tests/unit/chat-thread.test.ts` (18) + a delayed-hydration ordering page test; chat suites 42/42; full suite **2228 passed / 3 failed — all 3 stash-proven pre-existing on HEAD** (calculate-real-streak-tz ×2 + weather-widget strip ×1); tsc clean; eslint 0 on touched files. No PB schema/migration. Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record.
